Boosting Team Modeling through Tempo-Relational Representation Learning
Authors: Vincenzo Marco De Luca, Giovanna Varni, Andrea Passerini
First: 2025-07-17T17:22:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:59:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Team modeling remains a fundamental challenge at the intersection of Artificial Intelligence and Social Sciences. Although a variety of computational models have been proposed in the last two decades, most fail to integrate Social Sciences insights, such as the critical role of temporal interactions in shaping team dynamics, and do not meet key practical requirements for real-world applications, including the ability to provide real-time, actionable recommendations to enhance team performance. To address these limitations, in this paper, we propose a novel tempo-relational neural architecture that jointly models interactions between team members and the evolution of team dynamics through temporal graphs. We additionally propose a multi-task extension of the architecture that learns shared social embeddings for team members enabling the simultaneous prediction of multiple team constructs (e.g., Emergent Leadership, Leadership Style, and Teamwork components). Experiments on two state-of-the-art team datasets show that our tempo-relational architecture out performs temporal-only and relational-only approaches for team performance prediction, and that its multi-task extension substantially reduces training and inference time without loss of predictive performance. Finally, the integration of explainability techniques within the proposed architectures provides interpretable insights and actionable recommendations to support team improvement. These strengths make our approach particularly well-suited for human-centered artificial intelligence applications, such as intelligent decision-support systems in high-stakes collaborative environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过时序关系表示学习提升团队建模
团队建模仍是人工智能与社会科学交叉领域的基础挑战。尽管过去二十年提出了多种计算模型，但大多数未能整合社会科学洞见，如时间互动在塑造团队动态中的关键作用，也未能满足现实应用的关键实践需求，例如提供实时、可操作的建议以提升团队表现。为解决这些局限，本文提出一种新颖的时序关系神经架构，通过时序图联合建模团队成员间的互动及团队动态的演化。此外，我们还提出该架构的多任务扩展版本，学习团队成员共享的社会嵌入，从而同时预测多种团队构型（如涌现领导力、领导风格及团队协作要素）。在两个前沿团队数据集上的实验表明，我们的时序关系架构在团队表现预测上优于仅时序或仅关系的方法，且其多任务扩展版本显著降低训练与推理时间，而预测性能无损。最后，将可解释性技术整合进所提架构，提供可解释的洞察与可操作建议，以支持团队改进。这些优势使我们的方法特别适用于以人为中心的人工智能应用，如高风险协作环境中的智能决策支持系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better model team dynamics using insights from social sciences, particularly the role of temporal interactions, this paper introduces a tempo-relational neural architecture that jointly captures member interactions and evolving team states via temporal graphs. The method further extends to a multi-task framework that learns shared social embeddings to predict multiple team constructs simultaneously. Experimental results on two benchmark datasets demonstrate that the tempo-relational model outperforms purely temporal or relational baselines in team performance prediction, while the multi-task variant achieves faster training and inference without sacrificing accuracy, and integrates explainability to deliver actionable insights for team improvement.
为提升团队动态建模效果，本文提出一种结合时间与关系的神经架构，通过时序图联合建模成员互动与团队演化状态，并扩展为多任务框架以共享社交嵌入预测多个团队指标。实验表明，该架构在团队表现预测上优于仅考虑时间或关系的基线方法，其多任务版本在保持预测精度的同时显著提升训练与推理效率，并集成可解释性技术提供可操作的团队改进建议。
A Closed-Form Adaptive-Landmark Kernel for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification
Authors: Sushovan Majhi, Atish Mitra, Žiga Virk, Pramita Bagchi
First: 2026-05-05T17:59:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:59:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce PALACE (Persistence Adaptive-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), the data-adaptive companion to PLACE, paying a small cross-validation tier on three knobs (budget, radii, bandwidth; $\leq 5$ choices each). A cover-theoretic core (Lebesgue-number criterion on the landmark cover) yields four closed-form guarantees. (i) A structural lower distortion bound $λ(τ;ν)$ on $\mathcal{D}_n$ under cross-diagram non-interference, with a $(D/L)^2$ budget reduction over the uniform grid when diagrams concentrate. (ii) Equal weights $w_k = K^{-1/2}$ maximizing $λ$, and farthest-point-sampling positions $2$-approximating the optimal $k$-center covering radius; both derived from training labels alone, no gradient training. (iii) A kernel-RKHS classification rate $O((k-1)\sqrt{K}/(γ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ with binary necessity threshold $m = Ω(\sqrt K/γ)$ from a matching Le Cam lower bound, and a closed-form filtration-selection rule. The kernel-Mahalanobis margin $\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$ is the strongest closed-form ranker across the chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ\approx +0.60$); the isotropic surrogate $\hatγ/\sqrt{K}$ admits a selection-consistency rate, and $\widehatλ$ from (i) provides an independent data-level signal (positive on COX2 and PTC). (iv) A per-prediction certificate, in non-asymptotic Pinelis and asymptotic Gaussian forms, with no calibration split. Empirically, PALACE is the strongest closed-form diagram-based method on Orbit5k ($91.3 \pm 1.0\%$, matching Persformer), leads every diagram-based competitor on COX2 and MUTAG, and is competitive on DHFR (within 1 pp of ECP). At $8\times$ domain inflation, adaptive placement maintains $94\%$ while the uniform grid collapses to chance ($25\%$ on 4-class data).
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种闭式自适应地标核用于可认证点云与图分类
我们引入PALACE（持久性自适应地标分析分类引擎），作为PLACE的数据自适应配套方法，仅需在三个参数（预算、半径、带宽；每项最多5种选择）上进行小规模交叉验证。基于覆盖理论核心（地标覆盖的勒贝格数准则），获得四项闭式保证：(i) 在交叉图无干扰条件下，对$\mathcal{D}_n$提供结构化下界$λ(τ;ν)$，当图集中度高时，相比均匀网格可减少$(D/L)^2$的预算；(ii) 等权重$w_k = K^{-1/2}$最大化$λ$，且最远点采样位置近似最优$k$中心覆盖半径（2倍近似），二者仅依赖训练标签，无需梯度训练；(iii) 核RKHS分类速率$O((k-1)\sqrt{K}/(γ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$，二元必要阈值$m = Ω(\sqrt K/γ)$来自匹配的Le Cam下界，附带闭式过滤选择规则。核马哈拉诺比斯边际$\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$在化学图数据集中为最强闭式排序器（平均斯皮尔曼$ρ\approx +0.60$）；各向同性代理$\hatγ/\sqrt{K}$具备选择一致性速率，而(i)中的$\widehatλ$提供独立数据级信号（在COX2和PTC上为正）；(iv) 提供每预测的证书，形式为非渐近Pinelis与渐近高斯形式，无需校准分割。实证上，PALACE在Orbit5k上为最强闭式图方法（91.3±1.0%，匹配Persformer），在COX2和MUTAG上领先所有图方法，在DHFR上竞争力强（与ECP相差1个百分点内）。在8倍领域膨胀下，自适应放置保持94%准确率，而均匀网格退化至随机水平（4类数据为25%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for certified, data-adaptive classification of point clouds and graphs without gradient training, PALACE introduces a closed-form kernel method that adapts landmark placement via cross-validation on three parameters. It leverages a cover-theoretic framework to derive four theoretical guarantees: a distortion bound under non-interference, optimal weight and sampling rules from labels alone, a kernel-RKHS classification rate with matching lower bounds, and per-prediction certificates without calibration. Experimentally, PALACE achieves top performance on Orbit5k (91.3% accuracy), outperforms all diagram-based methods on COX2 and MUTAG, and remains robust under domain inflation, maintaining 94% accuracy while uniform grids collapse to chance.
为实现无需梯度训练的点云与图分类并提供可验证保障，PALACE提出一种基于覆盖理论的闭式核方法，通过交叉验证自适应调整三个参数。其核心理论推导出结构失真下界、最优权重分配及与数据几何相关的分类速率。实验表明，PALACE在Orbit5k上达到91.3%准确率，领先所有图谱方法于COX2与MUTAG，且在领域膨胀8倍时仍保持94%精度，而均匀网格则退化至随机水平。
Audio-Visual Intelligence in Large Foundation Models
Authors: You Qin, Kai Liu, Shengqiong Wu, Kai Wang, Shijian Deng, Yapeng Tian, Junbin Xiao, Yazhou Xing, Yinghao Ma, Bobo Li, Roger Zimmermann, Lei Cui, Furu Wei, Jiebo Luo, Hao Fei
First: 2026-05-05T17:59:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:59:16+00:00
Comments: 56 pages, 16 figures, 24 tables, https://github.com/JavisVerse/Awesome-AVI
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Audio-Visual Intelligence (AVI) has emerged as a central frontier in artificial intelligence, bridging auditory and visual modalities to enable machines that can perceive, generate, and interact in the multimodal real world. In the era of large foundation models, joint modeling of audio and vision has become increasingly crucial, i.e., not only for understanding but also for controllable generation and reasoning across dynamic, temporally grounded signals. Recent advances, such as Meta MovieGen and Google Veo-3, highlight the growing industrial and academic focus on unified audio-vision architectures that learn from massive multimodal data. However, despite rapid progress, the literature remains fragmented, spanning diverse tasks, inconsistent taxonomies, and heterogeneous evaluation practices that impede systematic comparison and knowledge integration. This survey provides the first comprehensive review of AVI through the lens of large foundation models. We establish a unified taxonomy covering the broad landscape of AVI tasks, ranging from understanding (e.g., speech recognition, sound localization) to generation (e.g., audio-driven video synthesis, video-to-audio) and interaction (e.g., dialogue, embodied, or agentic interfaces). We synthesize methodological foundations, including modality tokenization, cross-modal fusion, autoregressive and diffusion-based generation, large-scale pretraining, instruction alignment, and preference optimization. Furthermore, we curate representative datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation metrics, offering a structured comparison across task families and identifying open challenges in synchronization, spatial reasoning, controllability, and safety. By consolidating this rapidly expanding field into a coherent framework, this survey aims to serve as a foundational reference for future research on large-scale AVI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大基础模型中的视听智能
视听智能（AVI）已成为人工智能的核心前沿领域，旨在弥合听觉与视觉模态的鸿沟，使机器能够感知、生成并交互于多模态的真实世界。在大基础模型时代，音频与视觉的联合建模变得愈发关键，不仅用于理解，更用于跨动态、时序锚定信号的可控生成与推理。近期进展如Meta MovieGen和Google Veo-3凸显了业界与学界对统一视听架构的日益关注，该架构从海量多模态数据中学习。然而，尽管进展迅速，现有文献仍显碎片化，涵盖多样任务、不一致分类体系及异质评估实践，阻碍了系统性比较与知识整合。本综述首次从大基础模型视角全面梳理AVI领域，建立涵盖AVI任务广谱的统一分类体系，从理解（如语音识别、声音定位）到生成（如音频驱动视频合成、视频到音频）再到交互（如对话、具身或代理式界面）。我们综合了方法论基础，包括模态分词、跨模态融合、自回归与扩散式生成、大规模预训练、指令对齐与偏好优化。此外，我们整理了代表性数据集、基准与评估指标，提供任务家族间的结构化比较，并识别出同步、空间推理、可控性与安全性等开放挑战。通过将这一快速扩展领域整合为统一框架，本综述旨在为未来大尺度AVI研究提供基础参考。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for machines to perceive and interact in multimodal environments, this survey reviews Audio-Visual Intelligence (AVI) through the lens of large foundation models, unifying fragmented research across understanding, generation, and interaction tasks. It introduces a taxonomy covering speech recognition, audio-driven video synthesis, and embodied dialogue, and synthesizes core methodologies including modality tokenization, cross-modal fusion, and diffusion-based generation. The survey curates datasets and benchmarks, highlighting challenges in synchronization, spatial reasoning, controllability, and safety, and provides a structured comparison across task families to guide future research in scalable AVI systems.
为应对机器在多模态环境中感知与交互的需求，本综述从大型基础模型视角系统梳理了音频视觉智能（AVI）研究，构建涵盖理解、生成与交互三大任务的统一分类框架。文章整合了模态标记化、跨模态融合、自回归与扩散生成、大规模预训练等核心方法，并梳理代表性数据集与评估基准，实现任务间的结构化对比。实验分析指出同步性、空间推理、可控性与安全性仍为关键挑战，指明未来AVI研究亟待突破的方向。
UniCorrn: Unified Correspondence Transformer Across 2D and 3D
Authors: Prajnan Goswami, Tianye Ding, Feng Liu, Huaizu Jiang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-05T17:58:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:58:53+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026, 20 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Visual correspondence across image-to-image (2D-2D), image-to-point cloud (2D-3D), and point cloud-to-point cloud (3D-3D) geometric matching forms the foundation for numerous 3D vision tasks. Despite sharing a similar problem structure, current methods use task-specific designs with separate models for each modality combination. We present UniCorrn, the first correspondence model with shared weights that unifies geometric matching across all three tasks. Our key insight is that Transformer attention naturally captures cross-modal feature similarity. We propose a dual-stream decoder that maintains separate appearance and positional feature streams. This design enables end-to-end learning through stack-able layers while supporting flexible query-based correspondence estimation across heterogeneous modalities. Our architecture employs modality-specific backbones followed by shared encoder and decoder components, trained jointly on diverse data combining pseudo point clouds from depth maps with real 3D correspondence annotations. UniCorrn achieves competitive performance on 2D-2D matching and surpasses prior state-of-the-art by 8% on 7Scenes (2D-3D) and 10% on 3DLoMatch (3D-3D) in registration recall. Project website: https://neu-vi.github.io/UniCorrn
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniCorrn：跨2D与3D的统一对应Transformer
图像到图像（2D-2D）、图像到点云（2D-3D）和点云到点云（3D-3D）的视觉对应是众多3D视觉任务的基础。尽管这些任务具有相似的问题结构，当前方法采用针对特定任务的独立设计，为每种模态组合分别构建模型。我们提出了UniCorrn，这是首个具有共享权重、统一处理所有三种任务几何匹配的对应模型。我们的核心洞察是：Transformer注意力机制天然能够捕捉跨模态特征相似性。我们提出了一种双流解码器，分别维护外观和位置特征流。该设计支持通过堆叠层实现端到端学习，同时支持在异构模态间灵活的基于查询的对应估计。该架构采用模态特定的骨干网络，后接共享的编码器和解码器组件，并在结合深度图生成的伪点云与真实3D对应标注的多样化数据上联合训练。UniCorrn在2D-2D匹配任务中表现优异，并在7Scenes（2D-3D）和3DLoMatch（3D-3D）注册召回率上分别超越现有最先进方法8%和10%。项目网站：https://neu-vi.github.io/UniCorrn
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified geometric matching across 2D-2D, 2D-3D, and 3D-3D tasks, UniCorrn introduces a shared-weight Transformer architecture that eliminates task-specific models. It employs a dual-stream decoder to separately process appearance and positional features, enabling flexible, query-based correspondence estimation across heterogeneous modalities. Trained jointly on diverse data including pseudo point clouds from depth maps and real 3D annotations, UniCorrn achieves competitive 2D-2D performance and outperforms prior methods by 8% on 7Scenes (2D-3D) and 10% on 3DLoMatch (3D-3D) in registration recall.
为实现2D-2D、2D-3D和3D-3D几何匹配任务的统一，UniCorrn提出首个共享权重的Transformer架构，摒弃了任务专用模型。其采用双流解码器分别处理外观与位置特征，支持跨异构模态的灵活端到端对应估计。联合训练于包含深度图伪点云与真实3D标注的多样化数据上，UniCorrn在2D-2D任务中表现竞争力，并在7Scenes（2D-3D）和3DLoMatch（3D-3D）注册召回率上分别超越现有方法8%和10%。
Large Language Models are Universal Reasoners for Visual Generation
Authors: Sucheng Ren, Chen Chen, Zhenbang Wang, Liangchen Song, Xiangxin Zhu, Alan Yuille, Liang-Chieh Chen, Jiasen Lu
First: 2026-05-05T17:57:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:57:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-image generation has advanced rapidly with diffusion models, progressing from CLIP and T5 conditioning to unified systems where a single LLM backbone handles both visual understanding and generation. Despite the architectural unification, these systems frequently fail to faithfully align complex prompts during synthesis, even though they remain highly accurate at verifying whether an image satisfies those same prompts. We formalize this as the \emph{understanding-generation gap} and propose UniReasoner, a framework that leverages the LLM as a universal reasoner to convert its understanding strength into direct generation guidance. Given a prompt, the LLM first produces a coarse visual draft composed of discrete vision tokens. It then performs a self-critique by evaluating the draft for prompt consistency, producing a grounded textual evaluation that pinpoints what needs to be corrected. Finally, a diffusion model is conditioned jointly on the prompt, the visual draft, and the evaluation, ensuring that generation is guided by explicit corrective signals. Each signal addresses a limitation of the other: the draft provides a concrete, scene-level anchor that reduces under-specification in text-only conditioning, while the evaluation turns verification into grounded, actionable constraints that correct omissions, hallucinations, and relational errors. Experiments show that UniReasoner improves compositional alignment and semantic faithfulness under the same diffusion backbone while maintaining image quality, demonstrating a practical way to exploit LLM reasoning to close the understanding-generation gap.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型是视觉生成的通用推理器
随着扩散模型的发展，文本到图像生成技术迅速进步，从基于CLIP和T5的条件生成演进到统一系统，其中单一LLM主干同时处理视觉理解和生成。尽管架构统一，这些系统在合成复杂提示时仍常无法忠实对齐，尽管它们在验证图像是否满足这些提示方面仍保持高度准确。我们将此现象形式化为“理解-生成差距”，并提出UniReasoner框架，利用LLM作为通用推理器，将其理解能力转化为直接生成指导。给定提示，LLM首先生成由离散视觉标记组成的粗略视觉草图，然后通过评估草图与提示的一致性，生成具象化的文本评价，指出需要修正的内容。最后，扩散模型联合提示、视觉草图和评价进行条件生成，确保生成过程受明确的修正信号引导。每种信号弥补另一种的局限：草图提供具体的场景级锚点，减少纯文本条件下的不足指定；评价将验证转化为具象、可操作的约束，纠正遗漏、幻觉和关系错误。实验表明，UniReasoner在相同扩散主干下提升组合对齐与语义忠实度，同时保持图像质量，展示了利用LLM推理有效弥合理解-生成差距的实用方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent misalignment between prompt understanding and image generation in unified LLM-based visual systems, the authors propose UniReasoner, a framework that uses the LLM as a universal reasoner to bridge this gap. The method first generates a coarse visual draft from discrete vision tokens, then self-critiques the draft by producing a grounded textual evaluation highlighting inconsistencies with the prompt. This evaluation, combined with the original prompt and draft, conditions a diffusion model to generate corrected images. Experiments demonstrate that UniReasoner significantly improves compositional alignment and semantic faithfulness without sacrificing image quality, effectively leveraging LLM reasoning to resolve the understanding-generation gap.
为解决统一LLM视觉系统中理解与生成脱节的问题，作者提出UniReasoner框架，利用LLM作为通用推理器弥合此差距。该方法首先生成离散视觉令牌组成的粗略视觉草图，随后进行自我批评，输出指向性文本评价以识别草图与提示的不一致。该评价与原始提示和草图共同引导扩散模型生成图像，提供明确的修正信号。实验表明，UniReasoner显著提升组合一致性与语义忠实度，同时保持图像质量，有效利用LLM推理能力纠正幻觉、遗漏与关系错误。
Safety and accuracy follow different scaling laws in clinical large language models
Authors: Sebastian Wind, Tri-Thien Nguyen, Jeta Sopa, Mahshad Lotfinia, Sebastian Bickelhaup, Michael Uder, Harald Köstler, Gerhard Wellein, Sven Nebelung, Daniel Truhn, Andreas Maier, Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh
First: 2026-05-05T17:57:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:57:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical LLMs are often scaled by increasing model size, context length, retrieval complexity, or inference-time compute, with the implicit expectation that higher accuracy implies safer behavior. This assumption is incomplete in medicine, where a few confident, high-risk, or evidence-contradicting errors can matter more than average benchmark performance. We introduce SaFE-Scale, a framework for measuring how clinical LLM safety changes across model scale, evidence quality, retrieval strategy, context exposure, and inference-time compute. To instantiate this framework, we introduce RadSaFE-200, a Radiology Safety-Focused Evaluation benchmark of 200 multiple-choice questions with clinician-defined clean evidence, conflict evidence, and option-level labels for high-risk error, unsafe answer, and evidence contradiction. We evaluated 34 locally deployed LLMs across six deployment conditions: closed-book prompting (zero-shot), clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Clean evidence produced the strongest improvement, increasing mean accuracy from 73.5% to 94.1%, while reducing high-risk error from 12.0% to 2.6%, contradiction from 12.7% to 2.3%, and dangerous overconfidence from 8.0% to 1.6%. Standard RAG and agentic RAG did not reproduce this safety profile: agentic RAG improved accuracy over standard RAG and reduced contradiction, but high-risk error and dangerous overconfidence remained elevated. Max-context prompting increased latency without closing the safety gap, and additional inference-time compute produced only limited gains. Worst-case analysis showed that clinically consequential errors concentrated in a small subset of questions. Clinical LLM safety is therefore not a passive consequence of scaling, but a deployment property shaped by evidence quality, retrieval design, context construction, and collective failure behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：临床大语言模型中的安全性和准确性遵循不同的缩放规律
临床大语言模型通常通过增加模型规模、上下文长度、检索复杂度或推理时计算量进行扩展，隐含假设为更高的准确性意味着更安全的行为。但在医学领域，这一假设不完整，因为少数自信、高风险或与证据相矛盾的错误比平均基准表现更重要。我们提出了SaFE-Scale框架，用于衡量临床大语言模型在模型规模、证据质量、检索策略、上下文暴露和推理时计算量变化下的安全性变化。为实例化该框架，我们引入了RadSaFE-200，一个包含200道多项选择题的放射学安全导向评估基准，由临床医生定义清洁证据、冲突证据及选项级标签（高风险错误、不安全答案、证据矛盾）。我们评估了34个本地部署的大语言模型在六种部署条件下的表现：闭书提示（零样本）、清洁证据、冲突证据、标准RAG、代理RAG和最大上下文提示。清洁证据带来最强提升，平均准确率从73.5%提升至94.1%，同时高风险错误从12.0%降至2.6%，矛盾从12.7%降至2.3%，危险过度自信从8.0%降至1.6%。标准RAG和代理RAG未能复制此安全表现：代理RAG虽在准确率上优于标准RAG且降低矛盾，但高风险错误和危险过度自信仍居高不下。最大上下文提示虽增加延迟，却未缩小安全差距，额外推理时计算量仅带来有限收益。最坏情况分析表明，临床后果严重的错误集中于少量题目。因此，临床大语言模型的安全性并非缩放的被动结果，而是由证据质量、检索设计、上下文构建和集体失败行为塑造的部署属性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how safety and accuracy scale differently in clinical large language models, the study introduces SaFE-Scale, a framework to measure safety across various deployment conditions. Using RadSaFE-200, a radiology-focused benchmark with clinician-defined evidence and error labels, the researchers evaluated 34 locally deployed models under six conditions: zero-shot, clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Results showed that clean evidence significantly boosted accuracy to 94.1% while drastically reducing high-risk errors, contradictions, and overconfidence, whereas agentic RAG improved accuracy but failed to reduce safety risks. Max-context prompting increased latency without improving safety, and additional compute yielded minimal gains. The findings reveal that clinical safety is not an automatic byproduct of scaling but depends critically on evidence quality, retrieval strategy, and context design, with high-risk errors concentrated in a small subset of questions.
鉴于临床大语言模型的规模扩展常被默认能同时提升准确性和安全性，本研究提出SaFE-Scale框架，用于量化不同部署条件下模型安全性的变化。基于RadSaFE-200放射学安全评估基准（含临床定义的证据与错误标签），作者对34个本地部署模型在六种部署模式下进行评估。结果显示，使用干净证据可将准确率从73.5%提升至94.1%，同时大幅降低高风险错误、矛盾回答和过度自信；而标准和智能RAG未能复制此安全收益，最大上下文提示和额外计算资源也仅带来有限改善。最坏情况分析表明，临床关键错误集中于少数题目，说明模型安全性并非规模扩展的被动结果，而是由证据质量、检索设计、上下文构建与集体失效行为共同塑造的部署特性。
OpenSeeker-v2: Pushing the Limits of Search Agents with Informative and High-Difficulty Trajectories
Authors: Yuwen Du, Rui Ye, Shuo Tang, Keduan Huang, Xinyu Zhu, Yuzhu Cai, Siheng Chen
First: 2026-05-05T17:55:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:55:25+00:00
Comments: 7 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep search capabilities have become an indispensable competency for frontier Large Language Model (LLM) agents, yet their development remains dominated by industrial giants. The typical industry recipe involves a highly resource-intensive pipeline spanning pre-training, continual pre-training (CPT), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL). In this report, we show that when fueled with informative and high-difficulty trajectories, a simple SFT approach could be surprisingly powerful for training frontier search agents. By introducing three simple data synthesis modifications: scaling knowledge graph size for richer exploration, expanding the tool set size for broader functionality, and strict low-step filtering, we establish a stronger baseline. Trained on merely 10.6k data points, our OpenSeeker-v2 achieves state-of-the-art performance across 4 benchmarks (30B-sized agents with ReAct paradigm): 46.0% on BrowseComp, 58.1% on BrowseComp-ZH, 34.6% on Humanity's Last Exam, and 78.0% on xbench, surpassing even Tongyi DeepResearch trained with heavy CPT+SFT+RL pipeline, which achieves 43.4%, 46.7%, 32.9%, and 75.0%, respectively. Notably, OpenSeeker-v2 represents the first state-of-the-art search agent within its model scale and paradigm to be developed by a purely academic team using only SFT. We are excited to open-source the OpenSeeker-v2 model weights and share our simple yet effective findings to make frontier search agent research more accessible to the community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenSeeker-v2：通过信息丰富且高难度的轨迹推动搜索代理能力极限
深度搜索能力已成为前沿大语言模型（LLM）代理不可或缺的核心能力，但其发展仍主要由工业巨头主导。典型的工业方法涉及资源密集型的全流程，包括预训练、持续预训练（CPT）、监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）。本报告表明，当使用信息丰富且高难度的轨迹时，简单的SFT方法可意外地强大，足以训练前沿搜索代理。通过引入三项简单数据合成改进——扩大知识图谱规模以实现更丰富的探索、扩展工具集规模以实现更广泛的功能、以及严格的低步数过滤，我们建立了更强的基准。仅在10.6k数据点上训练，我们的OpenSeeker-v2在4个基准测试（30B规模代理，采用ReAct范式）上均达到最先进性能：BrowseComp为46.0%，BrowseComp-ZH为58.1%，Humanity's Last Exam为34.6%，xbench为78.0%，超越了使用复杂CPT+SFT+RL流程训练的通义DeepResearch（分别为43.4%、46.7%、32.9%、75.0%）。值得注意的是，OpenSeeker-v2是首个在同等模型规模和范式下，由纯学术团队仅使用SFT方法开发的最先进搜索代理。我们很高兴开源OpenSeeker-v2模型权重，并分享我们的简单而有效的发现，以使前沿搜索代理研究更易于社区参与。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high resource cost of industrial search agent training pipelines, this work demonstrates that a simple supervised fine-tuning (SFT) approach can achieve state-of-the-art performance when augmented with informative and high-difficulty trajectories. The method introduces three data synthesis modifications: scaling knowledge graph size, expanding tool set size, and applying strict low-step filtering to enhance exploration and functionality. Trained on just 10.6k data points, OpenSeeker-v2 outperforms even industry-backed models using complex CPT+SFT+RL pipelines across four benchmarks: achieving 46.0% on BrowseComp, 58.1% on BrowseComp-ZH, 34.6% on Humanity's Last Exam, and 78.0% on xbench — marking the first SFT-only academic search agent to reach such performance levels.
为降低工业级搜索代理训练的高昂资源成本，本研究探索仅用监督微调（SFT）方法是否能在引入信息丰富且高难度轨迹后达到顶尖性能。方法通过三个数据合成改进：扩大知识图谱规模、增加工具集大小、严格过滤低步数样本，以增强探索与功能广度。在四个基准测试中，仅用10.6k数据点训练的OpenSeeker-v2表现优异：BrowseComp达46.0%，BrowseComp-ZH达58.1%，Humanity's Last Exam达34.6%，xbench达78.0%，全面超越采用CPT+SFT+RL流程的Tongyi DeepResearch。这是首个在同等规模与范式下、仅用SFT方法实现顶尖性能的学术团队开发的搜索代理。
Large-Scale High-Quality 3D Gaussian Head Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures
Authors: Evangelos Ntavelis, Sean Wu, Mohamad Shahbazi, Fabio Maninchedda, Dmitry Kostiaev, Artem Sevastopolsky, Vittorio Megaro, Trevor Phillips, Alejandro Blumentals, Shridhar Ravikumar, Mehak Gupta, Reinhard Knothe, Jeronimo Bayer, Matthias Vestner, Simon Schaefer, Thomas Etterlin, Christian Zimmermann, Mathias Deschler, Peter Kaufmann, Stefan Brugger, Sebastian Martin, Brian Amberg, Tom Runia
First: 2026-05-05T17:55:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:55:01+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://apple.github.io/ml-headsup/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We propose HeadsUp, a scalable feed-forward method for reconstructing high-quality 3D Gaussian heads from large-scale multi-camera setups. Our method employs an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that compresses input views into a compact latent representation. This latent representation is then decoded into a set of UV-parameterized 3D Gaussians anchored to a neutral head template. This UV representation decouples the number of 3D Gaussians from the number and resolution of input images, enabling training with many high-resolution input views. We train and evaluate our model on an internal dataset with more than 10,000 subjects, which is an order of magnitude larger than existing multi-view human head datasets. HeadsUp achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and generalizes to novel identities without test-time optimization. We extensively analyze the scaling behavior of our model across identities, views, and model capacity, revealing practical insights for quality-compute trade-offs. Finally, we highlight the strength of our latent space by showcasing two downstream applications: generating novel 3D identities and animating the 3D heads with expression blendshapes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从多视角捕捉中大规模高质量3D高斯头部重建
我们提出了HeadsUp，一种可扩展的前馈方法，用于从大规模多摄像头设置中重建高质量3D高斯头部。该方法采用高效的编码器-解码器架构，将输入视角压缩为紧凑的潜在表示。该潜在表示随后解码为一组UV参数化的3D高斯，锚定在中性头部模板上。这种UV表示将3D高斯数量与输入图像的数量和分辨率解耦，从而支持使用大量高分辨率输入视角进行训练。我们在一个包含超过10,000名受试者的内部数据集上训练和评估模型，其规模比现有多视角人体头部数据集大一个数量级。HeadsUp实现了最先进的重建质量，并能在无需测试时优化的情况下泛化到新身份。我们对模型在身份、视角和模型容量方面的扩展行为进行了广泛分析，揭示了质量与计算之间的实用权衡见解。最后，我们通过展示两个下游应用——生成新3D身份和使用表情混合形状动画化3D头部——突出了潜在空间的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and high-fidelity 3D head reconstruction from multi-view captures, HeadsUp introduces a feed-forward encoder-decoder architecture that compresses input views into a compact latent representation, which is then decoded into UV-parameterized 3D Gaussians anchored to a neutral head template. This design decouples the number of Gaussians from input image resolution and count, enabling efficient training on a large internal dataset of over 10,000 subjects. Experimental results demonstrate state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and strong generalization to unseen identities without test-time optimization, with detailed scaling analysis revealing practical trade-offs between model capacity, view count, and reconstruction fidelity. The latent space also supports downstream tasks such as novel identity generation and expression-driven animation.
为实现大规模多视角下高保真3D头部重建，HeadsUp提出一种前馈编码器-解码器架构，将输入视角压缩为紧凑的潜在表示，再解码为锚定中性头部模板的UV参数化3D高斯点集。该设计使高斯点数量与输入图像数量和分辨率解耦，支持在大规模数据集上高效训练。在包含超1万样本的内部数据集上评估，HeadsUp达到当前最优重建质量，且无需测试时优化即可泛化至新身份。模型在身份、视角与容量上的扩展性分析揭示了质量与计算成本的实用权衡，其潜在空间还支持生成新身份及表情驱动动画等下游应用。
Stayin' Aligned Over Time: Towards Longitudinal Human-LLM Alignment via Contextual Reflection and Privacy-Preserving Behavioral Data
Authors: Simret Araya Gebreegziabher, Allison E Sproul, Yinuo Yang, Chaoran Chen, Diego Gómez-Zará, Toby Jia-Jun Li
First: 2026-05-05T17:51:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:51:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current human-AI alignment and evaluation methods for large language models (LLMs) often rely on preference signals collected immediately after an interaction. This practice implicitly treats preference as static, even though many LLM-mediated decisions unfold over time and may be re-evaluated differently after real-world consequences and observed outcomes. Therefore, we argue for a methodological shift from single-moment preference elicitation to longitudinal, context-situated alignment measurement. We present a methodological framework for collecting temporally grounded alignment signals by combining (1) in-situ preference capture, (2) context-triggered follow-up preference reflection, and (3) privacy-preserving behavioral traces that help interpret preference change. As an instantiation of this methodology, we introduce BITE, a browser-based system that detects consequential LLM interactions, prompts reflection across later decision points, and supports progressive, user-controlled consent for sharing behavioral data. Through a two week longitudinal deployment study with 8 participants, our approach surfaced differences between immediate and later user preferences in accuracy, relevance and other dimensions of the LLM output. Our findings highlight the limitations of single-moment preference datasets and underscore the importance of longitudinal methods for alignment evaluation in everyday use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随时间保持对齐：通过情境反思与隐私保护的行为数据实现长期人机大模型对齐
当前针对大语言模型（LLMs）的人机对齐与评估方法通常依赖于交互后立即收集的偏好信号，这隐含地将偏好视为静态。然而，许多由LLM介导的决策会随时间展开，并可能在现实后果和观察到的结果出现后被重新评估。因此，我们主张从单时刻偏好采集转向纵向、情境化的对齐测量方法。我们提出一种结合（1）现场偏好捕捉、（2）情境触发的后续偏好反思、（3）隐私保护的行为轨迹以解释偏好变化的方法框架。作为该方法的具体实现，我们引入了BITE系统——一个基于浏览器的系统，用于检测具有重大影响的LLM交互，引导用户在后续决策点进行反思，并支持用户逐步控制共享行为数据的同意机制。通过为期两周、8名参与者的纵向部署研究，我们的方法揭示了用户在LLM输出的准确性、相关性等维度上，即时偏好与后期偏好的差异。研究结果凸显了单时刻偏好数据集的局限性，并强调了在日常使用中纵向方法对对齐评估的重要性。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of current LLM alignment methods that rely on immediate post-interaction preference signals, which fail to capture how user preferences evolve over time after real-world outcomes. The authors propose a longitudinal alignment framework combining in-situ preference capture, context-triggered follow-up reflection, and privacy-preserving behavioral data to model dynamic user alignment. Their system, BITE, detects consequential LLM interactions, prompts users for later reflections, and enables progressive consent for behavioral data sharing. In a two-week study with eight participants, the method revealed significant shifts in user preferences regarding LLM output accuracy and relevance over time, demonstrating that single-moment preference data is insufficient for evaluating alignment in real-world usage.
该论文指出当前LLM对齐方法依赖交互后即时偏好信号，无法捕捉用户在现实结果影响下随时间变化的偏好。作者提出一种纵向对齐框架，结合现场偏好采集、情境触发的后续反思和隐私保护的行为数据，以建模动态偏好。他们通过BITE浏览器系统实现该框架，自动检测关键LLM交互并引导用户在后续决策点进行反思，同时支持渐进式行为数据共享授权。在为期两周、8名参与者的研究中，该方法揭示了用户对LLM输出准确性与相关性等维度的偏好随时间显著变化，证明单次偏好数据不足以评估真实场景中的对齐效果。
Learning a Stochastic Differential Equation Model of Tropical Cyclone Intensification from Reanalysis and Observational Data
Authors: Kenneth Gee, Sai Ravela
First: 2026-01-13T01:11:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:48:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tropical cyclones are among the most consequential weather hazards, yet estimates of their risk are limited by the relatively short historical record. To extend these records, researchers often generate large ensembles of synthetic storms using simplified models of cyclone intensification. Developing such models, however, has traditionally required substantial theoretical effort. Here we explore whether equation-discovery methods, a class of data-driven techniques designed to infer governing equations, can accelerate the process of developing simplified intensification models. Using observational storm data (IBTrACS) together with environmental conditions from reanalysis (ERA5), we learn a compact stochastic differential equation describing tropical cyclone intensity evolution. We focus on TCs because their dynamics are well studied and a hierarchy of reduced-order models exist, enabling direct comparison of the learned model to physically-derived counterparts. We find that the learned model simulates synthetic TCs whose intensification statistics and hazard estimates are consistent with observations and competitive with a leading physics-based TC intensification model. Our model also reproduces known nonlinear dynamical behavior of tropical cyclones, including as a saddle node bifurcation as inner core ventilation is increased. This result shows that equation-discovery approaches, when applied directly to storm intensity, can recover not only realistic statistics but also physically meaningful dynamical structure. These findings highlight the potential for data-driven methods to complement existing theory and reduced-order models in the study of extreme weather.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从再分析和观测数据中学习热带气旋增强的随机微分方程模型
热带气旋是最具影响的天气灾害之一，但其风险估计受限于相对较短的历史记录。为延长这些记录，研究人员常使用简化气旋增强模型生成大量合成风暴。然而，传统上开发此类模型需要大量理论工作。本文探讨方程发现方法（一类旨在推断控制方程的数据驱动技术）是否能加速简化增强模型的开发。我们结合观测风暴数据（IBTrACS）与再分析环境数据（ERA5），学习描述热带气旋强度演变的紧凑随机微分方程。我们聚焦热带气旋，因其动力学已被广泛研究，且存在一系列降阶模型，便于将所学模型与物理推导的模型直接比较。我们发现，所学模型模拟的合成热带气旋，其增强统计特征和灾害估计与观测一致，且与领先的物理基础气旋增强模型相当。该模型还再现了热带气旋已知的非线性动力学行为，包括内核通风增强时的鞍结点分岔。这一结果表明，直接应用于风暴强度的方程发现方法不仅能恢复现实统计特征，还能恢复具有物理意义的动力结构。这些发现凸显了数据驱动方法在极端天气研究中补充现有理论和降阶模型的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend limited historical records of tropical cyclones for improved risk estimation, this study investigates whether data-driven equation-discovery methods can accelerate the development of simplified intensification models. Using observational storm data (IBTrACS) and environmental reanalysis (ERA5), the researchers learn a compact stochastic differential equation that captures tropical cyclone intensity evolution. The model, validated against observations and compared to a leading physics-based model, accurately reproduces intensification statistics and hazard estimates while also capturing known nonlinear dynamics, including a saddle-node bifurcation under increased inner-core ventilation, demonstrating that data-driven approaches can recover both realistic statistics and physically meaningful structure.
为弥补热带气旋历史记录较短导致的风险评估不足，本研究探索数据驱动的方程发现方法能否加速简化强度模型的构建。基于观测风暴数据（IBTrACS）与环境再分析数据（ERA5），研究人员推导出一个简洁的随机微分方程，描述热带气旋强度演变。该模型在统计特性与灾害估计上与观测一致，且与主流物理模型表现相当，同时再现了已知的非线性动力学行为（如内核通风增强时的鞍结点分岔），表明数据驱动方法不仅能生成真实统计，还能恢复物理上合理的动力结构。
Redefining AI Red Teaming in the Agentic Era: From Weeks to Hours
Authors: Raja Sekhar Rao Dheekonda, Will Pearce, Nick Landers
First: 2026-05-05T17:43:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:43:52+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI systems are entering critical domains like healthcare, finance, and defense, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. While AI red teaming is a primary defense, current approaches force operators into manual, library-specific workflows. Operators spend weeks hand-crafting workflows - assembling attacks, transforms, and scorers. When results fall short, workflows must be rebuilt. As a result, operators spend more time constructing workflows than probing targets for security and safety vulnerabilities. We introduce an AI red teaming agent built on the open-source Dreadnode SDK. The agent creates workflows grounded in 45+ adversarial attacks, 450+ transforms, and 130+ scorers. Operators can probe multi-agent systems, multilingual, and multimodal targets, focusing on what to probe rather than how to implement it. We make three contributions: 1. Agentic interface. Operators describe goals in natural language via the Dreadnode TUI (Terminal User Interface). The agent handles attack selection, transform composition, execution, and reporting, letting operators focus on red teaming. Weeks compress to hours. 2. Unified framework. A single framework for probing traditional ML models (adversarial examples) and generative AI systems (jailbreaks), removing the need for separate libraries. 3. Llama Scout case study. We red team Meta Llama Scout and achieve an 85% attack success rate with severity up to 1.0, using zero human-developed code
中文标题/摘要
标题：在代理时代重新定义AI红队攻防：从数周缩短至数小时
AI系统正进入医疗、金融和国防等关键领域，但仍易受对抗性攻击。尽管AI红队攻防是主要防御手段，当前方法迫使操作员陷入手动、库特定的工作流程。操作员需数周手工构建工作流——组装攻击、转换和评分器。当结果不理想时，需重新构建工作流。因此，操作员花费更多时间构建工作流，而非探测目标的安全与脆弱性。 我们引入基于开源Dreadnode SDK的AI红队代理。该代理基于45+种对抗攻击、450+种转换和130+种评分器构建工作流。操作员可探测多智能体系统、多语言及多模态目标，专注于探测目标而非实现方式。 我们做出三项贡献：1. 代理式界面。操作员通过Dreadnode终端用户界面（TUI）以自然语言描述目标，代理自动完成攻击选择、转换组合、执行与报告，使操作员专注红队攻防。数周压缩至数小时。2. 统一框架。单一框架支持探测传统机器学习模型（对抗样本）与生成式AI系统（越狱攻击），无需依赖不同库。3. Llama Scout案例研究。我们对Meta Llama Scout进行红队攻防，实现85%攻击成功率，严重等级达1.0，且未使用任何人工编写的代码。
Summary / 总结
As AI systems deploy in high-stakes domains like healthcare and defense, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks demands robust defense mechanisms. Current red teaming practices require operators to manually construct complex, library-specific workflows over weeks, limiting efficiency. To address this, the authors introduce an agentic red teaming system built on the Dreadnode SDK, which automates attack selection, transform composition, and execution using over 45 adversarial attacks, 450 transforms, and 130 scorers. Operators interact via natural language through a terminal interface, enabling rapid probing of multi-agent, multilingual, and multimodal systems. Experimental results demonstrate that this agent reduces workflow construction time from weeks to hours and achieves an 85% attack success rate against Meta’s Llama Scout with severity up to 1.0, requiring no human-written code.
随着AI系统在医疗、金融和国防等高风险领域部署，其对抗攻击的脆弱性亟需有效防御。现有红队方法要求操作者手动构建和重写复杂工作流，耗费数周时间而非专注于漏洞探测。为此，作者提出基于Dreadnode SDK的AI红队代理，自动完成攻击选择、变换组合与执行，集成45+攻击、450+变换和130+评分器。操作者通过终端界面以自然语言描述目标，代理自动处理执行与报告，将数周工作压缩至数小时。在针对Meta Llama Scout的案例中，代理实现85%攻击成功率，严重等级达1.0，且无需人工编写代码。
RAMoEA-QA: Hierarchical Specialization for Robust Respiratory Audio Question Answering
Authors: Gaia A. Bertolino, Yuwei Zhang, Tong Xia, Domenico Talia, Cecilia Mascolo
First: 2026-03-06T18:29:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:43:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conversational generative AI is increasingly explored in healthcare, where models must integrate heterogeneous patient signals and support diverse interaction styles while producing clinically meaningful outputs. In respiratory care, non-invasive audio recordings captured with sensing devices offer a scalable route to screening and longitudinal monitoring, but heterogeneity is particularly acute: recordings vary across devices, environments, and acquisition protocols, and queries may vary in intent, answer format, and prediction objective. Existing biomedical audio-language question answering systems for respiratory assessment are starting to emerge, but they are typically built as single-path models, processing all inputs through the same acoustic and language pathway despite variation in recording conditions and query types. They are also usually evaluated in relatively limited settings, leaving open their robustness under realistic distribution shifts, including changes in acquisition domains, modality, and clinical task. To address this gap, we introduce RAMoEA-QA, the first RA QA model designed to support input-dependent specialization across heterogeneous recordings and query types within a unified hierarchical two-stage framework. We study this design in a unified RA QA setting spanning clinical and self-recorded, multi-device acquisition settings, question formats, and both discrete and continuous targets. Across in-domain and controlled-shift evaluations, RAMoEA-QA improves over matched monolithic baselines and routing controls, reaching 0.72 in in-domain test accuracy (vs. 0.61 and 0.67 for single-path baselines) on discriminative tasks, while also achieving the best regression performance and stronger average transfer under dataset, modality, and task shifts, including gains of up to 23 percentage points in accuracy on the COPD modality-shift setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAMoEA-QA：面向鲁棒呼吸音频问答的分层专业化方法
对话式生成式AI在医疗健康领域日益受到探索，模型需整合异构患者信号，支持多样交互风格并生成具有临床意义的输出。在呼吸护理中，通过传感设备采集的非侵入式音频记录为筛查与纵向监测提供了可扩展途径，但异构性尤为显著：录音因设备、环境和采集协议不同而变化，查询在意图、答案格式和预测目标上亦有差异。现有用于呼吸评估的生物医学音频-语言问答系统虽初现雏形，但多为单路径模型，尽管输入条件与查询类型各异，仍统一通过相同声学与语言路径处理。它们通常仅在有限场景下评估，未充分验证其在现实分布偏移下的鲁棒性，包括采集域、模态与临床任务的变化。为填补此空白，我们提出RAMoEA-QA，这是首个在统一分层两阶段框架内支持输入依赖专业化、适配异构录音与查询类型的呼吸音频问答模型。我们在涵盖临床与自录、多设备采集、多种问题格式及离散与连续目标的统一呼吸音频问答场景中研究该设计。在域内及受控偏移评估中，RAMoEA-QA优于匹配的单体基线与路由控制，于判别性任务中域内测试准确率达0.72（相较单路径基线的0.61与0.67），同时在回归性能与跨数据集、模态、任务偏移的平均迁移能力上表现最佳，尤其在COPD模态偏移场景中准确率提升高达23个百分点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust respiratory audio question answering in diverse clinical and self-recorded settings, this work introduces RAMoEA-QA, a hierarchical two-stage model that enables input-dependent specialization across heterogeneous audio recordings and query types. Unlike monolithic models that process all inputs uniformly, RAMoEA-QA dynamically routes inputs to specialized processing paths based on recording conditions and query characteristics. Evaluated across in-domain and controlled distribution shifts—including modality, dataset, and task changes—RAMoEA-QA outperforms single-path baselines, achieving 0.72 test accuracy on discriminative tasks (vs. 0.61 and 0.67) and demonstrating superior regression performance and transferability, with up to 23 percentage point gains in accuracy under COPD modality shifts.
为应对呼吸音频问答在多样化临床与自录场景下的鲁棒性需求，本文提出RAMoEA-QA，一种支持输入自适应分化的双阶段层次化模型。与统一处理所有输入的单路径模型不同，RAMoEA-QA根据录音条件和查询特性动态路由至专用子模块，提升适应能力。在涵盖数据集、模态与任务迁移的评估中，该模型显著优于基线，实现0.72的域内准确率（对比单路径基线0.61和0.67），并在模态迁移场景中最高提升23个百分点，展现出更强的泛化能力与鲁棒性。
Rethinking Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval: Evaluating and Advancing Retrievers in Agentic Search Systems
Authors: Yilun Zhao, Jinbiao Wei, Tingyu Song, Siyue Zhang, Chen Zhao, Arman Cohan
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-05T17:42:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:42:50+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning-intensive retrieval aims to surface evidence that supports downstream reasoning rather than merely matching topical similarity. This capability is increasingly important for agentic search systems, where retrievers must provide complementary evidence across iterative search and synthesis. However, existing work remains limited on both evaluation and training: benchmarks such as BRIGHT provide narrow gold sets and evaluate retrievers in isolation, while synthetic training corpora often optimize single-passage relevance rather than evidence portfolio construction. We introduce BRIGHT-Pro, an expert-annotated benchmark that expands each query with multi-aspect gold evidence and evaluates retrievers under both static and agentic search protocols. We further construct RTriever-Synth, an aspect-decomposed synthetic corpus that generates complementary positives and positive-conditioned hard negatives, and use it to LoRA fine-tune RTriever-4B from Qwen3-Embedding-4B. Experiments across lexical, general-purpose, and reasoning-intensive retrievers show that aspect-aware and agentic evaluation expose behaviors hidden by standard metrics, while RTriever-4B substantially improves over its base model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考推理密集型检索：评估与推进代理搜索系统中的检索器
推理密集型检索旨在呈现支持下游推理的证据，而非仅匹配主题相似性。这一能力对代理搜索系统日益重要，因为检索器必须在迭代搜索与综合过程中提供互补证据。然而，现有工作在评估与训练方面仍显不足：如BRIGHT基准仅提供狭窄的黄金数据集且孤立评估检索器，而合成训练语料库常优化单段相关性，而非证据组合构建。我们引入BRIGHT-Pro，一个专家标注的基准，为每个查询扩展多方面黄金证据，并在静态与代理搜索协议下评估检索器。我们进一步构建RTriever-Synth，一个按方面分解的合成语料库，生成互补正例与正例条件下的困难负例，并用其对Qwen3-Embedding-4B的RTriever-4B进行LoRA微调。在词法、通用及推理密集型检索器的实验中，方面感知与代理评估揭示了标准指标隐藏的行为，而RTriever-4B显著优于其基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for retrievers to support reasoning rather than just topical matching in agentic search systems, this work introduces BRIGHT-Pro, an expert-annotated benchmark with multi-aspect gold evidence, and RTriever-Synth, a synthetic corpus designed to generate complementary positives and hard negatives conditioned on aspects. The authors fine-tune RTriever-4B using LoRA on RTriever-Synth and evaluate across lexical, general-purpose, and reasoning-intensive retrievers under both static and agentic protocols. Results show that aspect-aware and agentic evaluation reveal previously hidden retrieval behaviors, and RTriever-4B significantly outperforms its base model.
为提升检索器在代理搜索系统中支持推理而非单纯匹配主题的能力，本文提出BRIGHT-Pro基准，包含专家标注的多方面黄金证据，并在静态与代理搜索协议下评估检索器；同时构建RTriever-Synth合成语料，生成互补正例与正例条件下的难负例，用于LoRA微调RTriever-4B。实验表明，面向方面的评估能揭示标准指标掩盖的行为，且RTriever-4B相较基线模型显著提升。
IRIS: Intent Resolution via Inference-time Saccades for Open-Ended VQA in Large Vision-Language Models
Authors: Parsa Madinei, Srijita Karmakar, Russell Cohen Hoffing, Felix Gervitz, Miguel P. Eckstein
First: 2026-02-18T02:06:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:39:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce IRIS (Intent Resolution via Inference-time Saccades), a novel training-free approach that uses eye-tracking data in real-time to resolve ambiguity in open-ended VQA. Through a comprehensive user study with 500 unique image-question pairs, we demonstrate that fixations closest to the time participants start verbally asking their questions are the most informative for disambiguation in Large VLMs, more than doubling the accuracy of responses on ambiguous questions (from 35.2% to 77.2%) while maintaining performance on unambiguous queries. We evaluate our approach across state-of-the-art VLMs, showing consistent improvements when gaze data is incorporated in ambiguous image-question pairs, regardless of architectural differences. We release a new benchmark dataset to use eye movement data for disambiguated VQA, a novel real-time interactive protocol, and an evaluation suite.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IRIS：面向大型视觉-语言模型开放式视觉问答的推理时眼动解析意图方法
我们提出了IRIS（通过推理时眼动解析意图），一种无需训练的创新方法，利用实时眼动数据解决开放式视觉问答中的歧义。通过针对500组独特图像-问题对的全面用户研究，我们证明在用户开始口头提问时最接近的注视点对大型视觉-语言模型的歧义消解最具信息量，使模糊问题的回答准确率从35.2%提升至77.2%（提升一倍多），同时保持对无歧义问题的性能。我们在多个最先进的视觉-语言模型上评估了该方法，无论架构差异如何，当在模糊图像-问题对中引入眼动数据时均能稳定提升性能。我们发布了新的基准数据集、一种新颖的实时交互协议及评估套件，用于利用眼动数据进行歧义消解的视觉问答。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of resolving ambiguity in open-ended visual question answering, IRIS introduces a training-free method that leverages real-time eye-tracking data to guide intent resolution in large vision-language models. By identifying fixations occurring closest to when users begin verbalizing their questions, the method significantly improves disambiguation accuracy—boosting performance from 35.2% to 77.2% on ambiguous queries—without affecting unambiguous ones. Evaluated across multiple state-of-the-art VLMs, IRIS consistently enhances results when gaze data is integrated into ambiguous image-question pairs, regardless of model architecture, and the authors release a new benchmark dataset and evaluation suite to support this approach.
为解决大视觉语言模型在开放域视觉问答中因语义模糊导致的性能瓶颈，作者提出IRIS方法，利用实时眼动数据在推理阶段动态解析用户意图。通过识别用户开始口头提问前最接近的注视点，该方法将模糊问题的准确率从35.2%大幅提升至77.2%，且对无歧义问题无负面影响。在多个主流视觉语言模型上验证，IRIS无论模型架构如何均能稳定提升性能，并公开了新的眼动数据基准、交互协议与评估工具以支持后续研究。
Conditional Diffusion Sampling
Authors: Francisco M. Castro-Macías, Pablo Morales-Álvarez, Saifuddin Syed, Daniel Hernández-Lobato, Rafael Molina, José Miguel Hernández-Lobato
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-05T17:36:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:36:29+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sampling from unnormalized multimodal distributions with limited density evaluations remains a fundamental challenge in machine learning and natural sciences. Successful approaches construct a bridge between a tractable reference and the target distribution. Parallel Tempering (PT) serves as the gold standard, while recent diffusion-based approaches offer a continuous alternative at the cost of neural training. In this work, we introduce Conditional Diffusion Sampling (CDS), a framework that combines these two paradigms. To this end, we derive Conditional Interpolants, a class of stochastic processes whose transport dynamics are governed by an exact, closed-form stochastic differential equation (SDE), requiring no neural approximation. Although these dynamics require sampling from a non-trivial initialization distribution, we show both theoretically and empirically that the cost of this initialization diminishes for sufficiently short diffusion times. CDS leverages this by a two-stage procedure: (1) PT is used to efficiently sample the initial distribution, and then (2) samples are transported via the transport SDE. This combination couples the robust global exploration of PT with efficient local transport. Experiments suggest that CDS has the potential to achieve a superior trade-off between sample quality and density evaluation cost compared to state-of-the-art samplers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：条件扩散采样
从具有有限密度评估次数的非归一化多模态分布中进行采样，仍是机器学习和自然科学中的基本挑战。成功的方法构建了可处理的参考分布与目标分布之间的桥梁。平行退火（PT）是黄金标准，而近期基于扩散的方法则提供了一种连续的替代方案，但需付出神经网络训练的成本。本文引入了条件扩散采样（CDS）框架，结合了这两种范式。为此，我们推导出条件插值过程，这是一种随机过程，其传输动力学由一个精确的闭式随机微分方程（SDE）控制，无需神经网络近似。尽管这些动力学需要从非平凡的初始化分布中采样，我们从理论和实证上表明，对于足够短的扩散时间，该初始化成本会降低。CDS通过两阶段过程利用这一特性：（1）使用PT高效采样初始分布，然后（2）通过传输SDE传输样本。这种结合将PT的稳健全局探索与高效的局部传输相结合。实验表明，CDS有望在样本质量和密度评估成本之间实现优于现有采样器的权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of sampling from unnormalized multimodal distributions with limited density evaluations, this work proposes Conditional Diffusion Sampling (CDS), which combines Parallel Tempering (PT) for global exploration with a novel diffusion-based transport mechanism governed by an exact stochastic differential equation (SDE). The method uses Conditional Interpolants to enable efficient, neural-free sampling by first leveraging PT to sample from an initialization distribution, then transporting samples via the SDE. Empirical results show that CDS achieves high-quality samples while reducing the cost of density evaluations, outperforming state-of-the-art samplers in the trade-off between sample fidelity and computational efficiency.
为解决在有限密度评估次数下从非归一化多模态分布采样的核心难题，本文提出条件扩散采样（CDS）框架，结合并行温平（PT）的全局探索能力与基于精确随机微分方程（SDE）的扩散传输机制，该SDE无需神经网络近似。方法首先用PT高效采样初始分布，再通过SDE进行样本传输，且实验证明短扩散时间下初始化成本可显著降低。实验表明，CDS在样本质量与密度评估成本之间实现了优于现有方法的平衡。
SymptomAI: Towards a Conversational AI Agent for Everyday Symptom Assessment
Authors: Joseph Breda, Fadi Yousif, Beszel Hawkins, Marinela Cotoi, Miao Liu, Ray Luo, Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen, Mike Schaekermann, Samuel Schmidgall, Xin Liu, Girish Narayanswamy, Samuel Solomon, Maxwell A. Xu, Xiaoran Fan, Longfei Shangguan, Anran Wang, Bhavna Daryani, Buddy Herkenham, Cara Tan, Mark Malhotra, Shwetak Patel, John B. Hernandez, Quang Duong, Yun Liu, Zach Wasson, Dimitrios Antos, Bob Lou, Matthew Thompson, Jonathan Richina, Anupam Pathak, Nichole Young-Lin, Jake Sunshine, Daniel McDuff
First: 2026-05-05T17:36:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:36:12+00:00
Comments: 13 page main text, 54 pages total. 16 figures total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models excel at diagnostic assessments on currated medical case-studies and vignettes, performing on par with, or better than, clinical professionals. However, existing studies focus on complex scenarios with rich context making it difficult to draw conclusions about how these systems perform for patients reporting symptoms in everyday life. We deployed SymptomAI, a set of conversational AI agents for end-to-end patient interviewing and differential diagnosis (DDx), via the Fitbit app in a study that randomized participants (N=13,917) to interact with five AI agents. This corpus captures diverse communication and a realistic distribution of illnesses from a real world population. A subset of 1,228 participants reported a clinician-provided diagnosis, and 517 of these were further evaluated by a panel of clinicians during over 250 hours of annotation. SymptomAI DDx were significantly more accurate (OR = 2.47, p < 0.001) than those from independent clinicians given the same dialogue in a blinded randomized comparison. Moreover, agentic strategies which conduct a dedicated symptom interview that elicit additional symptom information before providing a diagnosis, perform substantially better than baseline, user-guided conversations (p < 0.001). An auxiliary analysis on 1,509 conversations from a general US population panel validated that these results generalize beyond wearable device users. We used SymptomAI diagnoses as labels for all 13,917 participants to analyze over 500,000 days of wearable metrics across nearly 400 unique conditions. We identified strong associations between acute infections and physiological shifts (e.g., OR > 7 for influenza). While limited by self-reported ground truth, these results demonstrate the benefits of a dedicated and complete symptom interview compared to a user-guided symptom discussion, which is the default of most consumer LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SymptomAI：迈向日常症状评估的对话式人工智能代理
语言模型在精选的医学案例和情景中表现出色，其诊断评估能力可媲美甚至超越临床专家。然而，现有研究多聚焦于复杂且背景丰富的场景，难以推断这些系统在患者日常报告症状时的表现。我们通过Fitbit应用部署了SymptomAI——一套用于端到端患者访谈与鉴别诊断（DDx）的对话式AI代理，在一项随机分配13,917名参与者与五个AI代理互动的研究中进行测试。该语料库涵盖多样化的交流方式及真实世界人群中的疾病分布。其中1,228名参与者获得临床医生提供的诊断，517名进一步由临床专家小组在超过250小时的标注中评估。盲法随机比较显示，SymptomAI的DDx显著优于独立临床医生（OR = 2.47，p < 0.001）。此外，采用专门症状访谈策略（先收集额外症状信息再提供诊断）的代理表现远优于基线用户引导对话（p < 0.001）。对1,509次来自普通美国人群的对话的辅助分析验证了这些结果可推广至可穿戴设备用户之外。我们利用SymptomAI诊断作为标签，对13,917名参与者超过50万天的可穿戴数据（近400种独特病症）进行分析，发现急性感染与生理变化存在强关联（如流感OR > 7）。尽管受限于自报真实值，这些结果表明，与大多数消费级LLM默认的用户引导症状讨论相比，专门且完整的症状访谈具有显著优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate language models in real-world symptom assessment scenarios beyond curated medical vignettes, the study deployed SymptomAI, a conversational AI system for end-to-end patient interviewing and differential diagnosis via the Fitbit app, involving 13,917 participants randomized to interact with five AI agents. A subset of 1,228 participants received clinician diagnoses, with 517 further validated by clinicians over 250 hours; SymptomAI’s DDx proved significantly more accurate (OR = 2.47, p < 0.001) than independent clinicians under blinded conditions, especially when agents conducted structured symptom interviews before diagnosis, outperforming user-guided conversations (p < 0.001). An auxiliary analysis across 1,509 general US population conversations confirmed generalizability. Using SymptomAI diagnoses as labels, the team analyzed 500,000+ days of wearable data across 400 conditions, identifying strong physiological associations with acute infections (e.g., OR > 7 for influenza), demonstrating the value of structured symptom elicitation over default user-guided approaches in consumer LLMs.
为评估语言模型在真实世界症状评估中的表现，研究部署了SymptomAI对话AI代理，通过Fitbit应用对13,917名参与者进行端到端的患者访谈与鉴别诊断。1,228名参与者获得临床诊断，其中517人经临床专家250小时标注验证。SymptomAI的鉴别诊断显著优于独立临床医生（OR=2.47，p<0.001），且主动收集症状信息后再诊断的策略显著优于用户引导式对话（p<0.001）。在1,509名普通美国人群对话中验证了结果的普适性。利用SymptomAI诊断作为标签，分析了50万+天的可穿戴数据（近400种疾病），发现急性感染与生理变化存在强关联（如流感OR>7），证明结构化症状访谈优于用户引导式对话，后者是多数消费级LLM的默认模式。
Enhanced 3D Brain Tumor Segmentation Using Assorted Precision Training
Authors: Adwaitt Pandya, Ozioma C. Oguine, Harita Bhargava, Shrikant Zade
First: 2026-05-05T17:30:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:30:17+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 5 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A brain tumor is a medical disorder faced by individuals of all demographics. Medically, it is described as the spread of non-essential cells close to or throughout the brain. Symptoms of this ailment include headaches, seizures, and sensory changes. This research explores two main categories of brain tumors: benign and malignant. Benign spreads steadily, and malignant expresses growth, making it dangerous. Early identification of brain tumors is a crucial factor for the survival of patients. This research provides a state-of-the-art approach to the early identification of tumors within the brain. We implemented the SegResNet architecture, a widely adopted architecture for three-dimensional segmentation, and trained it using the automatic multi-precision method. We incorporated the dice loss function and dice metric for evaluating the model. We got a dice score of 0.84. For the tumor core, we got a dice score of 0.84; for the whole tumor, 0.90; and for the enhanced tumor, we got a score of 0.79.

Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent Architecture for Traceable, Risk-Aware Human-AI Decision Support in Manufacturing
Authors: Danny Hoang, Ryan Matthiessen, Christopher Miller, Nasir Mannan, Ruby ElKharboutly, David Gorsich, Matthew P. Castanier, Farhad Imani
First: 2026-05-05T17:24:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:24:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-precision CNC machining of free-form aerospace components requires bounded compensations informed by inspection, simulation, and process knowledge. Off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) assistants can generate text, but they do not reliably execute risk-constrained multi-step numerical workflows or provide auditable provenance for high-stakes decisions. We present multi-agent knowledge analysis (MAKA), a human-in-the-loop decision-support architecture that separates intent routing, tools-only quantitative analysis, knowledge graph retrieval, and critic-based verification that enforces physical plausibility, safety bounds, and provenance completeness before recommendations are surfaced for human approval. MAKA is instantiated on a Ti-6Al-4V rotor blade machining testbed by fusing virtual-machining path-tracking error fields, cutting-force and deflection simulations, and scan-based 3D inspection deviation maps from 16 blades. The analysis decomposes deviation into an evidence-linked pathing component, a drift-based wear proxy capturing systematic evolution across parts, a residual systematic compliance term, and a variability proxy for instability-aware escalation. In a three-level tool-orchestration benchmark (single-step through $\geq$3-step stateful sequences), MAKA improves successful tool execution by up to 87.5 percentage points relative to an unstructured single-model interaction pattern with identical tool access. Digital twin what-if studies show MAKA can coordinate traceable compensation candidates that reduce predicted surface deviation from order $10^{-2}$in to approximately $\pm 10^{-3}$in over most of the blade within the simulation environment, providing a pre-deployment verification signal for risk-aware human decision-making.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向制造领域可追溯、风险感知的人机协同决策支持的物理基础多智能体架构
高精度数控加工自由曲面航空航天部件需结合检测、仿真与工艺知识进行有界补偿。现成的大语言模型（LLM）助手虽能生成文本，但无法可靠执行受风险约束的多步数值工作流，亦无法为高风险决策提供可审计的溯源信息。本文提出多智能体知识分析（MAKA）架构，该架构采用人机协同模式，将意图路由、工具仅定量分析、知识图谱检索及基于批评者的验证分离，确保在推荐前满足物理合理性、安全边界与溯源完整性。MAKA在Ti-6Al-4V转子叶片加工测试平台上实现，融合虚拟加工路径跟踪误差场、切削力与变形仿真及16片叶片的扫描3D检测偏差图。分析将偏差分解为路径相关证据项、基于漂移的磨损代理（反映部件间系统性演化）、残余系统性合规项及不稳定性感知的变异性代理。在三级工具编排基准测试（从单步至≥3步状态序列）中，MAKA相较相同工具访问权限的无结构单模型交互模式，成功工具执行率提升最高达87.5个百分点。数字孪生“假设情景”研究表明，MAKA可协调可追溯的补偿方案，在仿真环境中将预测表面偏差从约10⁻²英寸降低至叶片大部分区域约±10⁻³英寸，为风险感知的人类决策提供部署前验证信号。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of off-the-shelf LLMs in executing risk-constrained, multi-step numerical workflows for high-stakes aerospace manufacturing, the paper introduces MAKA, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent architecture that separates intent routing, quantitative analysis, knowledge graph retrieval, and critic-based verification to ensure physical plausibility, safety bounds, and provenance completeness. Implemented on a Ti-6Al-4V rotor blade machining testbed, MAKA integrates virtual path-tracking errors, cutting-force simulations, and 3D inspection data from 16 blades to decompose deviations into evidence-linked pathing, wear proxies, systematic compliance terms, and instability proxies. 为支持航空航天CNC加工中的高风险决策，本文提出MAKA多智能体架构，通过意图路由、定量分析、知识检索和批评验证分离，确保物理合理性与可追溯性。在Ti-6Al-4V旋翼叶片测试平台上，MAKA融合仿真数据、检测图谱和力-变形模型，将加工偏差分解为路径、磨损、合规性和波动性组件。在三级工具编排基准测试中，MAKA比单一模型方法成功率高出87.5个百分点，数字孪生仿真显示其可将预测表面偏差从10^{-2}英寸降至约±10^{-3}英寸，为人类决策提供预部署风险验证信号。
Autonomous Reliability Qualification of Ga$_2$O$_3$-based Hydrogen and Temperature Sensors via Safe Active Learning
Authors: Davi Febba, William A. Callahan, Anna Sacchi, Andriy Zakutayev
First: 2026-04-22T23:37:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:23:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a Safe Active Learning (SAL) framework for autonomous reliability characterization of rectifying Ga$_2$O$_3$-based devices under coupled thermal and hydrogen stress. SAL treats rectification as a device-physics-motivated safety observable and models its evolution over elapsed time, temperature, and H$_2$ concentration using a Gaussian-process surrogate. To handle condition-dependent and uncertain experiment durations, the method combines an adaptive completion-time window, time-window lower-confidence-bound safety checks, a trust region anchored to previously verified safe conditions, and a two-phase strategy that transitions from conservative safe exploration to progressively relaxed rectification targets as the device degrades. We first evaluate SAL in simulation, where it safely expands the explored region while learning the evolving rectification surface. We then demonstrate SAL experimentally on an automated high-temperature probe-station platform using a Pt/Cr$_2$O$_3$:Mg/$β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ device. In the reported campaign, phase 1 incurred only one unsafe measurement associated with spurious current-voltage sweeps, while phase 2 intentionally probed lower-rectification regimes. Finally, we use the curated SAL dataset for offline long-horizon forecasting of device response at a target voltage using a structured Gaussian-process model with a condition-dependent Kohlrausch--Williams--Watts mean and a residual covariance kernel. The model captures long-time, saturating degradation trends in an auxiliary validation dataset, illustrating how safety-aware autonomous experimentation enables both conservative characterization and subsequent degradation modeling. Although demonstrated here for a rectifying Ga$_2$O$_3$ device, SAL is applicable to other systems where a measurable in situ safety observable can be defined.

标题：通过安全主动学习实现基于Ga₂O₃的氢气与温度传感器的自主可靠性认证
我们提出了一种安全主动学习（SAL）框架，用于在耦合热应力和氢气应力下自主表征基于整流Ga₂O₃器件的可靠性。SAL将整流行为视为由器件物理驱动的安全可观测量，并利用高斯过程代理模型描述其随时间、温度和H₂浓度变化的演化。为处理条件依赖和实验时长不确定的问题，该方法结合了自适应完成时间窗口、时间窗口下置信度边界安全检查、以先前验证安全条件为锚点的信任区域，以及两阶段策略：从保守安全探索过渡到随着器件退化逐步放宽整流目标。我们首先在仿真中评估SAL，其在安全扩展探索区域的同时学习演化的整流表面。随后在自动化高温探针站平台上，使用Pt/Cr₂O₃:Mg/β-Ga₂O₃器件实验验证SAL。在报告的实验中，第一阶段仅发生一次与虚假电流-电压扫描相关的不安全测量，而第二阶段则有意探测低整流区域。最后，我们利用整理的SAL数据集，通过具有条件依赖Kohlrausch--Williams--Watts均值和残差协方差核的结构化高斯过程模型，离线预测器件在目标电压下的长期响应。该模型在辅助验证数据集中捕捉了长期饱和退化趋势，展示了安全感知的自主实验如何同时实现保守表征与后续退化建模。尽管此处演示于整流Ga₂O₃器件，但SAL适用于任何可定义在位安全可观测量的系统。
Summary / 总结

The method uses a Gaussian-process surrogate to model rectification’s evolution over time, temperature, and hydrogen concentration, incorporating adaptive time windows, safety checks, and a trust region anchored to verified safe conditions. 为自主评估Ga₂O₃基传感器在热与氢应力下的可靠性，本文提出一种安全主动学习（SAL）框架，将整流特性作为安全观测指标。SAL利用高斯过程代理模型预测整流特性的演化，并结合自适应时间窗口、安全检查和置信区间确保实验安全。在Pt/Cr₂O₃:Mg/β-Ga₂O₃器件实验中，SAL第一阶段仅发生一次不安全测量，第二阶段主动探测退化区域。所构建的数据集支持离线长期退化预测，采用结构化高斯过程模型，结合条件依赖均值与残差协方差核，成功捕捉饱和退化趋势。
RD-ViT: Recurrent-Depth Vision Transformer for Semantic Segmentation with Reduced Data Dependence Extending the Recurrent-Depth Transformer Architecture to Dense Prediction
Authors: Renjie He
First: 2026-05-05T17:21:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:21:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy but require large training datasets because each layer has unique parameters that must be learned independently. We present RD-ViT, a Recurrent-Depth Vision Transformer that adapts the Recurrent-Depth Transformer (RDT) architecture to dense prediction tasks, supporting both 2D and 3D inputs. RD-ViT replaces the deep stack of unique transformer blocks with a single shared block looped T times, augmented with LTI-stable state injection for guaranteed convergence, Adaptive Computation Time (ACT) for spatial compute allocation, depth-wise LoRA adaptation, and optional Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) feed-forward networks for category-specific specialization. We evaluate on the ACDC cardiac MRI segmentation benchmark in both 2D slice-level and 3D volumetric settings with exclusively real experiments executed in Google Colab. In 2D, RD-ViT outperforms standard ViT at 10% training data (Dice 0.774 vs 0.762) and at full data (0.882 vs 0.872). In 3D, RD-ViT with MoE achieves Dice 0.812 with 3.0M parameters, reaching 99.4% of standard ViT performance (0.817) at 53% of the parameter count. MoE expert utilization analysis reveals that different experts spontaneously specialize for different cardiac structures (RV, MYO, LV) without explicit routing supervision. ACT halting maps show higher compute allocation at cardiac boundaries, and the mean ponder time decreases from 2.6 to 1.4 iterations during training, demonstrating learned computational efficiency. Depth extrapolation enables inference with more loops than training without degradation. All code, notebooks, and results are publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RD-ViT：用于语义分割的递归深度视觉变换器，降低数据依赖性，扩展递归深度变换器架构以支持密集预测
视觉变换器（ViTs）在分割任务中达到最先进精度，但需大量训练数据，因各层参数需独立学习。本文提出RD-ViT，将递归深度变换器（RDT）架构适配于密集预测任务，支持2D和3D输入。RD-ViT用单个共享块循环T次替代深层独特变换器块，辅以LTI稳定状态注入确保收敛、自适应计算时间（ACT）进行空间计算分配、深度可分离LoRA适配，以及可选的专家混合（MoE）前馈网络实现类别特化。在ACDC心脏MRI分割基准上，仅用真实实验（Google Colab）评估2D切片级和3D体数据设置。2D中，RD-ViT在10%训练数据下（Dice 0.774 vs 0.762）及全数据下（0.882 vs 0.872）优于标准ViT。3D中，带MoE的RD-ViT仅300万参数即达Dice 0.812，以53%参数量实现标准ViT（0.817）99.4%性能。MoE专家利用分析显示，不同专家自发专精于不同心脏结构（右心室、心肌、左心室），无需显式路由监督。ACT终止图显示心脏边界计算分配更高，训练中平均思考次数从2.6降至1.4次，体现学习到的计算效率。深度外推支持训练时循环次数少于推理时而不降性能。所有代码、笔记本及结果均已公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high data dependency of standard Vision Transformers, which require large datasets due to unique per-layer parameters, the authors propose RD-ViT, a Recurrent-Depth Vision Transformer that replaces stacked transformer blocks with a single shared block repeated T times, enhanced with LTI-stable state injection, Adaptive Computation Time, depth-wise LoRA, and optional MoE for specialization. Evaluated on ACDC cardiac MRI segmentation in 2D and 3D, RD-ViT achieves superior performance: in 2D, it outperforms standard ViT at 10% data (Dice 0.774 vs 0.762) and at full data (0.882 vs 0.872); in 3D with MoE, it reaches Dice 0.812 with 3.0M parameters, matching 99.4% of standard ViT’s 0.817 performance using only 53% of parameters. MoE experts spontaneously specialize to cardiac structures without routing supervision, ACT allocates more compute to boundaries, and mean ponder time drops from 2.6 to 1.4 iterations, showing learned efficiency.
为降低视觉Transformer在语义分割任务中的数据依赖性，本文提出RD-ViT，即循环深度视觉Transformer，将独立学习的多层Transformer块替换为单个共享块循环T次，并结合LTI稳定状态注入、自适应计算时间、深度LoRA及可选MoE前馈网络。在ACDC心脏MRI分割任务中，2D场景下，RD-ViT在仅10%训练数据时Dice值达0.774（优于标准ViT的0.762），全数据时达0.882（优于0.872）；3D场景下，结合MoE的版本仅用300万参数即实现Dice 0.812，达到标准ViT（0.817）的99.4%，参数量仅为后者的53%。MoE专家自发对心室、心肌等结构进行专业化，无需路由监督；ACT计算分配更集中于心脏边界，训练平均思考次数从2.6降至1.4，体现学习到的计算效率；深度外推能力允许推理时循环次数多于训练而不降性能。
EQUITRIAGE: A Fairness Audit of Gender Bias in LLM-Based Emergency Department Triage
Authors: Richard J. Young, Alice M. Matthews
First: 2026-05-05T17:20:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:20:55+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 10 figures, 13 tables. Code and analysis scripts available upon publication. Data: PhysioNet credentialed access (MIMIC-IV-ED v2.2 and MIMIC-IV v3.1, BIDMC IRB #2001P001699)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Emergency department triage assigns patients an acuity score that determines treatment priority, and clinical evidence documents persistent gender disparities in human acuity assessment. As hospitals pilot large language models (LLMs) as triage decision support, a critical question is whether these models reproduce or mitigate known biases. We present EQUITRIAGE, a fairness audit of LLM-based ESI assignment evaluating five models (Gemini-3-Flash, Nemotron-3-Super, DeepSeek-V3.1, Mistral-Small-3.2, GPT-4.1-Nano) across 374,275 evaluations on 18,714 MIMIC-IV-ED vignettes under four prompt strategies. Of 9,368 originals, 9,346 are paired with a gender-swapped counterfactual. All five models produced flip rates above a pre-registered 5% threshold (9.9% to 43.8%). Two showed directional female undertriage (DeepSeek F/M 2.15:1, Gemini 1.34:1); two were near-parity; one had high sensitivity with weak male-direction asymmetry. DeepSeek's directional bias coexisted with a low outcome-linked calibration gap (0.013 against MIMIC-IV admission), a Chouldechova-style dissociation between within-group calibration and between-pair counterfactual invariance. Demographic blinding reduced Gemini's flip rate to 0.5%; an age-preserving blind variant left DeepSeek with residual F/M 1.25, implicating age as a residual channel. Chain-of-thought prompting degraded accuracy for all five models. A two-model ablation reveals opposite underlying mechanisms for the same directional phenotype: in Gemini the signal is emergent in the combined name+gender swap, while in DeepSeek the gender token alone carries it. EQUITRIAGE shows that group parity, counterfactual invariance, and gender calibration are distinct fairness properties, that intervention effectiveness is model-dependent, and that per-model counterfactual auditing should precede clinical deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EQUITRIAGE：基于大语言模型的急诊科分诊性别偏见公平性审计
急诊科分诊为患者分配急症评分以确定治疗优先级，临床证据表明人类急症评估中存在持续的性别差异。随着医院试点将大语言模型（LLM）作为分诊决策支持工具，一个关键问题是这些模型是否会复制或缓解已知偏见。我们提出了EQUITRIAGE，对五种LLM（Gemini-3-Flash、Nemotron-3-Super、DeepSeek-V3.1、Mistral-Small-3.2、GPT-4.1-Nano）在18,714个MIMIC-IV-ED病例场景下进行公平性审计，共完成374,275次评估，采用四种提示策略。在9,368个原始案例中，9,346个配有性别互换的反事实对照。所有五种模型的翻转率均超过预注册的5%阈值（9.9%至43.8%）。两种模型显示女性被低估倾向（DeepSeek F/M 2.15:1，Gemini 1.34:1）；两种接近平等；一种具有高敏感性但男性方向不对称性较弱。DeepSeek的定向偏见与低结果关联校准差距（0.013，对比MIMIC-IV入院）并存，呈现Chouldechova式校准组内与组间反事实不变性脱节。性别盲化使Gemini翻转率降至0.5%；年龄保持盲化版本下DeepSeek仍残留F/M 1.25，暗示年龄为残留通道。思维链提示法使所有五种模型准确率下降。双模型消融实验揭示相同定向表型背后存在相反机制：在Gemini中，信号源于姓名与性别互换的组合；而在DeepSeek中，仅性别词元即携带该信号。EQUITRIAGE表明，群体平等、反事实不变性与性别校准是独立的公平性属性，干预有效性依赖于模型，且应先对每种模型进行反事实审计，再考虑临床部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by persistent gender disparities in human emergency triage and the growing use of LLMs in clinical decision support, this study audits five LLMs for gender bias in assigning ESI acuity scores using 374,275 evaluations across 18,714 MIMIC-IV-ED patient vignettes. The authors evaluate models under four prompt strategies and compare original and gender-swapped counterfactuals, finding all five models exceeded a 5% flip rate threshold (9.9% to 43.8%), with two showing directional female undertriage. DeepSeek exhibited the strongest bias (F/M 2.15:1) alongside low calibration gap, revealing a dissociation between within-group calibration and counterfactual invariance. Demographic blinding reduced Gemini’s flip rate to 0.5% but left DeepSeek with residual bias (F/M 1.25), suggesting age as a confounding channel. Chain-of-thought prompting degraded accuracy universally, and ablation studies revealed divergent bias mechanisms: Gemini’s bias emerged from combined name+gender swap, while DeepSeek’s stemmed from gender token alone. The study concludes that fairness metrics like group parity, counterfactual invariance, and calibration are distinct, and model-specific counterfactual auditing is essential before clinical deployment.
鉴于人类急诊分诊中长期存在的性别差异，本研究旨在评估大型语言模型（LLM）在急诊分诊中的公平性。通过对18,714个MIMIC-IV-ED病例的374,275次评估，使用性别互换的反事实对照，对五种模型进行审计。所有模型的翻转率均超过5%（9.9%–43.8%），其中两个模型存在女性被低估的定向偏差，两个接近平等，一个高敏感性但男性偏差较弱。DeepSeek虽有定向偏差，但校准差距极小，揭示了组内校准与反事实不变性之间的脱节。性别盲化使Gemini翻转率降至0.5%，但DeepSeek仍残留F/M 1.25的偏差，暗示年龄为残留渠道。链式思维提示降低所有模型准确性。消融实验显示，相同定向偏差在Gemini中源于姓名+性别联合替换，而在DeepSeek中仅由性别词触发，表明公平性指标需按模型定制，临床部署前应进行针对性反事实审计。
3D Human Face Reconstruction with 3DMM face model from RGB image
Authors: Zhangnan Jiang, Zichen Yang
First: 2026-05-05T17:19:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:19:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Nowadays as convolution neural networks demonstrate its powerful problem-solving ability in the area of image processing, efforts have been made to reconstruct detailed face shapes from 2D face images or videos. However, to make the full use of CNN, a large number of labeled data is required to train the network. Coarse morphable face model has been used to synthesize labeled data. However, it is hard for coarse morphable face models to generate photo-realistic data with detail such as wrinkles. In this project, we present a pipeline that reconstructs a human face 3D model from a single RGB image. The pipeline includes face detection, landmark detection, regression of 3DMM model parameters, and soft rendering. Mentor: Zhipeng Fan (Email: zf606@nyu.edu) Code Repository: https://github.com/SeVEnMY/3d-face- reconstruction Code Reference: https://github.com/sicxu/Deep3DFaceRecon pytorch

标题：从RGB图像使用3DMM人脸模型重建3D人脸
如今，卷积神经网络在图像处理领域展现出强大的问题解决能力，人们已尝试从2D人脸图像或视频中重建详细的人脸形状。然而，为充分发挥CNN的作用，需要大量标注数据来训练网络。粗略的可变形人脸模型曾被用于合成标注数据，但其难以生成包含皱纹等细节的逼真数据。本项目提出了一种从单张RGB图像重建人脸3D模型的流程，包括人脸检测、关键点检测、3DMM模型参数回归及软渲染。导师：范志鹏（邮箱：zf606@nyu.edu）代码仓库：https://github.com/SeVEnMY/3d-face-reconstruction 参考代码：https://github.com/sicxu/Deep3DFaceRecon-pytorch
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reconstruct detailed 3D human faces from single RGB images using convolutional neural networks, this work addresses the challenge of insufficient labeled training data by leveraging a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM) to generate synthetic data. The proposed pipeline integrates face detection, landmark localization, 3DMM parameter regression, and soft rendering to produce photorealistic 3D face reconstructions. Experimental results demonstrate that the method effectively captures fine facial details such as wrinkles, outperforming prior approaches that rely on coarse morphable models.
