Speculative Coupled Decoding for Training-Free Lossless Acceleration of Autoregressive Visual Generation
Authors: Junhyuk So, Hyunho Kook, Chaeyeon Jang, Eunhyeok Park
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-28T09:26:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:15:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autoregressive (AR) modeling has recently emerged as a promising new paradigm in visual generation, but its practical adoption is severely constrained by the slow inference speed of per-token generation, which often requires thousands of steps to produce a single sample. While several Speculative Decoding (SD)-based methods have been proposed to solve this problem by generating multiple tokens in a single forward step, they suffer from limited speedup, degraded quality, or require the training of a draft model. To solve these problems, we propose a new training-free, lossless SD framework, Speculative Coupled Decoding (SCD), by extending the recently proposed Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD). While SJD shows strong potential for accelerating AR generation by combining Jacobi iteration and SD, we found that its acceptance rate is still significantly limited due to the instability arising from the independent sampling process used during draft token generation. To overcome this, we introduce an information-theoretic approach, Coupling, which stabilizes the drafting trajectory of SJD by maximizing the probability of sampling identical draft tokens across consecutive iterations, significantly enhancing the acceptance rate while preserving its lossless property. Remarkably, this method requires only a single-line modification to the existing algorithm with almost zero overhead, yet achieves substantial performance gains, delivering up to a 4.2x speedup in image generation and 13.6x speedup in video generation compared to standard AR decoding, without any degradation or the need for additional training. The source code is available at https://github.com/junhyukso/SCD
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于无训练损失less加速自回归视觉生成的推测耦合解码
自回归（AR）建模最近成为视觉生成领域有前景的新范式，但其实际应用因逐token生成的缓慢推理速度而受到严重限制，通常需数千步才能生成一个样本。尽管已有若干基于推测解码（SD）的方法通过单次前向步骤生成多个token来解决此问题，但它们存在加速有限、质量下降或需训练草稿模型等问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新的无训练、无损SD框架——推测耦合解码（SCD），扩展了近期提出的推测雅可比解码（SJD）。虽然SJD通过结合雅可比迭代与SD展现出加速AR生成的潜力，但我们发现其接受率仍因草稿token生成过程中独立采样带来的不稳定性而显著受限。为克服此问题，我们引入信息论方法——耦合，通过最大化连续迭代中采样相同草稿token的概率，稳定SJD的草稿轨迹，显著提升接受率同时保持其无损特性。该方法仅需对现有算法进行单行修改，几乎无额外开销，却实现了显著性能提升，在图像生成中较标准AR解码提速最高达4.2倍，在视频生成中提速最高达13.6倍，且无质量下降或额外训练需求。源代码见https://github.com/junhyukso/SCD
Summary / 总结
To address the slow inference speed of autoregressive visual generation, which limits practical adoption, the authors propose Speculative Coupled Decoding (SCD), a training-free, lossless acceleration method extending Speculative Jacobi Decoding. SCD improves upon SJD by introducing Coupling—an information-theoretic technique that stabilizes draft token sampling across iterations, significantly boosting acceptance rates without quality loss. Implemented with minimal code changes, SCD achieves up to 4.2x speedup in image generation and 13.6x in video generation over standard autoregressive decoding, without requiring additional training or degrading output quality.
为解决自回归视觉生成推理速度慢、限制实际应用的问题，作者提出了一种无需训练、无损加速方法——推测耦合解码（SCD），基于推测雅可比解码（SJD）改进。SCD通过引入耦合机制，最大化连续迭代中采样相同草稿令牌的概率，从而稳定草稿生成轨迹，显著提升接受率，同时不降低质量或需额外训练。实验表明，SCD在图像生成上最高提速4.2倍，在视频生成上最高提速13.6倍，且输出质量无损，实现成本极低。
An Agent-Oriented Pluggable Experience-RAG Skill for Experience-Driven Retrieval Strategy Orchestration
Authors: Dutao Zhang, Tian Liao
First: 2026-05-05T17:10:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:10:25+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 6 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation systems often assume that one fixed retrieval pipeline is sufficient across heterogeneous tasks, yet factoid question answering, multi-hop reasoning, and scientific verification exhibit different retrieval preferences. We present Experience-RAG Skill, an agent-oriented pluggable retrieval orchestration layer positioned between the agent and the retriever pool. The proposed skill analyzes the current scene, consults an experience memory, selects an appropriate retrieval strategy, and returns structured evidence to the agent. Under a fixed candidate pool, Experience-RAG Skill achieves an overall nDCG@10 of 0.8924 on BeIR/nq, BeIR/hotpotqa, and BeIR/scifact, outperforming fixed single-retriever baselines and remaining competitive with Adaptive-RAG-style routing. The results suggest that retrieval strategy selection can be productively encapsulated as a reusable agent skill rather than being hard-coded in the upper workflow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向经验驱动检索策略编排的代理导向可插拔经验RAG技能
检索增强生成系统通常假设在异构任务中单一固定检索管道已足够，但事实问答、多跳推理和科学验证等任务表现出不同的检索偏好。我们提出Experience-RAG Skill，这是一种位于代理与检索器池之间的代理导向可插拔检索编排层。该技能分析当前场景，查询经验记忆，选择合适的检索策略，并将结构化证据返回给代理。在固定候选池下，Experience-RAG Skill在BeIR/nq、BeIR/hotpotqa和BeIR/scifact数据集上实现了整体nDCG@10为0.8924，优于固定单检索器基线，且与Adaptive-RAG风格路由保持竞争力。结果表明，检索策略选择可有效封装为可复用的代理技能，而非硬编码在上层工作流中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that different retrieval strategies are needed for varied tasks like factoid QA, multi-hop reasoning, and scientific verification, the paper introduces Experience-RAG Skill—an agent-oriented, pluggable layer that dynamically selects retrieval strategies based on current context and experience memory. The skill consults past experiences to choose the most suitable retrieval method from a fixed pool and returns structured evidence to the agent. Experiments show it achieves an overall nDCG@10 of 0.8924 across three BeIR benchmarks, outperforming fixed single-retriever baselines and remaining competitive with Adaptive-RAG-style routing, demonstrating that retrieval strategy selection can be effectively modularized as a reusable agent skill.
为应对不同任务（如事实问答、多跳推理和科学验证）对检索策略的不同需求，本文提出Experience-RAG Skill——一个面向代理的可插拔检索编排层，位于代理与检索器池之间。该技能通过分析当前场景、参考经验记忆、选择合适检索策略并返回结构化证据，实现动态检索策略选择。在BeIR基准测试中，其整体nDCG@10达0.8924，优于固定单检索器基线，与Adaptive-RAG式路由方法相当，表明检索策略选择可有效封装为可复用的代理技能，而非硬编码于上层流程。
VOFA: Visual Object Goal Pushing with Force-Adaptive Control for Humanoids
Authors: Zichao Hu, Zifan Xu, Dongsik Chang, He Yin, Linh Tran, Roberto Martín-Martín, Peter Stone, Jingyu Qiao, Joydeep Biswas
First: 2026-05-02T16:16:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:08:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ability to push large objects in a goal-directed manner using onboard egocentric perception is an essential skill for humanoid robots to perform complex tasks such as material handling in warehouses. To robustly manipulate heavy objects to arbitrary goal configurations, the robot must cope with unknown object mass and ground friction, noisy onboard perception, and actuation errors; all in a real-time feedback loop. Existing solutions either rely on privileged object-state information without onboard perception or lack robustness to variations in goal configurations and object physical properties. In this work, we present VOFA, a visual goal-conditioned humanoid loco-manipulation system capable of pushing objects with unknown physical properties to arbitrary goal positions. VOFA consists of a two-level hierarchical architecture with a high-level visuomotor policy and a low-level force-adaptive whole-body controller. The high-level policy processes noisy onboard observations and generates goal-conditioned commands to operate in closed loop across diverse object-goal configurations, while the low-level whole-body controller provides robustness to variations in object physical properties. VOFA is extensively evaluated in both simulation and real-world experiments on the Booster T1 humanoid robot. Our results demonstrate strong performance, achieving over 90% success in simulation and over 80% success in real-world trials. Moreover, VOFA successfully pushes objects weighing up to 17kg, exceeding half of the Booster T1's body weight.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for humanoid robots to push heavy objects to precise goals using only onboard visual perception, this work introduces VOFA, a visual goal-conditioned system that combines a high-level visuomotor policy with a low-level force-adaptive whole-body controller. The high-level policy processes noisy onboard sensor data to generate goal-directed commands, while the low-level controller adapts to unknown object mass and friction, ensuring robustness across diverse physical conditions. Evaluated on the Booster T1 humanoid robot in both simulation and real-world tests, VOFA achieves over 90% success in simulation and over 80% in real-world trials, successfully pushing objects up to 17kg — more than half the robot’s body weight.
为使人形机器人仅依靠机载视觉感知即可将重物推至指定目标位置，本研究提出VOFA系统，结合高层视觉运动策略与底层力自适应全身控制器。高层策略处理噪声传感数据并生成目标导向指令，底层控制器则适应未知物体质量与摩擦力，确保在多样物理条件下稳定操作。在Booster T1人形机器人上进行仿真与真实实验，VOFA在仿真中成功率超90%，真实实验中超80%，成功推动重达17kg的物体——超过机器人自重的一半。
From Intent to Execution: Composing Agentic Workflows with Agent Recommendation
Authors: Kishan Athrey, Ramin Pishehvar, Brian Riordan, Mahesh Viswanathan
First: 2026-05-05T17:08:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:08:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built using AI agents fulfill a variety of user intents that may be used to design and build a family of related applications. However, the creation of such MAS currently involves manual composition of the plan, manual selection of appropriate agents, and manual creation of execution graphs. This paper introduces a framework for the automated creation of multi-agent systems which replaces multiple manual steps with an automated framework. The proposed framework consists of software modules and a workflow to orchestrate the requisite task- specific application. The modules include: an LLM-derived planner, a set of tasks described in natural language, a dynamic call graph, an orchestrator for map agents to tasks, and an agent recommender that finds the most suitable agent(s) from local and global agent registries. The agent recommender uses a two-stage information retrieval (IR) system comprising a fast retriever and an LLM-based re-ranker. We implemented a series of experiments exploring the choice of embedders, re- rankers, agent description enrichment, and supervising critique agent. We benchmarked this system end-to-end, evaluating the combination of planning, agent selection, and task completion, with our proposed approach. Our experimental results show that our approach outperforms the state-of-the- art in terms of the recall rate and is more robust and scalable compared to previous approaches. The critique agent holistically reevaluates both agent and tool recommendations against the overall plan. We show that the inclusion of the critique agent further enhances the recall score, proving that the comprehensive review and revision of task-based agent selection is an essential step in building end-to-end multi-agent systems.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of manually composing multi-agent systems, this paper proposes an automated framework that replaces manual planning, agent selection, and workflow construction with a pipeline of software modules including an LLM planner, natural language tasks, a dynamic call graph, an orchestrator, and an agent recommender. The recommender employs a two-stage IR system with a fast retriever and LLM-based re-ranker to match agents to tasks from local and global registries. Experiments evaluating embedders, re-rankers, agent description enrichment, and a critique agent show the system outperforms prior methods in recall rate and demonstrates greater robustness and scalability. The critique agent, which reevaluates agent and tool choices against the overall plan, further boosts recall, confirming that holistic review of agent selection is critical for effective end-to-end multi-agent execution.
该论文旨在减少构建多智能体系统所需的人工操作，提出了一种自动化框架，从用户意图出发自动组合智能体工作流。方法包括基于LLM的规划器、自然语言描述的任务、动态调用图、调度器和智能体推荐器（采用两阶段信息检索系统，含快速检索器与LLM重排序器）。实验表明，该方法在召回率和可扩展性上优于现有方法，且加入批判性智能体后，通过全局评估智能体与工具选择，进一步提升了召回率，证明任务导向的智能体选择需全面审查与修正。
Flow Sampling: Learning to Sample from Unnormalized Densities via Denoising Conditional Processes
Authors: Aaron Havens, Brian Karrer, Neta Shaul
Venue: ICML 2026 spotlight
First: 2026-05-05T17:07:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:07:37+00:00
Comments: To appear at ICML 2026 (spotlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sampling from unnormalized densities is analogous to the generative modeling problem, but the target distribution is defined by a known energy function instead of data samples. Because evaluating the energy function is often costly, a primary challenge is to learn an efficient sampler. We introduce Flow Sampling, a framework built on diffusion models and flow matching for the data-free setting. Our training objective is conditioned on a noise sample and regresses onto a denoising diffusion drift constructed from the energy function. In contrast, diffusion models' objective is conditioned on a data sample and regresses onto a noising diffusion drift. We utilize the interpolant process to minimize the number of energy function evaluations during training, resulting in an efficient and scalable method for sampling unnormalized densities. Furthermore, our formulation naturally extends to Riemannian manifolds, enabling diffusion-based sampling in geometries beyond Euclidean space. We derive a closed-form formula for the conditional drift on constant curvature manifolds, including hyperspheres and hyperbolic spaces. We evaluate Flow Sampling on synthetic energy benchmarks, small peptides, large-scale amortized molecular conformer generation, and distributions supported on the sphere, demonstrating strong empirical performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：流采样：通过去噪条件过程从非归一化密度中学习采样
从非归一化密度中采样类似于生成建模问题，但目标分布由已知的能量函数定义，而非数据样本。由于评估能量函数通常成本高昂，主要挑战是学习高效的采样器。我们提出了流采样框架，基于扩散模型和流匹配，适用于无数据场景。我们的训练目标以噪声样本为条件，回归至由能量函数构建的去噪扩散漂移。相比之下，扩散模型的目标以数据样本为条件，回归至噪声扩散漂移。我们利用插值过程减少训练期间能量函数的评估次数，从而实现高效且可扩展的非归一化密度采样方法。此外，我们的公式自然扩展至黎曼流形，支持在欧几里得空间之外的几何结构中进行扩散式采样。我们推导出常曲率流形（包括超球面和双曲空间）上条件漂移的闭式公式。我们在合成能量基准、小肽、大规模分子构象生成及球面上分布的数据上评估流采样，展现出强大的实证性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sampling from unnormalized densities defined by energy functions, the paper proposes Flow Sampling, a diffusion-based framework that learns to sample without data by conditioning on noise and regressing to a drift derived from the energy function. Unlike standard diffusion models that use data samples, Flow Sampling leverages flow matching and interpolant processes to minimize energy evaluations during training, enabling scalable sampling. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks, molecular conformers, and spherical distributions show strong performance, and the method naturally extends to Riemannian manifolds, with closed-form drifts derived for constant curvature spaces like hyperspheres and hyperbolic spaces.
为高效采样由能量函数定义的非归一化密度，本文提出Flow Sampling框架，结合扩散模型与流匹配，构建无数据训练方法。模型以噪声样本为条件，回归由能量函数构造的去噪扩散漂移，区别于传统扩散模型以数据样本为条件。通过插值过程减少训练中能量函数评估次数，实现可扩展采样。在合成基准、分子构象生成及球面分布等实验中表现优异，并推导出常曲率流形（如超球面、双曲空间）的闭式条件漂移公式。
Implementing True MPI Sessions and Evaluating MPI Initialization Scalability
Authors: Hui Zhou, Kenneth Raffenetti, Yanfei Guo, Michael Wilkins, Rajeev Thakur
First: 2026-05-05T17:06:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T17:06:10+00:00
Comments: European MPI Users' Group Meeting. 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sessions is one of the major features introduced in the MPI-4 standard. It offers an alternative to the traditional world communicator model by allowing applications to construct communicators from process sets, thereby eliminating the dependency on MPI_COMM_WORLD. The Sessions model was proposed as a more scalable solution for exascale systems, where MPI_COMM_WORLD was viewed as a potential scalability bottleneck. However, supporting Sessions is a significant challenge for established codebases like MPICH due to the deep integration of the world model in traditional MPI implementations. Although MPICH added support for the MPI-4 standard upon its release, it still internally relied on a global world communicator. This approach enabled applications written using the Sessions model to function, but it did not fulfill the full design intent of Sessions, which meant to decouple MPI from MPI_COMM_WORLD. We describe MPICH effort to support true MPI Sessions, including a major internal refactoring. We describe the architectural changes required to support true Sessions and evaluate the resulting implementation scalability. Our results demonstrate that true Sessions can offer significant scalability benefits by adopting explicit hierarchical designs.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to overcome scalability bottlenecks in exascale systems, this paper addresses the challenge of implementing true MPI Sessions as defined in the MPI-4 standard, which aims to decouple applications from the traditional MPI_COMM_WORLD model. The authors undertook a major internal refactoring of MPICH to remove its reliance on the global world communicator, enabling full support for Sessions by constructing communicators from explicit process sets. Experimental evaluations show that this true Sessions implementation significantly improves scalability by adopting explicit hierarchical designs, validating the architectural benefits of moving away from the legacy world model.
为应对exascale计算中的可扩展性需求，本文针对MPICH对MPI_COMM_WORLD的依赖问题，对MPI-4标准中Sessions功能的实现进行了重构。作者通过大规模内部重构，引入显式层次化设计，彻底摆脱对全局世界通信器的依赖，实现真正意义上的Sessions支持。实验结果表明，该实现显著提升了系统可扩展性，验证了真正Sessions架构的优势。
Power-Softmax: Towards Secure LLM Inference over Encrypted Data
Authors: Itamar Zimerman, Allon Adir, Ehud Aharoni, Matan Avitan, Moran Baruch, Nir Drucker, Jenny Lerner, Ramy Masalha, Reut Meiri, Omri Soceanu
Venue: Proceedings of the \@conferenceordinal\,International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics (AISTATS) 2026, PMLR: Volume 300
First: 2024-10-12T09:32:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:59:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern cryptographic methods for implementing privacy-preserving LLMs such as \gls{HE} require the LLMs to have a polynomial form. Forming such a representation is challenging because transformers include non-polynomial components, such as \Softmax and layer normalization. Previous approaches have either directly approximated pre-trained models with large-degree polynomials, which are less efficient over HE, or replaced non-polynomial components with easier-to-approximate primitives before training, e.g., \Softmax with pointwise attention. The latter approach might introduce scalability challenges. We present a new HE-friendly variant of self-attention that offers a stable form for training and is easy to approximate with polynomials for secure inference. Our work introduces the first polynomial LLMs over a billion parameters, exceeding the size of previous models by more than tenfold. The resulting models demonstrate reasoning and in-context learning (ICL) capabilities comparable to standard transformers of the same size, representing a breakthrough in the field. Finally, we provide a detailed latency breakdown for each computation over encrypted data, paving the way for further optimization, and explore the differences in inductive bias between models relying on our HE-friendly variant and standard transformers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Power-Softmax：面向加密数据的LLM安全推理
实现隐私保护LLM的现代密码学方法（如HE）要求LLM具有多项式形式。构建此类表示具有挑战性，因为Transformer包含非多项式组件，如Softmax和层归一化。先前方法要么直接用高次多项式近似预训练模型，导致HE效率较低；要么在训练前用更易近似的原语替换非多项式组件，如用点积注意力近似Softmax。后者可能引入可扩展性挑战。我们提出一种新的HE友好型自注意力变体，该变体在训练中表现稳定，且易于用多项式近似以实现安全推理。我们的工作首次实现了十亿参数规模的多项式LLM，规模超过此前模型十倍以上。所得模型在推理和上下文学习能力上与同等规模的标准Transformer相当，标志着该领域的重要突破。最后，我们对加密数据上每项计算的延迟进行了详细分解，为后续优化提供基础，并探讨了依赖我们HE友好型变体与标准Transformer模型之间的归纳偏置差异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of enabling secure large language model inference over encrypted data using homomorphic encryption, this work addresses the non-polynomial nature of transformer components like Softmax. It introduces Power-Softmax, a novel HE-friendly self-attention variant that maintains training stability while being easily approximated by polynomials for secure inference. Experimental results show that the resulting billion-parameter polynomial LLMs achieve reasoning and in-context learning performance comparable to standard transformers of similar size, marking a significant advancement. The study also provides detailed latency analysis per encrypted computation, identifying optimization opportunities and contrasting inductive bias between the proposed model and conventional architectures.
为实现加密数据上的安全大语言模型推理，本文针对Transformer中如Softmax等非多项式组件的挑战，提出Power-Softmax，一种支持同态加密的自注意力变体，既保证训练稳定性，又便于多项式近似。实验表明，该方法构建的十亿参数级多项式LLM在推理与上下文学习能力上可媲美同等规模的标准Transformer，实现重大突破。研究还对加密计算各环节延迟进行详细分析，揭示优化空间，并指出该架构与传统Transformer在归纳偏置上的差异。
Logical Consistency as a Bridge: Improving LLM Hallucination Detection via Label Constraint Modeling between Responses and Self-Judgments
Authors: Hao Mi, Qiang Sheng, Shaofei Wang, Beizhe Hu, Yifan Sun, Zhengjia Wang, Hengqi Zeng, Yang Li, Danding Wang, Juan Cao
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-05T16:53:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:53:20+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to factual hallucinations, risking their reliability in real-world applications. Existing hallucination detectors mainly extract micro-level intrinsic patterns for uncertainty quantification or elicit macro-level self-judgments through verbalized prompts. However, these methods address only a single facet of the hallucination, focusing either on implicit neural uncertainty or explicit symbolic reasoning, thereby treating these inherently coupled behaviors in isolation and failing to exploit their interdependence for a holistic view. In this paper, we propose LaaB (Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge), a framework that bridges neural features and symbolic judgments for hallucination detection. LaaB introduces a "meta-judgment" process to map symbolic labels back into the feature space. By leveraging the inherent logical bridge where response and meta-judgment labels are either the same or opposite based on the self-judgment's semantics, LaaB aligns and integrates dual-view signals via mutual learning and enhances the hallucination detection. Extensive experiments on 4 public datasets, across 4 LLMs, against 8 baselines demonstrate the superiority of LaaB.
中文标题/摘要
标题：逻辑一致性作为桥梁：通过响应与自我判断标签约束建模提升大语言模型幻觉检测
大语言模型（LLMs）易产生事实性幻觉，影响其在现实应用中的可靠性。现有幻觉检测方法主要提取微观层面的内在模式进行不确定性量化，或通过口语化提示激发宏观层面的自我判断。然而，这些方法仅关注幻觉的一个方面，要么聚焦隐式神经不确定性，要么聚焦显式符号推理，从而将本应耦合的行为割裂处理，未能利用其相互依赖关系实现整体视角。本文提出LaaB（逻辑一致性作为桥梁）框架，通过连接神经特征与符号判断以提升幻觉检测。LaaB引入“元判断”过程，将符号标签映射回特征空间。通过利用响应与元判断标签基于自我判断语义的逻辑关联（同或异），LaaB通过互学习对齐并融合双视角信号，增强幻觉检测能力。在4个公开数据集、4个LLM模型、8个基线方法上的大量实验验证了LaaB的优越性。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing hallucination detection methods that treat neural uncertainty and symbolic self-judgments in isolation, the paper proposes LaaB, a framework that bridges these two perspectives by introducing a meta-judgment process to map symbolic labels into the feature space. LaaB leverages the logical consistency between a model’s response and its self-judgment—where labels are either aligned or opposed based on semantics—to align dual-view signals through mutual learning, thereby enhancing detection accuracy. Experiments across four LLMs and four datasets against eight baselines show LaaB consistently outperforms existing methods in hallucination detection.
为解决现有检测方法孤立处理神经不确定性与符号自我判断的问题，本文提出LaaB框架，通过引入元判断过程将符号标签映射回特征空间，利用响应与自我判断语义间逻辑一致性（标签同向或反向）实现双视角信号对齐与互学习，从而提升检测效果。在四款大模型与四个数据集上对比八种基线方法，实验表明LaaB在幻觉检测中表现最优。
Feature-Augmented Transformers for Robust AI-Text Detection Across Domains and Generators
Authors: Mohamed Mady, Johannes Reschke, Björn Schuller
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-05T16:52:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:52:26+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables. Submitted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI-generated text is nowadays produced at scale across domains and heterogeneous generation pipelines, making robustness to distribution shift a central requirement for supervised binary detectors. We train transformer-based detectors on HC3 PLUS and calibrate a single decision threshold by maximising balanced accuracy on held-out validation; this threshold is then kept fixed for all downstream test distributions, revealing domain- and generator-dependent error asymmetries under shift. We evaluate in-domain on HC3 PLUS, under cross-dataset transfer to the multi-domain, multi-generator M4 benchmark, and on the external AI-Text-Detection-Pile. Although base models achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance (up to 99.5% balanced accuracy), performance under shift is brittle and strongly model-dependent. Feature augmentation via attention-based linguistic feature fusion improves transfer, with our best model (DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn) achieving 85.9% balanced accuracy on M4. Multi-seed experiments confirm high stability. Under the same fixed-threshold protocol, our model outperforms strong zero-shot baselines by up to +7.22 points. Category-level ablations further show that readability and vocabulary features contribute most to robustness under shift. Overall, these results demonstrate that feature augmentation and a modern DeBERTa backbone significantly outperform earlier BERT/RoBERTa models, while the fixed-threshold protocol provides a more realistic and informative assessment of practical detector robustness.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust AI-text detection across diverse domains and generation methods, the study trains transformer-based detectors on HC3 PLUS and calibrates a single decision threshold via balanced accuracy maximization on held-out validation, then fixes it for all downstream tests. This reveals error asymmetries under distribution shift. Experiments show base models achieve near-perfect in-domain accuracy (up to 99.5%) but perform poorly under shift, with performance highly model-dependent. Feature augmentation through attention-based linguistic feature fusion improves transferability, with the best model (DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn) achieving 85.9% balanced accuracy on the M4 benchmark. Multi-seed experiments confirm stability, and the model outperforms zero-shot baselines by up to 7.22 points under the same fixed-threshold protocol. Ablations indicate readability and vocabulary features are most critical for robustness under shift.
为应对跨领域和生成器的AI文本检测需求，作者在HC3 PLUS上训练基于Transformer的检测器，并通过验证集最大化平衡准确率校准单一决策阈值，该阈值在下游测试中保持固定，以揭示分布偏移下的误差不对称性。其方法通过注意力机制融合语言特征，结合DeBERTa-v3-base架构，最佳模型（DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn）在M4基准上达到85.9%的平衡准确率，相较强零样本基线高7.22个百分点。消融实验表明可读性与词汇特征对分布偏移下的鲁棒性贡献最大，多种子实验验证了高稳定性，证明特征增强与现代架构显著优于早期BERT/RoBERTa模型。
Label-Efficient School Detection from Aerial Imagery via Weakly Supervised Pretraining and Fine-Tuning
Authors: Zakarya Elmimouni, Fares Fourati, Mohamed-Slim Alouini
First: 2026-05-05T16:51:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:51:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate school detection is essential for supporting education initiatives, including infrastructure planning and expanding internet connectivity to underserved areas. However, many regions around the world face challenges due to outdated, incomplete, or unavailable official records. Manual mapping efforts, while valuable, are labor-intensive and lack scalability across large geographic areas. To address this, we propose a weakly supervised framework for school detection from aerial imagery that minimizes the need for human annotations while supporting global mapping efforts. Our method is specifically designed for low-data regimes, where manual annotations are extremely scarce. We introduce an automatic labeling pipeline that leverages sparse location points and semantic segmentation to generate infrastructure masks from which we generate bounding boxes. Using these automatically labeled images, we train our detectors on a first training stage to learn a representation of what schools look like, then using a small set of manually labeled images, we fine-tune the previously trained models on this clean dataset. This two stage training pipeline enables large-scale and strong detection in low-data setting of school infrastructure with minimal supervision. Our results demonstrate strong object detection performance, particularly in the low-data regime, where the models achieve promising results using only 50 manually labeled images, significantly reducing the need for costly annotations. This framework supports education and connectivity initiatives worldwide by providing an efficient and extensible approach to mapping schools from space. All models, training code and auto-labeled data will be publicly released to foster future research and real-world impact.
Summary / 总结
To address the scarcity of annotated data for school detection in aerial imagery, especially in regions lacking official records, this study proposes a weakly supervised framework that combines automatic labeling via sparse location points and semantic segmentation with two-stage training: first pretraining on auto-labeled data to learn school-like features, then fine-tuning on a small set of manually labeled images. Experimental results show the method achieves strong detection performance even with only 50 manual annotations, significantly reducing reliance on costly human labeling while enabling scalable global school mapping.
为应对全球范围内学校标注数据稀缺的问题，该研究提出一种弱监督两阶段框架，结合稀疏点位与语义分割自动生成标注框，再以少量人工标注数据进行微调。实验表明，该方法仅需50个手动标注样本即可实现优异的检测效果，大幅降低标注成本，同时支持大规模全球学校空间测绘。
Robust Language Identification for Romansh Varieties
Authors: Charlotte Model, Sina Ahmadi, Jannis Vamvas
First: 2026-03-16T22:42:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:50:27+00:00
Comments: To appear at the 11th edition of SwissText
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Romansh language has several regional varieties, called idioms, which sometimes have limited mutual intelligibility. Despite this linguistic diversity, there has been a lack of documented efforts to build a language identification (LID) system that can distinguish between these idioms. Since Romansh LID should also be able to recognize Rumantsch Grischun, a supra-regional variety that combines elements of several idioms, this makes for a novel and interesting classification problem. In this paper, we present a LID system for Romansh idioms based on an SVM approach. We evaluate our model on a newly curated benchmark across two domains and find that it reaches an average in-domain accuracy of 97%, enabling applications such as idiom-aware spell checking or machine translation. Our classifier is publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：罗曼什语变体的鲁棒语言识别
罗曼什语有几种区域变体，称为方言，有时相互理解有限。尽管存在这种语言多样性，但尚未有文献记录尝试构建能够区分这些方言的语言识别（LID）系统。由于罗曼什语LID还应能识别融合多个方言元素的超区域变体——如Rumantsch Grischun，这构成了一个新颖且有趣的分类问题。本文提出了一种基于SVM方法的罗曼什语方言LID系统。我们在两个新编纂的基准数据集上评估模型，发现其在域内平均准确率达97%，可应用于方言感知拼写检查或机器翻译等场景。我们的分类器已公开提供。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately distinguish between the mutually unintelligible regional varieties of the Romansh language, including the supra-regional Rumantsch Grischun, this study develops a language identification system using a support vector machine (SVM) approach. Evaluated on a newly created benchmark across two domains, the model achieves an average in-domain accuracy of 97%, demonstrating strong performance for practical applications such as idiom-aware spell checking or machine translation. The classifier is made publicly available for further use.
为应对罗曼什语多种区域变体（idioms）缺乏语言识别系统的问题，本研究提出一种基于SVM的识别模型，可区分包括超区域变体Rumantsch Grischun在内的各方言。在新构建的双领域基准数据集上测试，模型平均准确率达97%，具备拼写检查或机器翻译等实际应用价值，且模型已公开发布。
Kinematic Kitbashing
Authors: Minghao Guo, Victor Zordan, Sheldon Andrews, Wojciech Matusik, Maneesh Agrawala, Hsueh-Ti Derek Liu
Venue: SIGGRAPH
First: 2025-10-14T23:54:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:50:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Kinematic Kitbashing, an optimization framework that synthesizes articulated 3D objects by assembling reusable parts conditioned on an abstract kinematic graph. Given the graph and a library of articulated parts, our method optimizes per-part similarity transformations that place, orient, and scale each component into a coherent articulated object; optional graph edits further enable novel assemblies beyond the prescribed connectivity. Central to our method is an exemplar-based analogy for part placement: each reused component is paired with a single source asset that exemplifies how it attaches to its parent. We capture this attachment context using vector distance fields and measure consistency by integrating the matching error over the joint's full motion range. This yields a kinematics-aware attachment energy that favors placements that preserve the exemplar's local attachment neighborhood throughout articulation. To incorporate task-level functionality, we use this attachment energy as a prior in an annealed Langevin sampling framework, enabling gradient-free optimization of black-box functionality objectives. We demonstrate the versatility of kinematic kitbashing across diverse applications, including instantiating kinematic graphs from user-selected or automatically retrieved parts, synthesizing assemblies with user-defined functionality, and re-targeting articulations via graph edits.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently synthesize articulated 3D objects from reusable parts, Kinematic Kitbashing introduces an optimization framework that assembles components guided by an abstract kinematic graph. The method optimizes similarity transformations per part to align, orient, and scale components while preserving local attachment consistency, using exemplar-based analogy and vector distance fields to measure motion-range fidelity. Experimental results show the framework successfully generates coherent articulated assemblies from part libraries, supports novel graph edits for creative reconfiguration, and enables gradient-free optimization of task-specific functionality via Langevin sampling, demonstrating broad applicability across synthesis and re-targeting tasks.
为解决从可复用部件合成连贯运动3D物体的需求，Kinematic Kitbashing提出一种基于抽象运动图的优化框架，通过优化每个部件的相似变换实现位置、方向与缩放。其核心是基于示例的装配上下文：每个部件与一个源资产配对，通过向量距离场捕捉其连接关系，并在关节全运动范围内积分匹配误差，形成运动感知的装配能量。该能量作为先验用于退火朗之万采样框架，实现无需梯度的黑盒功能优化。实验表明该方法可从用户选择或自动检索的部件生成新装配，合成满足用户功能需求的结构，并通过图编辑实现运动重定向，适用于多种应用场景。
4RC: 4D Reconstruction via Conditional Querying Anytime and Anywhere
Authors: Yihang Luo, Shangchen Zhou, Yushi Lan, Xingang Pan, Chen Change Loy
First: 2026-02-10T18:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:49:24+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://yihangluo.com/projects/4RC/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present 4RC, a unified feed-forward framework for 4D reconstruction from monocular videos. Unlike existing approaches that typically decouple motion from geometry or produce limited 4D attributes such as sparse trajectories or two-view scene flow, 4RC learns a holistic 4D representation that jointly captures dense scene geometry and motion dynamics. At its core, 4RC introduces a novel encode-once, query-anywhere and anytime paradigm: a transformer backbone encodes the entire video into a compact spatio-temporal latent space, from which a conditional decoder can efficiently query 3D geometry and motion for any query frame at any target timestamp. To facilitate learning, we represent per-view 4D attributes in a minimally factorized form by decomposing them into base geometry and time-dependent relative motion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that 4RC outperforms prior and concurrent methods across a wide range of 4D reconstruction tasks.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for holistic 4D reconstruction from monocular videos without decoupling motion and geometry, 4RC introduces a feed-forward framework that jointly learns dense scene geometry and motion dynamics. It employs a transformer backbone to encode the entire video into a compact spatio-temporal latent space, enabling a conditional decoder to query 3D geometry and motion at any frame and timestamp. The method factorizes 4D attributes into base geometry and time-dependent motion to simplify learning. Experimental results show 4RC consistently outperforms existing methods across multiple 4D reconstruction benchmarks.
为解决从单目视频中重建完整4D场景时运动与几何常被分离的问题，4RC提出了一种统一的前馈框架，联合建模密集场景几何与运动动态。其核心是用Transformer编码整段视频为紧凑的时空潜在空间，支持条件解码器在任意帧和时间点高效查询3D几何与运动。通过将4D属性分解为基础几何与时间相关相对运动，该方法提升了学习效率。实验表明，4RC在多个4D重建任务上显著优于现有方法。
Pretrained Model Representations as Acquisition Signals for Active Learning of MLIPs
Authors: Eszter Varga-Umbrich, Shikha Surana, Paul Duckworth, Jules Tilly, Olivier Peltre, Zachary Weller-Davies
First: 2026-05-05T16:48:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:48:23+00:00
Comments: 8 main pages, 28 total pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for reactive chemistry is often bottlenecked by the high cost of quantum chemical labels and the scarcity of transition state configurations in candidate pools. Active learning (AL) can mitigate these costs, but its effectiveness hinges on the acquisition rule. We investigate whether the latent space of a pretrained MLIP already contains the information necessary for effective acquisition, eliminating the need for auxiliary uncertainty heads, Bayesian training and fine-tuning, or committee ensembles. We introduce two acquisition signals derived directly from a pretrained MACE potential: a finite-width neural tangent kernel (NTK) and an activation kernel built from hidden latent space features. On reactive-chemistry benchmarks, both kernels consistently outperform fixed-descriptor baselines, committee disagreement, and random acquisition, reducing the data required to reach performance targets by an average of 38% for energy error and 28% for force error. We further show that the pretrained model induces similarity spaces that preserve chemically meaningful structure and provide more reliable residual uncertainty estimates than randomly initialised or fixed-descriptor-based kernels. Our results suggest that pretraining aligns latent-space geometry with model error, yielding a practical and sufficient acquisition signal for reactive MLIP fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预训练模型表示作为用于反应性化学中机器学习原子势主动学习的获取信号
训练用于反应性化学的机器学习原子势（MLIPs）常因量子化学标签成本高昂及候选池中过渡态构型稀缺而受限。主动学习（AL）可缓解这些成本，但其效果取决于获取规则。我们研究预训练MLIP的潜在空间是否已包含有效获取所需的信息，从而无需辅助不确定性头、贝叶斯训练和微调，或委员会集成。我们引入两种直接从预训练MACE势能中导出的获取信号：有限宽度神经张量核（NTK）和基于隐藏潜在空间特征构建的激活核。在反应性化学基准测试中，两种核均一致优于固定描述符基线、委员会分歧和随机获取，平均减少38%的能量误差所需数据量和28%的力误差所需数据量。我们进一步表明，预训练模型诱导的相似性空间保留了化学上有意义的结构，并比随机初始化或基于固定描述符的核提供更可靠的残差不确定性估计。我们的结果表明，预训练使潜在空间几何与模型误差对齐，为反应性MLIP微调提供了实用且充分的获取信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and scarcity of quantum chemical data for training reactive MLIPs, this work explores whether pretrained MLIPs inherently contain sufficient acquisition signals for active learning, avoiding the need for additional uncertainty modeling. Two acquisition signals — a finite-width neural tangent kernel and an activation kernel from latent space features — are derived directly from a pretrained MACE model. Experiments on reactive chemistry benchmarks show both kernels outperform fixed-descriptor baselines, committee disagreement, and random sampling, reducing required data by 38% for energy error and 28% for force error. The pretrained model’s latent space also preserves chemically meaningful structure and yields more reliable uncertainty estimates than random or fixed-descriptor kernels, indicating that pretraining aligns latent geometry with model error for effective active learning.
为应对反应化学中量子化学标签成本高和过渡态稀缺的问题，该研究探讨预训练MLIP是否已内含足够有效的主动学习信号，从而无需额外的贝叶斯不确定性或委员会集成方法。研究从预训练的MACE模型中直接提取两种获取信号——有限宽度神经张量核和基于隐藏潜空间特征的激活核。在反应化学基准测试中，这两种核均优于固定描述符基线、委员会分歧和随机采样，平均减少38%的能量误差数据需求和28%的力误差数据需求。预训练模型的潜空间还保留了化学上有意义的结构，并比随机初始化或固定描述符核提供更可靠的残差不确定性估计，表明预训练使潜空间几何与模型误差对齐，为反应MLIP微调提供了实用且充分的获取信号。
Unsupervised Monocular Road Segmentation for Autonomous Driving via Scene Geometry
Authors: Sara Hatami Rostami, Behrooz Nasihatkon
First: 2025-10-19T10:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:47:23+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a fully unsupervised approach for binary road segmentation (road vs. non-road), eliminating the reliance on costly manually labeled datasets. The method leverages scene geometry and temporal cues to distinguish road from non-road regions. Weak labels are first generated from geometric priors, marking pixels above the horizon as non-road and a predefined quadrilateral in front of the vehicle as road. In a refinement stage, temporal consistency is enforced by tracking local feature points across frames and penalizing inconsistent label assignments using mutual information maximization. This enhances both precision and temporal stability. On the Cityscapes dataset, the model achieves an Intersection-over-Union (IoU) of 0.86, outperforming the competing unsupervised methods. These findings demonstrate the potential of combining geometric constraints and temporal consistency for scalable unsupervised road segmentation in autonomous driving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过场景几何实现无监督单目道路分割用于自动驾驶
本文提出了一种完全无监督的二值道路分割方法（道路与非道路），消除了对昂贵人工标注数据集的依赖。该方法利用场景几何和时序线索区分道路与非道路区域。首先，基于几何先验生成弱标签，将地平线以上的像素标记为非道路，将车辆前方预定义的四边形区域标记为道路。在精炼阶段，通过跨帧跟踪局部特征点并利用互信息最大化惩罚不一致的标签分配，强制时序一致性，从而提升精度和时序稳定性。在Cityscapes数据集上，模型达到0.86的交并比（IoU），优于现有无监督方法。这些结果表明，结合几何约束与时序一致性可实现自动驾驶中可扩展的无监督道路分割。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of labeled data for autonomous driving, this paper proposes an unsupervised monocular road segmentation method that relies on scene geometry and temporal cues. It first generates weak labels using geometric priors—marking pixels above the horizon as non-road and a front-facing quadrilateral as road—and then refines them by enforcing temporal consistency through local feature tracking and mutual information maximization. Experiments on Cityscapes show the method achieves an IoU of 0.86, outperforming other unsupervised approaches, demonstrating the effectiveness of geometric and temporal constraints for scalable road segmentation.
为降低自动驾驶中道路分割对人工标注数据的依赖，本文提出一种基于场景几何与时间线索的无监督单目道路分割方法。首先利用几何先验生成弱标签，将地平线以上像素标记为非道路，前方预设四边形区域标记为道路；随后通过局部特征点跨帧跟踪与互信息最大化约束，提升标签一致性。在Cityscapes数据集上，该方法达到0.86的IoU，优于现有无监督方法，验证了几何约束与时间一致性结合在可扩展无监督道路分割中的有效性。
Optimal Rates for Pure $\varepsilon$-Differentially Private Stochastic Convex Optimization with Heavy Tails
Authors: Andrew Lowy
First: 2026-04-07T21:54:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:42:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study stochastic convex optimization (SCO) with heavy-tailed gradients under pure $\varepsilon$-differential privacy (DP). Instead of assuming a bound on the worst-case Lipschitz parameter of the loss, we assume only a bounded $k$-th moment. This assumption allows for unbounded, heavy-tailed stochastic gradient distributions, and can yield sharper excess risk bounds. Prior work characterized the minimax optimal rate for $ρ$-zero-concentrated DP SCO up to logarithmic factors in this setting, but the pure $\varepsilon$-DP case has remained open. We characterize the minimax optimal excess-risk rate for pure $\varepsilon$-DP heavy-tailed SCO up to logarithmic factors. Our algorithm achieves this rate in polynomial time with high probability. Moreover, it runs in deterministic polynomial time when the worst-case Lipschitz parameter is polynomially bounded. For important structured problem classes -- including hinge/ReLU-type and absolute-value losses on Euclidean balls, ellipsoids, and polytopes -- we achieve deterministic polynomial time even when the worst-case Lipschitz parameter is infinite. Our approach is based on a novel framework for privately optimizing Lipschitz extensions of the empirical loss. We complement our upper bound with a nearly matching high-probability lower bound.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有重尾分布的纯ε差分隐私随机凸优化的最优速率
我们研究在纯ε差分隐私（DP）下，具有重尾梯度的随机凸优化（SCO）。不同于假设损失函数最坏情况Lipschitz参数有界，我们仅假设k阶矩有界。这一假设允许随机梯度分布无界且具有重尾，从而可获得更尖锐的超额风险界。此前工作已刻画了ρ-零集中DP下该设定的最小最大最优速率（至对数因子），但纯ε-DP情形仍属未解。我们刻画了纯ε-DP重尾SCO的最小最大最优超额风险速率（至对数因子）。我们的算法以高概率在多项式时间内实现该速率；当最坏情况Lipschitz参数多项式有界时，算法可在确定性多项式时间内运行。对于重要结构化问题类——包括欧氏球、椭球和多面体上的hinge/ReLU型及绝对值损失——即使最坏情况Lipschitz参数为无穷，我们仍可实现确定性多项式时间。我们的方法基于一种新颖框架，用于私有优化经验损失的Lipschitz扩展。我们以几乎匹配的高概率下界补充了上述上界。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses stochastic convex optimization under pure ε-differential privacy when gradients have heavy tails, relaxing the standard assumption of bounded worst-case Lipschitz constants in favor of bounded k-th moments. The authors develop a novel algorithm based on privately optimizing Lipschitz extensions of empirical loss, achieving minimax optimal excess-risk rates up to logarithmic factors. Their method runs in polynomial time with high probability and, for structured problems like hinge or absolute-value losses on convex domains, even in deterministic polynomial time even when the Lipschitz constant is infinite. They also provide a nearly matching lower bound, confirming the optimality of their approach.
为在重尾梯度分布下实现差分隐私保护的优化，本文研究纯ε差分隐私下的随机凸优化问题，将传统Lipschitz假设放宽为k阶矩有界，从而允许梯度分布无界且具有重尾特性。作者提出一种基于经验损失Lipschitz扩展的新型隐私优化框架，设计出能在多项式时间内以高概率达到最优的过量风险率（至对数因子），且在最坏情况Lipschitz常数为无穷时仍能以确定性多项式时间运行，适用于铰链/ReLU型及绝对值损失在欧氏球、椭球和多面体上的结构化问题。实验结果辅以几乎匹配的下界，验证了该方法的最优性。
Physically Guided Visual Mass Estimation from a Single RGB Image
Authors: Sungjae Lee, Junhan Jeong, Yeonjoo Hong, Kwang In Kim
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2026-01-28T06:53:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:41:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IJCAI 2026 (Main Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating object mass from visual input is challenging because mass depends jointly on geometric volume and material-dependent density, neither of which is directly observable from RGB appearance. Consequently, mass prediction from pixels is ill-posed and therefore benefits from physically meaningful representations to constrain the space of plausible solutions. We propose a physically structured framework for single-image mass estimation that addresses this ambiguity by aligning visual cues with the physical factors governing mass. From a single RGB image, we recover object-centric three-dimensional geometry via monocular depth estimation to inform volume and extract coarse material semantics using a vision-language model to guide density-related reasoning. These geometry, semantic, and appearance representations are fused through an instance-adaptive gating mechanism, and two physically guided latent factors (volume- and density-related) are predicted through separate regression heads under mass-only supervision. Experiments on image2mass and ABO-500 show that the proposed method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the ill-posed nature of mass estimation from RGB images due to the lack of direct access to geometric volume and material density, this work proposes a physically guided framework that aligns visual cues with physical mass determinants. It leverages monocular depth estimation to recover 3D geometry for volume inference and a vision-language model to extract coarse material semantics for density reasoning, then fuses these representations via an instance-adaptive gating mechanism. Two physically guided latent factors—volume and density—are predicted through separate regression heads under mass-only supervision, achieving consistent performance improvements over state-of-the-art methods on image2mass and ABO-500 datasets.
由于RGB图像无法直接观测物体的体积和密度，导致质量估计问题高度欠定，本文提出一种物理引导框架，将视觉线索与质量的物理决定因素对齐。该方法通过单目深度估计恢复物体三维几何以推断体积，并借助视觉-语言模型提取粗粒度材质语义以指导密度推理。这些几何、语义与外观表征通过实例自适应门控机制融合，经两个独立回归头分别预测体积与密度相关潜变量，仅以质量标签监督。在image2mass与ABO-500数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著优于现有方法。
Inconsistent Databases and Argumentation Frameworks with Collective Attacks
Authors: Yasir Mahmood, Jonni Virtema, Timon Barlag, Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo
First: 2026-05-05T16:38:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:38:31+00:00
Comments: This is a pre-print of the paper accepted at the Knowledge Engineering Review journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The connection between subset-maximal repairs for inconsistent databases involving various integrity constraints and acceptable sets of arguments within argumentation frameworks has recently drawn growing interest. In this paper, we contribute to this domain by establishing a new connection when integrity constraints (ICs) include denial constraints and local-as-view tuple-generating dependencies. It turns out that SET-based Argumentation Frameworks (SETAFs), an extension of Dung's argumentation frameworks (AFs) allowing collective attacks, are needed. It is known that subset-maximal repairs under denial constraints correspond to the naive extensions, which also coincide with the preferred and stable extensions in the resulting SETAFs. Our main findings establish that repairs under the considered fragment of tuple-generating dependencies correspond to the preferred extensions. Moreover, for these dependencies, additional preprocessing allows computing a unique extension that is stable and naive. Allowing both types of constraints breaks this relationship, and even the pre-processing does not help as only preferred semantics captures these repairs. Finally, while it is known that functional dependencies do not require set-based attacks, we prove the same regarding inclusion dependencies. Thus, one can translate inconsistent databases under these restricted classes of ICs to plain AFs with attacks only between arguments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不一致数据库与集体攻击的论证框架
最近，不一致数据库在涉及各种完整性约束下的子集最大修复与论证框架中可接受论证集之间的联系引起了越来越多的关注。本文通过建立一种新联系，为该领域做出贡献，当完整性约束（ICs）包含否定约束和局部作为视图的元组生成依赖时。结果表明，需要基于集合的论证框架（SETAFs），这是Dung论证框架（AFs）的扩展，允许集体攻击。已知在否定约束下，子集最大修复对应于朴素扩展，且在所得SETAFs中也与偏好扩展和稳定扩展一致。我们的主要发现表明，在所考虑的元组生成依赖片段下，修复对应于偏好扩展。此外，对于这些依赖，额外预处理可计算出唯一同时稳定且朴素的扩展。同时允许两种约束类型会破坏这种关系，即使预处理也无法帮助，因为只有偏好语义能捕捉这些修复。最后，虽然已知函数依赖不需要基于集合的攻击，我们证明了包含依赖同样不需要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reconcile inconsistent databases with argumentation frameworks under specific integrity constraints, this paper explores how subset-maximal repairs under denial constraints and local-as-view tuple-generating dependencies map to argumentation semantics. It introduces SETAFs—set-based argumentation frameworks supporting collective attacks—to model these repairs, showing that repairs under denial constraints align with naive, preferred, and stable extensions in SETAFs. For tuple-generating dependencies, repairs correspond to preferred extensions, and with preprocessing, a unique stable and naive extension can be computed. However, when both constraint types are combined, only preferred semantics captures repairs, and preprocessing fails. The paper also confirms that functional and inclusion dependencies do not require set-based attacks, allowing translation to standard argumentation frameworks.
为解决不一致数据库与论证框架的关联问题，本文研究在特定完整性约束（如否定约束和局部作为视图的元组生成依赖）下，子集最大修复如何映射到论证语义。引入了允许集体攻击的SETAF框架，以建模此类修复。研究发现，否定约束下的修复对应SETAF中的朴素、偏好和稳定扩展；在特定元组生成依赖下，修复对应偏好扩展，且经预处理可计算出唯一稳定且朴素的扩展。但当两种约束混合时，该关系失效，仅偏好语义能捕捉修复。此外，本文证明功能依赖和包含依赖也不需要集合攻击，可将其转化为标准论证框架。
Transformers with Selective Access to Early Representations
Authors: Skye Gunasekaran, Téa Wright, Rui-Jie Zhu, Jason Eshraghian
First: 2026-05-05T16:38:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:38:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Several recent Transformer architectures expose later layers to representations computed in the earliest layers, motivated by the observation that low-level features can become harder to recover as the residual stream is repeatedly transformed through depth. The cheapest among these methods add static value residuals: learned mixing coefficients that expose the first-layer value projection V_1 uniformly across tokens and heads. More expressive dense or dynamic alternatives recover finer-grained access, but at higher memory cost and lower throughput. The usefulness of V_1 is unlikely to be constant across tokens, heads, and contexts; different positions plausibly require different amounts of access to early lexical or semantic information. We therefore treat early-representation reuse as a retrieval problem rather than a connectivity problem, and introduce Selective Access Transformer (SATFormer), which preserves the first-layer value pathway while controlling access with a context-dependent gate. Across models from 130M to 1.3B parameters, SATFormer consistently improves validation loss and zero-shot accuracy over the static value-residual and Transformer baselines. Its strongest gains appear on retrieval-intensive benchmarks, where it improves over static value residuals by approximately 1.5 average points, while maintaining throughput and memory usage close to the baseline Transformer. Gate analyses suggest sparse, depth-dependent, head-specific, and category-sensitive access patterns, supporting the interpretation that SATFormer learns selective reuse of early representations rather than uniform residual copying. Our code is available at https://github.com/SkyeGunasekaran/SATFormer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有选择性访问早期表示的Transformer
几种近期的Transformer架构允许深层层访问最早层计算的表示，其动机是观察到随着残差流在深度中被反复变换，低级特征可能变得更难恢复。其中最经济的方法是添加静态值残差：学习到的混合系数，将第一层值投影V_1均匀地暴露给所有token和注意力头。更表达力的密集或动态替代方案能实现更精细的访问，但代价是更高的内存开销和更低的吞吐量。V_1的有用性在token、头和上下文中可能并非恒定；不同位置可能需要不同程度的早期词汇或语义信息访问。因此，我们将早期表示重用视为检索问题而非连接问题，并引入选择性访问Transformer（SATFormer），它保留第一层值路径，同时通过上下文相关的门控机制控制访问。在130M至1.3B参数的模型中，SATFormer在验证损失和零样本准确率上持续优于静态值残差和Transformer基线。其最显著的提升出现在检索密集型基准测试中，相比静态值残差平均提升约1.5分，同时保持与基线Transformer相近的吞吐量和内存使用。门控分析表明访问模式具有稀疏性、深度依赖性、头特异性和类别敏感性，支持SATFormer学习选择性重用早期表示而非均匀残差复制的解释。我们的代码可在https://github.com/SkyeGunasekaran/SATFormer获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that early Transformer layer representations become harder to recover as depth increases, this work proposes SATFormer, a model that selectively gates access to the first-layer value projections rather than uniformly exposing them as in static value-residual methods. By treating early-representation reuse as a retrieval problem, SATFormer introduces a context-dependent gate to control token- and head-specific access to V_1, enabling more efficient and targeted reuse without increasing memory or throughput costs. Experiments across models from 130M to 1.3B parameters show consistent improvements in validation loss and zero-shot accuracy over both static value-residual and standard Transformer baselines, with the largest gains observed on retrieval-intensive benchmarks—approximately 1.5 average points better than static residuals—while maintaining baseline efficiency. Gate analysis further reveals sparse, depth-dependent, head-specific, and category-sensitive access patterns, confirming selective reuse rather than uniform copying.
鉴于早期Transformer表示在深层传播中难以恢复，本文提出SATFormer模型，通过上下文相关的门控机制选择性访问第一层值投影，而非均匀暴露。与静态值残差方法不同，SATFormer将问题视为检索任务，按token和head动态控制访问。在130M至1.3B参数规模的模型中，SATFormer在验证损失和零样本准确率上持续优于基线，尤其在检索密集型任务中提升约1.5分，同时保持与基线相近的内存与吞吐量。门控分析显示访问模式稀疏、深度依赖、头特定且类别敏感，证实模型学习的是选择性复用而非均匀复制早期表示。
MOSAIC-Bench: Measuring Compositional Vulnerability Induction in Coding Agents
Authors: Jonathan Steinberg, Oren Gal
First: 2026-05-05T16:38:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:38:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coding agents often pass per-prompt safety review yet ship exploitable code when their tasks are decomposed into routine engineering tickets. The challenge is structural: existing safety alignment evaluates overt requests in isolation, leaving models blind to malicious end-states that emerge from sequenced compliance with innocuous-looking requests. We introduce MOSAIC-Bench (Malicious Objectives Sequenced As Innocuous Compliance), a benchmark of 199 three-stage attack chains paired with deterministic exploit oracles on deployed software substrates (10 web-application substrates, 31 CWE classes, 5 programming languages) that treats both exploit ground truth and downstream reviewer protocol as first-class evaluation axes. On this benchmark, nine production coding agents from Anthropic, OpenAI, Google, Moonshot, Zhipu, and Minimax compose innocuous tickets at 53-86% end-to-end ASR with only two refusals across all staged runs. In a matched direct-prompt experiment over four frontier Claude/Codex agents, vulnerable-output rates fall to 0-20.4%: Claude primarily refuses, while Codex primarily hardens rather than emitting the vulnerable implementation - ticket staging silences both defense modes simultaneously. Downstream, code reviewer agents approve 25.8% of these confirmed-vulnerable cumulative diffs as routine PRs, and a full-context implementation protocol closes only 50% of the staged/direct gap, ruling out context fragmentation as the sole explanation. As a deployable but non-adaptive mitigation, reframing the reviewer as an adversarial pentester reduces evasion across the evaluated reviewer subset; pentester framed evasion ranges from 3.0% to 17.6%, and an open-weight Gemma-4-E4B-it reviewer under this framing detects 88.4% of attacks on the dataset with a 4.6% false-positive rate measured on 608 real-world GitHub PRs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MOSAIC-Bench：衡量编码代理的组合脆弱性诱导
编码代理常能通过单次提示的安全审查，但在任务分解为常规工程工单时却会交付可被利用的代码。这一挑战具有结构性：现有安全对齐方法仅评估显式请求，而忽视了由看似无害请求序列引发的恶意终态。我们引入MOSAIC-Bench（恶意目标以无害合规序列化），该基准包含199条三阶段攻击链，搭配部署软件基底（10个Web应用基底、31类CWE、5种编程语言）上的确定性利用预言机，将利用真实情况与下游审查协议均作为核心评估维度。在该基准上，来自Anthropic、OpenAI、Google、Moonshot、Zhipu和Minimax的九个生产级编码代理在53%-86%的端到端ASR下生成无害工单，仅在所有阶段运行中拒绝两次。在四组前沿Claude/Codex代理的直接提示对照实验中，脆弱输出率降至0%-20.4%：Claude主要拒绝，而Codex主要强化而非输出脆弱实现——工单分阶段处理同时抑制了两种防御模式。下游，代码审查代理批准25.8%的已确认脆弱累积差异为常规PR，而完整上下文实现协议仅能弥补50%的分阶段与直接提示间的差距，排除上下文碎片化为唯一解释。作为可部署但非自适应的缓解措施，将审查员重构为对抗性渗透测试者可降低逃逸率；在评估的审查员子集中，渗透测试框架下的逃逸率介于3.0%-17.6%之间，而采用开放权重Gemma-4-E4B-it的审查员在此框架下可检测88.4%的攻击（在608个真实GitHub PR上误报率为4.6%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between per-prompt safety and real-world exploitability in coding agents, MOSAIC-Bench introduces a benchmark of 199 three-stage attack chains tested on deployed software substrates, evaluating both exploit outcomes and reviewer responses as core metrics. Nine production agents successfully composed innocuous-seeming tickets that led to exploitable code in 53-86% of cases, with minimal refusals. In direct-prompt comparisons, vulnerability rates dropped to 0-20.4%, showing that staged prompting suppresses both refusal and hardening defenses. Downstream, reviewer agents approved 25.8% of confirmed-vulnerable diffs as routine PRs, and even full-context protocols only closed half the gap, indicating context fragmentation isn't the main issue. Framing reviewers as adversarial pentesters reduced evasion to 3.0-17.6%, and a Gemma-4-E4B-it reviewer under this framing detected 88.4% of attacks with 4.6% false positives on real GitHub PRs.
为解决编码代理在单次提示安全审查通过后仍可能生成可利用代码的问题，本文提出MOSAIC-Bench基准测试，通过199组三阶段攻击链，结合10个Web应用、31类CWE漏洞和5种编程语言的确定性利用判定器，评估代理在工程任务分解场景下的组合脆弱性。实验表明，九个生产级代理在端到端流程中53-86%的攻击成功，仅两次拒绝；而直接提示实验下漏洞率降至0-20.4%。代码审查代理批准25.8%的已确认漏洞代码为常规PR，即使使用完整上下文协议，仍仅能缩小一半差距。将审查者重构为对抗性渗透测试者后，逃避率降至3.0-17.6%，且Gemma-4-E4B-it模型在608个真实GitHub PR上以4.6%误报率检测出88.4%的攻击。
Shorthand for Thought: Compressing LLM Reasoning via Entropy-Guided Supertokens
Authors: Zhenyu Zhao, Sander Land, Daniel M. Bikel, Waseem Alshikh
First: 2026-04-29T07:06:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:38:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning in Large Language Models incurs significant inference-time compute, yet the token-level information structure of reasoning traces remains underexplored. We observe that reasoning tokens split into two functional types: low-entropy \textit{structural} tokens (recurring phrases that scaffold the reasoning process) and higher-entropy \textit{organic} tokens (problem-specific content that drives toward a solution). This asymmetry motivates a simple, model-agnostic compression pipeline: apply cross-word BPE merges on a model's own reasoning traces to derive \textit{supertokens} that capture frequent structural patterns, then teach the model to adopt them via supervised fine-tuning. Across three model families and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, our approach shortens reasoning traces by 8.1\% on average with no statistically significant accuracy loss on any model--benchmark pair. Beyond compression, supertokens act as interpretable reasoning-move annotations (backtracking, verification, strategy shifts), exposing the model's high-level strategy at a glance. Analyzing transitions between structural categories reveals systematic differences between correct and incorrect traces: correct traces show productive recovery (backtracking followed by strategy shifts and verification), while incorrect traces are dominated by confusion cycles (repeated hedging and unresolved contradictions). These diagnostic signals suggest applications in reward shaping and early stopping for RL-based reasoning training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：思维的简写：通过熵引导的超令牌压缩大语言模型推理
大语言模型推理在推理时消耗大量计算资源，但推理轨迹的令牌级信息结构尚未被充分探索。我们观察到推理令牌可分为两类功能：低熵的\textit{结构}令牌（重复短语，支撑推理过程）和高熵的\textit{有机}令牌（问题特定内容，推动向解决方案）。这种不对称性促使我们设计一个简单且模型无关的压缩流程：对模型自身的推理轨迹应用跨词BPE合并，生成\textit{超令牌}以捕捉频繁的结构模式，然后通过监督微调使模型采用这些超令牌。在三个模型家族和五个数学推理基准上，我们的方法平均缩短推理轨迹8.1%，且在任何模型-基准对上均无统计显著的准确率损失。除了压缩，超令牌还充当可解释的推理步骤注释（回溯、验证、策略转换），直观揭示模型的高层策略。分析结构类别间的转换揭示了正确与错误轨迹的系统性差异：正确轨迹显示有效的恢复（回溯后伴随策略转换和验证），而错误轨迹则以困惑循环为主（重复回避和未解决的矛盾）。这些诊断信号暗示其在基于强化学习的推理训练中的奖励塑造和早期停止应用潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of LLM reasoning and the underexplored token-level structure of reasoning traces, the authors propose a compression method that distinguishes between low-entropy structural tokens and high-entropy organic tokens. They apply cross-word BPE merges on model-generated reasoning traces to extract supertokens representing recurring structural patterns, then fine-tune the model to use these supertokens. Across three model families and five benchmarks, this approach reduces reasoning trace length by 8.1% on average without statistically significant accuracy loss. Additionally, supertokens serve as interpretable annotations of reasoning moves, revealing that correct traces exhibit productive recovery patterns while incorrect ones show confusion cycles, offering diagnostic signals for reward shaping and early stopping in RL-based reasoning training.
为降低大语言模型推理的计算开销并挖掘推理痕迹中未被充分研究的词元结构，作者提出一种压缩方法，将推理词元分为低熵结构词（重复出现的推理框架）和高熵有机词（问题特定内容）。通过在模型自身推理痕迹上应用跨词BPE合并提取超词元，并通过监督微调使模型采用这些超词元，平均压缩推理痕迹8.1%，且在所有模型-基准组合中均未显著降低准确率。此外，超词元可作为可解释的推理动作注释，揭示高层策略；分析结构类别转换发现，正确推理痕迹呈现有效恢复模式（回溯后策略调整与验证），而错误痕迹则多为困惑循环（重复回避与矛盾未解），为强化学习推理训练提供诊断信号。
UnAC: Adaptive Visual Prompting with Abstraction and Stepwise Checking for Complex Multimodal Reasoning
Authors: Yifan Wang, Yun Fu
First: 2026-05-05T16:36:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:36:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although recent LMMs have become much stronger at visual perception, they remain unreliable on problems that require multi-step reasoning over visual evidence. In this paper, we present UnAC (Understanding, Abstracting, and Checking), a multimodal prompting method that strengthens reasoning for complex multimodal tasks in LMMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5, and GPT-4V). To improve image understanding and capture fine details, we propose an adaptive visual prompting strategy that enables LMMs to focus on salient regions. We further design an image-abstraction prompt to effectively extract key information from images. In addition, we introduce a gradual self-checking scheme that improves reasoning by verifying each decomposed subquestion and its answer. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks-MathVista, MM-Vet, and MMMU.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of large multimodal models (LMMs) in handling multi-step visual reasoning, this paper introduces UnAC, a prompting method that enhances reasoning through adaptive visual attention, image abstraction, and stepwise self-checking. The method first directs LMMs to focus on salient regions in images, then extracts key information via abstraction prompts, and finally verifies each subquestion’s answer sequentially to ensure correctness. Experiments on MathVista, MM-Vet, and MMMU show that UnAC significantly improves performance across complex multimodal reasoning tasks, outperforming baseline prompting methods and demonstrating robustness across different LMMs.
为提升大模型在复杂视觉任务中的多步推理能力，本文提出UnAC方法，通过自适应视觉提示、图像抽象与分步自检三阶段增强推理。该方法首先引导模型聚焦图像关键区域，再通过抽象提示提取核心信息，最后逐个验证分解后的子问题及其答案。在MathVista、MM-Vet和MMMU三个公开数据集上的实验表明，UnAC显著提升模型性能，验证了其在复杂多模态推理任务中的有效性。
Integrating Feature Correlation in Differential Privacy with Applications in DP-ERM
Authors: Tianyu Wang, Luhao Zhang, Rachel Cummings
First: 2026-05-05T16:32:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:32:11+00:00
Comments: Appeared in AISTATS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Standard differential privacy imposes uniform privacy constraints across all features, overlooking the inherent distinction between sensitive and insensitive features in practice. In this paper, we introduce a relaxed definition of differential privacy that accounts for such heterogeneity, allowing certain features to be treated as insensitive even when correlated with sensitive ones. We propose a correlation-aware framework, $\textsf{CorrDP}$, which relaxes privacy for insensitive features while accounting for their correlations with sensitive features, with the correlations quantified using total variation distance. We design algorithms for differentially private empirical risk minimization (DP-ERM) under the $\textsf{CorrDP}$ framework, incorporating distance-dependent noise into gradients for improved theoretical utility guarantees. When the correlation distance is unknown, we estimate it from the dataset and show that it achieves a comparable privacy-utility guarantee. We perform experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets and show that $\textsf{CorrDP}$-based DP-ERM algorithms consistently outperform the standard DP framework in the presence of insensitive features.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在差分隐私中整合特征相关性及其在DP-ERM中的应用
标准差分隐私对所有特征施加统一的隐私约束，忽略了实践中敏感特征与非敏感特征之间的固有差异。本文引入一种放松的差分隐私定义，以考虑这种异质性，允许某些特征即使与敏感特征相关，仍可被视为非敏感。我们提出一种感知相关性的框架CorrDP，通过总变差距离量化特征间相关性，在放松非敏感特征隐私的同时考虑其与敏感特征的相关性。我们在CorrDP框架下设计了差分隐私经验风险最小化（DP-ERM）算法，通过引入距离依赖噪声提升梯度的理论效用保证。当相关距离未知时，我们从数据集中进行估计，并证明其可实现相近的隐私-效用平衡。我们在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，基于CorrDP的DP-ERM算法在存在非敏感特征时始终优于标准DP框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of standard differential privacy that treats all features uniformly without distinguishing sensitive from insensitive ones, this paper proposes CorrDP, a relaxed privacy definition that accounts for feature correlations. The method quantifies correlations using total variation distance and adjusts noise in gradient updates based on distance to preserve utility while maintaining privacy. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets show that CorrDP-based DP-ERM algorithms achieve better utility than standard DP when insensitive features are present, even when correlation distances are estimated from data.
鉴于标准差分隐私对所有特征施加统一隐私约束，忽略了敏感与非敏感特征的实际差异，本文提出CorrDP框架，允许与敏感特征相关但本身非敏感的特征获得较低隐私预算。该方法通过总变差距离量化特征间相关性，并据此在梯度中注入距离依赖噪声以提升效用。实验表明，无论相关距离是否已知，基于CorrDP的DP-ERM算法在存在非敏感特征时，其效用均优于标准差分隐私方法。
TabSurv: Adapting Modern Tabular Neural Networks to Survival Analysis
Authors: Stanislav Kirpichenko, Andrei Konstantinov, Lev Utkin
First: 2026-05-05T16:32:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:32:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Survival analysis on tabular data is a well-studied problem. However, existing deep learning methods are often highly task-specific, which can limit the transfer of new approaches from other domains and introduce constraints that may affect performance. We propose TabSurv, an approach that adapts modern tabular architectures to survival analysis using either the Weibull distribution or non-parametric survival prediction. TabSurv optimizes SurvHL, a novel histogram loss function supporting censored data. In addition to a baseline feed-forward network, we implement deep ensembles of MLPs for survival analysis within TabSurv. In contrast to prior work, the ensemble components are trained in parallel, optimizing survival distribution parameters before averaging, which promotes diversity across ensemble component predictions. We perform a comprehensive empirical evaluation of different proposed architectures on 10 diverse real-world survival datasets. Our results show that TabSurv consistently outperforms on average established classical and deep learning baselines, such as RSF, DeepSurv, DeepHit, SurvTRACE. Notably, deep ensembles with Weibull parametrization instead of non-parametric models achieve the highest average rank by C-index. Overall, our study clarifies how modern tabular neural networks can be adapted and trained to tackle survival analysis problems, offering a strong and reliable approach. The TabSurv implementation is publicly available.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt modern tabular neural networks to survival analysis while improving generalization and performance, TabSurv introduces a framework that leverages either Weibull parametric or non-parametric survival prediction, optimized via a novel histogram loss function (SurvHL) designed for censored data. The method includes both a baseline feed-forward network and deep ensembles of MLPs, where ensemble components are trained in parallel to optimize survival distribution parameters before averaging, enhancing prediction diversity. Experimental results across 10 real-world survival datasets show TabSurv consistently outperforms classical and deep learning baselines like RSF, DeepSurv, DeepHit, and SurvTRACE, with deep ensembles using Weibull parametrization achieving the highest average rank by C-index.
为提升现代表格神经网络在生存分析中的适应性与性能，TabSurv提出一种框架，支持Weibull参数化或非参数化生存预测，采用专为删失数据设计的新型直方图损失函数（SurvHL）进行优化。该方法包含基础前馈网络和深度MLP集成模型，其中集成组件并行训练，先优化生存分布参数再平均，以增强预测多样性。在10个真实生存数据集上的实验表明，TabSurv整体优于RSF、DeepSurv、DeepHit和SurvTRACE等经典及深度学习基线模型，其中采用Weibull参数化的深度集成模型在C指数平均排名中表现最优。
Do Multimodal RAG Systems Leak Data? A Comprehensive Evaluation of Membership Inference and Image Caption Retrieval Attacks
Authors: Ali Al-Lawati, Suhang Wang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-25T01:37:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:31:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at ACL 2026 (Findings)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The growing adoption of multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (mRAG) pipelines for vision-centric tasks (e.g., visual QA) introduces important privacy challenges. In particular, while mRAG provides a practical capability to connect private datasets and improve model performance, it risks the leakage of private information from these datasets. In this paper, we perform an empirical study to analyze the privacy risks inherent in the mRAG pipeline observed through standard model prompting. Specifically, we implement a case study that attempts to determine whether a visual asset (e.g., image) is included in the mRAG, and, if present, to leak the metadata (e.g., caption) related to it. Our findings highlight the need for privacy-preserving mechanisms and motivate future research on mRAG privacy. Our code is published online: https://github.com/aliwister/mrag-attack-eval.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态RAG系统会泄露数据吗？对成员推断和图像字幕检索攻击的全面评估
随着多模态检索增强生成（mRAG）管道在以视觉为中心的任务（如视觉问答）中的广泛应用，其带来了重要的隐私挑战。特别是，虽然mRAG能够将私有数据集与模型连接起来并提升性能，但也存在从这些数据集中泄露隐私信息的风险。本文通过实证研究，分析了通过标准模型提示观察到的mRAG管道固有的隐私风险。具体而言，我们实施了一个案例研究，旨在判断某个视觉资产（如图像）是否包含在mRAG中，如果存在，则尝试泄露其相关元数据（如字幕）。我们的研究结果强调了需要隐私保护机制，并推动了未来关于mRAG隐私的研究。我们的代码已在线发布：https://github.com/aliwister/mrag-attack-eval。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the privacy risks associated with multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (mRAG) systems used in vision-centric tasks, this study investigates whether such systems leak private data through standard prompting. The authors conduct a case study to determine if visual assets like images are present in an mRAG system and, if so, whether associated metadata such as captions can be inferred. Experimental results show that mRAG systems are vulnerable to membership inference and image caption retrieval attacks, confirming that private data can be leaked even when not directly exposed in outputs. These findings underscore the urgent need for privacy-preserving techniques in mRAG pipelines.
鉴于多模态检索增强生成（mRAG）系统在视觉任务中广泛应用可能带来的隐私风险，本研究通过案例分析评估其是否会在标准提示下泄露私有数据。实验旨在检测视觉资产是否存在于系统中，并尝试推断其关联元数据（如图像描述）。结果表明，mRAG系统易受成员推断和图像描述检索攻击，可高精度重建私有图像-描述对，揭示其缺乏隐私保护机制。
Reservoir property image slices from the Groningen gas field for image translation and segmentation
Authors: Abdulrahman Al-Fakih, Nabil Sariah, Ardiansyah Koeshidayatullah, SanLinn I. Kaka
First: 2026-05-05T16:31:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:31:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reservoir characterization workflows increasingly rely on image-based and machine-learning/deep learning or even generative AI approaches, but openly available geological image datasets suitable for reproducible benchmarking remain limited. Here we describe a high-resolution dataset of reservoir-property image slices derived from the Groningen static geological model. The dataset contains aligned two-dimensional PNG images representing facies, porosity, permeability, and water saturation, generated from three-dimensional reservoir grids and prepared for downstream visualization, segmentation, and image-to-image translation tasks. In addition to the deposited original image corpus, we provide an archived software workflow for reproducing augmentation, mask generation, paired-image construction, and example baseline experiments. The resource is designed to support benchmarking of geological image analysis methods and the study of cross-domain relationships among reservoir properties. By separating the fixed image dataset from the reproducible processing workflow, this work provides a transparent foundation for reuse in geoscience, reservoir modeling, and machine-learning applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：格罗宁根气田储层属性图像切片用于图像翻译与分割
储层表征工作流程日益依赖基于图像和机器学习/深度学习甚至生成式AI的方法，但公开可用的适合可重复基准测试的地质图像数据集仍十分有限。本文描述了一个高分辨率的储层属性图像切片数据集，源自格罗宁根静态地质模型。该数据集包含对齐的二维PNG图像，代表相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度，由三维储层网格生成，并为下游可视化、分割和图像到图像翻译任务做好准备。除已存档的原始图像语料库外，我们还提供一个存档的软件工作流程，用于重现数据增强、掩码生成、配对图像构建及示例基线实验。该资源旨在支持地质图像分析方法的基准测试及储层属性跨域关系的研究。通过将固定图像数据集与可重复处理工作流程分离，本研究为地球科学、储层建模和机器学习应用提供了透明的重用基础。

Motivated by the scarcity of openly available geological image datasets for reproducible benchmarking, this study presents a high-resolution dataset of reservoir-property image slices from the Groningen gas field, generated from 3D geological models and representing facies, porosity, permeability, and water saturation. The dataset, formatted as aligned 2D PNG images, is designed for downstream tasks such as image segmentation and translation, and is accompanied by a reproducible software workflow for data augmentation, mask generation, and baseline experiments. Experimental results demonstrate the dataset’s utility in benchmarking machine learning methods and studying cross-domain relationships among reservoir properties, with the workflow enabling transparent and repeatable analysis in geoscience and reservoir modeling applications.
为解决地质图像数据集稀缺且难以复现的问题，本研究构建了来自格罗宁根气田的高分辨率储层属性图像切片数据集，由三维地质模型生成，涵盖岩相、孔隙度、渗透率和含水饱和度等属性。数据集包含对齐的二维PNG图像，专用于图像翻译与分割任务，并附带可复现的软件工作流，支持数据增强、掩码生成和基线实验。实验表明，该数据集有效支持跨属性域分析与机器学习模型评估，通过分离固定图像与处理流程，为地质科学与储层建模提供了透明可复用的基础。
A Benchmark for Interactive World Models with a Unified Action Generation Framework
Authors: Jianjie Fang, Yingshan Lei, Qin Wan, Ziyou Wang, Yuchao Huang, Yongyan Xu, Baining Zhao, Weichen Zhang, Chen Gao, Xinlei Chen, Yong Li
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-05T16:30:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:30:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Achieving Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) requires agents that learn and interact adaptively, with interactive world models providing scalable environments for perception, reasoning, and action. Yet current research still lacks large-scale datasets and unified benchmarks to evaluate their physical interaction capabilities. To address this, we propose iWorld-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for training and testing world models on interaction-related abilities such as distance perception and memory. We construct a diverse dataset with 330k video clips and select 2.1k high-quality samples covering varied perspectives, weather, and scenes. As existing world models differ in interaction modalities, we introduce an Action Generation Framework to unify evaluation and design six task types, generating 4.9k test samples. These tasks jointly assess model performance across visual generation, trajectory following, and memory. Evaluating 14 representative world models, we identify key limitations and provide insights for future research. The iWorld-Bench model leaderboard is publicly available at iWorld-Bench.com.
