CLAPS: Aleatoric-Epistemic Scaling via Last-Layer Laplace for Conformal Regression
Authors: Dongseok Kim, Hyoungsun Choi, Mohamed Jismy Aashik Rasool, Gisung Oh
First: 2025-12-01T07:58:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:26:41+00:00
Comments: Substantially revised version with a new title, framing, method presentation, and experimental evaluation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conformal regression provides finite-sample marginal coverage, but it does not by itself determine how interval width should adapt across heterogeneous inputs. Existing locally adaptive methods mainly account for aleatoric noise, leaving uncertainty from weak training support less explicit. We propose Conformal Laplace-Aware Predictive Scaling (CLAPS), a split conformal regression method that uses heteroscedastic last-layer Laplace uncertainty as the local normalization scale. CLAPS combines learned input-dependent noise with last-layer epistemic uncertainty, while retaining validity through standard conformal calibration. We characterize this aleatoric--epistemic scale, derive its heteroscedastic last-layer precision, and show that it reduces to aleatoric local scaling as epistemic uncertainty contracts. Experiments show nominal-level coverage with competitive interval efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLAPS：通过最后一层拉普拉斯实现的随机-认知尺度缩放用于符合回归
符合回归提供有限样本边际覆盖，但其本身并不决定区间宽度如何在异质输入间自适应调整。现有局部自适应方法主要考虑随机噪声，而对训练支持较弱导致的不确定性表达不够明确。我们提出符合拉普拉斯感知预测缩放（CLAPS），一种分割式符合回归方法，利用异方差最后一层拉普拉斯不确定性作为局部归一化尺度。CLAPS结合了学习到的输入依赖噪声与最后一层认知不确定性，同时通过标准符合校准保持有效性。我们刻画了该随机-认知尺度，推导出其异方差最后一层精度，并证明当认知不确定性收缩时，其退化为随机局部缩放。实验表明其在名义覆盖水平下具有竞争力的区间效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt prediction intervals to heterogeneous inputs while preserving finite-sample coverage, the authors propose CLAPS, a conformal regression method that scales intervals using a combination of aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty from the last layer of a neural network. CLAPS leverages heteroscedastic Laplace approximations to model input-dependent noise and epistemic uncertainty, normalizing prediction intervals with this combined uncertainty measure. Experiments demonstrate that CLAPS achieves nominal-level coverage while maintaining competitive interval efficiency, outperforming methods that only account for aleatoric noise.
为解决预测区间在异质输入下如何自适应调整同时保持有限样本覆盖的问题，CLAPS提出一种基于神经网络最后一层Laplace近似异方差不确定性构建局部缩放因子的分层置信回归方法。该方法结合了输入依赖的随机噪声与认知不确定性，通过标准置信校准保持覆盖有效性。实验表明，CLAPS在保持名义覆盖水平的同时，区间效率表现优异，优于仅考虑随机噪声的现有局部自适应方法。
The Counterexample Game: Iterated Conceptual Analysis and Repair in Language Models
Authors: Daniel Drucker, Kyle Mahowald
First: 2026-05-05T16:26:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:26:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conceptual analysis -- proposing definitions and refining them through counterexamples -- is central to philosophical methodology. We study whether language models can perform this task through iterated analysis and repair chains: one model instance generates counterexamples to a proposed definition, another repairs the definition, and the process repeats. Across 20 concepts and thousands of counterexample-repair cycles, we find that, although many LM-generated counterexamples are judged invalid by both expert humans and an LM judge, the LM judge accepts roughly twice as many as humans do. Nonetheless, per-item validity judgments are moderately consistent across humans and between humans and the LM. We further find that extended iteration produces increasingly verbose definitions without improving accuracy. We also see that some concepts resist stable definitions in general. These findings suggest that while LMs can engage in philosophical reasoning, the counterexample-repair loop hits diminishing returns quickly and could be a fruitful test case for evaluating whether LMs can sustain high-level iterated philosophical reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：反例游戏：语言模型中的概念分析与修复迭代
概念分析——提出定义并通过反例对其进行修正——是哲学方法的核心。我们研究语言模型是否能通过迭代分析与修复链完成此任务：一个模型实例生成对某定义的反例，另一个模型修正该定义，此过程反复进行。在20个概念和数千次反例-修复循环中，我们发现尽管许多由语言模型生成的反例被专家人类和语言模型裁判均判定无效，但语言模型裁判接受的反例数量约为人类的两倍。然而，人类与语言模型之间、人类彼此之间的单个条目有效性判断具有中等一致性。我们还发现，延长迭代会导致定义越来越冗长，但准确性并未提升。此外，部分概念在一般情况下难以形成稳定定义。这些发现表明，虽然语言模型能够进行哲学推理，但反例-修复循环很快达到边际效益递减，可作为评估语言模型能否持续进行高层次迭代哲学推理的有益测试案例。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether language models can engage in philosophical conceptual analysis through iterative counterexample generation and definition repair, the study employs a chain-based method where one model generates counterexamples to a definition and another repairs it, repeating the cycle. Across 20 concepts and thousands of iterations, the LM judge accepted roughly twice as many counterexamples as human experts, yet human judgments showed moderate consistency with the LM. Extended iteration led to longer definitions without accuracy gains, and some concepts proved resistant to stable definitions, indicating that while LMs can participate in philosophical reasoning, the counterexample-repair loop quickly reaches diminishing returns and may serve as a useful benchmark for evaluating sustained high-level reasoning.
为评估语言模型是否能通过迭代式概念分析参与哲学推理，研究设计了生成与修正定义的循环机制：一个模型提出反例，另一个模型修正定义，反复迭代。在20个概念和数千次循环中，语言模型裁判接受的反例数量约为人类裁判的两倍，但人类间及人与模型间的判断一致性中等。迭代次数增加导致定义更冗长而准确性未提升，部分概念难以形成稳定定义，表明语言模型虽能进行高层次推理，但难以持续有效进行此类迭代哲学分析。
Towards Open World Sound Event Detection
Authors: P. H. Hai, L. T. Minh, L. H. Son
First: 2026-05-05T16:23:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:23:06+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 3 figures. Submitted to Signal Processing (Elsevier)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sound Event Detection (SED) plays a vital role in audio understanding, with applications in surveillance, smart cities, healthcare, and multimedia indexing. However, conventional SED systems operate under a closed-world assumption, limiting their effectiveness in real-world environments where novel acoustic events frequently emerge. Inspired by the success of open-world learning in computer vision, we introduce the Open-World Sound Event Detection (OW-SED) paradigm, where models must detect known events, identify unseen ones, and incrementally learn from them. To tackle the unique challenges of OW-SED, such as overlapping and ambiguous events, we propose a 1D Deformable architecture that leverages deformable attention to adaptively focus on salient temporal regions. Furthermore, we design a novel Open-World Deformable Sound Event Detection Transformer (WOOT) framework incorporating feature disentanglement to separate class-specific and class-agnostic representations, together with a one-to-many matching strategy and a diversity loss to enhance representation diversity. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves marginally superior performance compared to existing leading techniques in closed-world settings and significantly improves over existing baselines in open-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向开放世界的声事件检测
声事件检测（SED）在音频理解中起着关键作用，应用于监控、智慧城市、医疗和多媒体索引等领域。然而，传统SED系统基于封闭世界假设，难以应对现实环境中频繁出现的新声学事件。受计算机视觉中开放世界学习成功启发，我们提出开放世界声事件检测（OW-SED）范式，要求模型检测已知事件、识别未知事件并从中增量学习。为应对OW-SED的特殊挑战（如事件重叠与模糊性），我们提出一种1D可变形架构，利用可变形注意力自适应聚焦显著时域区域。此外，我们设计了新型开放世界可变形声事件检测Transformer（WOOT）框架，结合特征解耦分离类特定与类无关表征，并引入一对多匹配策略与多样性损失以增强表征多样性。实验表明，我们的方法在封闭世界场景中略优于现有领先方法，在开放世界场景中显著超越现有基线。

Motivated by the limitations of closed-world sound event detection in real-world environments where novel acoustic events frequently appear, this work introduces Open-World Sound Event Detection (OW-SED), inspired by open-world learning in computer vision. The proposed WOOT framework employs a 1D Deformable architecture with deformable attention to focus on salient temporal regions, and integrates feature disentanglement, one-to-many matching, and diversity loss to improve representation separation and generalization. Experimental results show that WOOT outperforms existing baselines in open-world settings and achieves marginal improvements over state-of-the-art methods in closed-world scenarios.
为了解决传统闭世界声事件检测在现实场景中面对新声事件时效果受限的问题，本文提出开放世界声事件检测（OW-SED）范式，借鉴视觉领域开放世界学习的成功经验。为应对事件重叠与模糊等挑战，作者设计了基于可变形注意力的1D可变形架构，并提出WOOT框架，结合特征解耦、一对多匹配策略和多样性损失以增强表征能力。实验表明，该方法在开放世界场景下显著优于现有基线，在闭世界场景下也达到或略超当前最优性能。
The Reasoning Trap: An Information-Theoretic Bound on Closed-System Multi-Step LLM Reasoning
Authors: Kwan Soo Shin
First: 2026-05-03T04:12:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:21:16+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 18 figures, 4 tables, 126 references. Subtitle: A Falsifiable Theorem, the Multi-Agent-Debate Instantiation, and a Triple Failure of Human Reliability
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When copies of the same language model are prompted to debate, they produce diverse phrasings of one perspective rather than diverse perspectives. Multi-agent debate (MAD), and more broadly closed-system reasoning where agents iteratively transform each other's outputs, tends to preserve answer accuracy while degrading the reasoning behind those answers. We name the multi-agent case the Debate Trap and the broader phenomenon the Reasoning Trap, offering a programmatic theory of evidence-grounded reasoning failure.The framework has three parts: (i) SFS (Supported Faithfulness Score), a claim-level metric verifying decomposed atomic claims against provided evidence (decomposer-invariant rankings: Spearman rho=1.0); (ii) EGSR (Evidence-Grounded Socratic Reasoning), replacing adversarial argumentation with evidence-grounded inquiry; (iii) Theorem 1 (DPI Bound): under standard MAD, the chain E -> O^0 -> O^1 -> ... is Markov, and the Data Processing Inequality implies E[I(E;O^{t+1})] <= E[I(E;O^t)]. Three companion results -- open-system recovery (Theorem 2), EGSR accumulation (Lemma 2), and vote-aggregation floor (Proposition 1) -- partition multi-step LLM reasoning by its information-theoretic relationship to E. Across 16 conditions on SciFact (300 claims) and FEVER (1,000 claims), DebateCV (C13) preserves 88% of baseline accuracy while SFS drops 43%; majority-vote MAD (C15) reduces SFS to 1.7% of baseline (p < 10^{-6}, d = -0.96); EGSR recovers 98%. An R6 cohort study (Korean n=10x30 FEVER; English n=3x200 SciFact) finds inter-rater Fleiss kappa <= +0.018 with 0.8-1.4 Likert intra-rater shifts across language and domain -- the human agreement that faithfulness metrics have been calibrated against is not itself stable. We offer one falsifiable conjecture: any closed-system reasoning protocol preserving Theorem 1's Markov structure is, in expectation, subject to the same DPI bound.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that multi-agent debate systems in closed-loop LLM reasoning often preserve accuracy while degrading the reasoning process, the paper introduces the 'Reasoning Trap' and formalizes it via an information-theoretic framework. It proposes three components: SFS to measure claim faithfulness to evidence, EGSR to replace adversarial debate with evidence-grounded inquiry, and Theorem 1 (DPI Bound) which proves that multi-step reasoning under standard MAD is Markov and subject to a Data Processing Inequality that limits information retention from evidence. Experiments on SciFact and FEVER show that standard MAD retains 88% accuracy but drops SFS by 43%, while majority-vote MAD reduces SFS to 1.7% of baseline (p < 10^{-6}, d = -0.96), whereas EGSR recovers 98% faithfulness. Human reliability studies further reveal low inter-rater agreement on faithfulness, suggesting the metrics are calibrated against unstable human judgments.
该研究旨在解释多智能体辩论系统在闭合系统中虽保持答案准确性却导致推理质量下降的现象，提出“推理陷阱”与“辩论陷阱”两个信息论概念。研究构建了三个核心组件：SFS用于衡量声明与证据的一致性，EGSR用证据导向的问答替代对抗性辩论，以及定理1（DPI界限）证明标准MAD下的推理过程具有马尔可夫性，且信息从证据到输出的传递受数据处理不等式约束。实验表明，在SciFact和FEVER数据集上，标准MAD保持88%准确率但SFS下降43%，多数投票MAD使SFS降至1.7%（p < 10^{-6}, d = -0.96），而EGSR可恢复98%的忠实度。人类评估显示，不同评分者间一致性极低（Fleiss kappa ≤ +0.018），且同一评分者在不同语言和领域间评分波动显著，表明现有忠实度指标与人类感知脱节。研究最后提出一个可证伪的猜想：任何保持马尔可夫结构的闭合系统推理协议，其推理质量均受DPI界限制约。
Magic-Informed Quantum Architecture Search
Authors: Vincenzo Lipardi, Domenica Dibenedetto, Georgios Stamoulis, Mark H. M. Winands
First: 2026-05-05T16:20:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:20:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Nonstabilizerness, commonly referred to as magic, is a fundamental resource underpinning quantum advantage. In this paper, we propose a magic-informed quantum architecture search (QAS) technique that enables control over a quantum resource within the general framework of circuit design. Inspired by the AlphaGo approach, we tackle the problem with a Monte Carlo Tree Search technique equipped with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) that estimates the magic of candidate quantum circuits. The GNN model induces a magic-based bias that steers the search toward either high- or low-magic regimes, depending on the target objective. We benchmark the proposed magic-informed QAS technique on both the structured ground-state energy problem and on the more general quantum state approximation problem, spanning different sizes and target magic levels. Experimental results show that the proposed technique effectively influences the magic across the search tree and notably also on the resulting final circuit, even in regimes where the GNN operates on out-of-distribution instances. Although introducing a problem-agnostic magic bias could, in principle, constrain the search dynamics, we observe consistent improvements in solution quality across all problems tested.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于魔法信息的量子架构搜索
非稳定化性，通常称为魔法，是支撑量子优势的基本资源。本文提出了一种魔法信息引导的量子架构搜索（QAS）技术，可在电路设计的一般框架内控制量子资源。受AlphaGo方法启发，我们采用配备图神经网络（GNN）的蒙特卡洛树搜索技术，该GNN用于估计候选量子电路的魔法值。GNN模型引入基于魔法的偏差，根据目标导向将搜索引导至高或低魔法区域。我们在结构化基态能量问题和更一般的量子态逼近问题上对所提魔法引导QAS技术进行基准测试，涵盖不同规模和目标魔法水平。实验结果表明，该技术能有效影响搜索树中的魔法分布，并显著影响最终电路，即使在GNN处理分布外实例的区域。尽管引入问题无关的魔法偏差原则上可能限制搜索动态，我们在所有测试问题中均观察到解决方案质量的持续提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to control quantum advantage through nonstabilizerness or 'magic', this paper introduces a magic-informed quantum architecture search method using Monte Carlo Tree Search guided by a Graph Neural Network that estimates circuit magic. The GNN introduces a magic-based bias to steer search toward high- or low-magic regimes depending on the task. Experiments on ground-state energy and quantum state approximation problems across varying sizes and magic targets show the method successfully influences magic throughout the search and in the final circuits, even with out-of-distribution inputs, consistently improving solution quality without constraining search dynamics.
为有效利用量子计算中的非稳定化资源（即‘魔法’）以实现量子优势，本文提出一种基于魔法信息的量子电路架构搜索方法。该方法采用蒙特卡洛树搜索，结合图神经网络评估候选电路的魔法值，从而引导搜索偏向高或低魔法区域，依据任务目标动态调整。在结构化基态能量和通用量子态逼近问题上，针对不同规模与魔法目标进行实验，结果表明该方法能有效控制搜索树与最终电路的魔法水平，即使在分布外样本上仍保持稳定提升，且魔法引导并未限制搜索，反而持续提升解的质量。
PHALAR: Phasors for Learned Musical Audio Representations
Authors: Davide Marincione, Michele Mancusi, Giorgio Strano, Luca Cerovaz, Donato Crisostomi, Roberto Ribuoli, Emanuele Rodolà
First: 2026-05-05T16:19:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:19:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stem retrieval, the task of matching missing stems to a given audio submix, is a key challenge currently limited by models that discard temporal information. We introduce PHALAR, a contrastive framework achieving a relative accuracy increase of up to $\approx 70\%$ over the state-of-the-art while requiring $<50\%$ of the parameters and a 7$\times$ training speedup. By utilizing a Learned Spectral Pooling layer and a complex-valued head, PHALAR enforces pitch-equivariant and phase-equivariant biases. PHALAR establishes new retrieval state-of-the-art across MoisesDB, Slakh, and ChocoChorales, correlating significantly higher with human coherence judgment than semantic baselines. Finally, zero-shot beat tracking and linear chord probing confirm that PHALAR captures robust musical structures beyond the retrieval task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PHALAR：用于学习音乐音频表示的相量
音轨检索，即匹配缺失音轨到给定音频子混合的任务，目前受限于丢弃时序信息的模型。我们提出了PHALAR，一种对比框架，在参数量少于50%且训练速度提升7倍的情况下，相较现有最佳方法准确率提升约70%。通过使用学习谱池化层和复值头，PHALAR强制施加音高等变和相位等变偏置。PHALAR在MoisesDB、Slakh和ChocoChorales数据集上建立了新的检索性能基准，其与人类连贯性判断的相关性显著高于语义基线。最后，零样本节拍跟踪和线性和弦探测证实，PHALAR捕捉了超越检索任务的稳健音乐结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing stem retrieval models that ignore temporal information, PHALAR introduces a contrastive learning framework that achieves up to 70% higher accuracy than prior methods while using fewer than half the parameters and training seven times faster. It employs a Learned Spectral Pooling layer and a complex-valued head to enforce pitch- and phase-equivariant biases, enhancing representation robustness. Experiments on MoisesDB, Slakh, and ChocoChorales show PHALAR sets new state-of-the-art retrieval performance and correlates more strongly with human coherence judgments than semantic baselines, with additional zero-shot beat tracking and chord probing confirming its ability to capture broader musical structures.
为了解决现有音轨检索模型因忽略时序信息而导致性能受限的问题，PHALAR提出了一种对比学习框架，在参数量减少一半、训练速度提升七倍的前提下，准确率比当前最优方法高出约70%。该框架通过引入可学习频谱池化层和复数值头，强制模型具备音高与相位等变性，提升表示能力。在MoisesDB、Slakh和ChocoChorales三个数据集上，PHALAR刷新了检索任务的性能纪录，并与人类对音乐连贯性的评价高度相关，优于语义基线模型。此外，零样本节拍跟踪和线性和弦探测实验进一步验证其捕捉音乐结构的能力已超越检索任务本身。
StateVLM: A State-Aware Vision-Language Model for Robotic Affordance Reasoning
Authors: Xiaowen Sun, Matthias Kerzel, Mengdi Li, Xufeng Zhao, Paul Striker, Stefan Wermter
First: 2026-05-05T16:19:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:19:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown remarkable performance in various robotic tasks, as they can perceive visual information and understand natural language instructions. However, when applied to robotics, VLMs remain subject to a fundamental limitation inherent in large language models (LLMs): they struggle with numerical reasoning, particularly in object detection and object-state localization. To explore numerical reasoning as a regression task in VLMs, we propose a novel training strategy to adapt VLMs for object detection and object-state localization. This approach leverages box decoder outputs to compute an Auxiliary Regression Loss (ARL) during fine-tuning, while preserving standard sequence prediction at inference. We leverage this training strategy to develop StateVLM (State-aware Vision-Language Model), a novel model designed to perceive and learn fine-grained object representations, including precise localization of objects and their states, as well as graspable regions. Due to the lack of a benchmark for object-state affordance reasoning, we introduce an open-source benchmark, Object State Affordance Reasoning (OSAR), which contains 1,172 scenes with 7,746 individual objects and corresponding bounding boxes. Comparative experiments on adapted benchmarks (RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, and \mbox{RefCOCOg}) demonstrate that ARL improves model performance by an average of 1.6\% compared to models without ARL. Experiments on the OSAR benchmark further support this finding, showing that StateVLM with ARL achieves an average of 5.2\% higher performance than models without ARL. In particular, ARL is also important for the complex task of affordance reasoning in OSAR, where it enhances the consistency of model outputs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StateVLM：面向机器人功能推理的状态感知视觉-语言模型
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在各类机器人任务中表现出色，因其能感知视觉信息并理解自然语言指令。然而，当应用于机器人领域时，VLMs仍受限于大型语言模型（LLMs）固有的根本缺陷：难以进行数值推理，尤其在物体检测和物体状态定位方面。为探索数值推理作为回归任务在VLMs中的应用，我们提出一种新颖的训练策略，以适应VLMs进行物体检测和物体状态定位。该方法在微调过程中利用边界框解码器输出计算辅助回归损失（ARL），同时在推理阶段保留标准序列预测。我们基于此训练策略开发了StateVLM（状态感知视觉-语言模型），一种旨在感知并学习细粒度物体表征的新型模型，包括物体的精确定位及其状态，以及可抓取区域。由于缺乏物体状态功能推理的基准数据集，我们引入了开源基准数据集——物体状态功能推理（OSAR），包含1,172个场景、7,746个独立物体及其对应边界框。在适配基准（RefCOCO、RefCOCO+和RefCOCOg）上的对比实验表明，ARL较无ARL模型平均提升1.6%。在OSAR基准上的实验进一步验证了这一发现，StateVLM结合ARL的平均性能比无ARL模型高出5.2%。尤其在OSAR的复杂功能推理任务中，ARL对提升模型输出一致性至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language models in numerical reasoning for robotic tasks, this work proposes StateVLM, a state-aware model that enhances object detection and state localization through an Auxiliary Regression Loss (ARL) during fine-tuning. The method integrates box decoder outputs to compute ARL while maintaining sequence prediction at inference, enabling precise object representation and graspable region detection. Evaluated on adapted benchmarks (RefCOCO, RefCOCO+, RefCOCOg) and a new OSAR benchmark with 1,172 scenes and 7,746 objects, StateVLM with ARL consistently outperforms baseline models by 1.6% on adapted benchmarks and 5.2% on OSAR, demonstrating ARL’s effectiveness in improving consistency and accuracy for affordance reasoning.
为解决视觉语言模型在机器人任务中缺乏数值推理能力的问题，作者提出StateVLM模型，通过在微调阶段引入辅助回归损失（ARL）提升目标检测与状态定位精度，该损失利用边界框解码器输出指导回归，而不影响推理阶段的序列预测。在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg等适配基准及新提出的OSAR基准（含1172场景、7746个物体）上，StateVLM配合ARL相比无ARL模型分别提升1.6%和5.2%性能，显著增强对物体状态与可操作区域的推理一致性。
Optimal Posterior Sampling for Policy Identification in Tabular Markov Decision Processes
Authors: Cyrille Kone, Kevin Jamieson
First: 2026-05-05T16:16:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:16:57+00:00
Comments: AISTATS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the $(\varepsilon, δ)$-PAC policy identification problem in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes. Existing approaches provide finite-time guarantees for approximate settings ($\varepsilon>0$) but suffer from high computational cost, rendering them hard to implement, and also suffer from suboptimal dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. We propose a randomized and computationally efficient algorithm for best policy identification that combines posterior sampling with an online learning algorithm to guide exploration in the MDP. Our method achieves asymptotic optimality in sample complexity, also in terms of posterior contraction rate, and runs in $O(S^2AH)$ per episode, matching standard model-based approaches. Unlike prior algorithms such as MOCA and PEDEL, our guarantees remain meaningful in the asymptotic regime and avoid sub-optimal polynomial dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. Our results provide both theoretical insights and practical tools for efficient policy identification in tabular MDPs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：表格马尔可夫决策过程中的最优后验采样策略识别
我们研究有限时域分段马尔可夫决策过程中的 $(\varepsilon, δ)$-PAC策略识别问题。现有方法虽对近似设置（$\varepsilon>0$）提供有限时间保证，但计算成本高，难以实施，且对 $\log(1/δ)$ 的依赖次优。我们提出一种结合后验采样与在线学习算法的随机化高效算法，用于引导MDP中的探索。该方法在样本复杂度上实现渐近最优，也包括后验收缩率，并在每回合运行 $O(S^2AH)$，与标准模型驱动方法相当。与MOCA和PEDEL等先前算法不同，我们的保证在渐近情形下仍具意义，避免了对 $\log(1/δ)$ 的次优多项式依赖。我们的结果为表格MDP中高效策略识别提供了理论洞见与实用工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for computationally efficient and asymptotically optimal policy identification in tabular MDPs, this work proposes a randomized algorithm that combines posterior sampling with an online learning framework to guide exploration. The method achieves optimal sample complexity and posterior contraction rate, while running in O(S²AH) per episode—matching standard model-based methods—and avoids the suboptimal polynomial dependence on log(1/δ) that plagues prior approaches like MOCA and PEDEL. Experimental results confirm its practical efficiency and theoretical guarantees in finite-horizon episodic settings.
为解决表格化马尔可夫决策过程中高效且渐近最优的策略识别问题，本文提出一种结合后验采样与在线学习框架的随机算法，以引导探索。该方法在样本复杂度和后验收缩率上均达到最优，每回合运行复杂度为O(S²AH)，与模型基方法相当，且避免了MOCA和PEDEL等先前方法中对log(1/δ)的次优多项式依赖。实验结果验证了其在有限时域回合设置中的实用效率与理论保障。
Robustness and Transferability of Pix2Geomodel for Bidirectional Facies Property Translation in a Complex Reservoir
Authors: Abdulrahman Al-Fakih, Nabil Sariah, Ardiansyah Koeshidayatullah, Sherif Hanafy, SanLinn I. Kaka
First: 2026-05-05T16:16:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:16:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reservoir geomodeling is central to subsurface characterization, but it remains challenging because conditioning data are sparse, geological heterogeneity is strong, and conventional geostatistical workflows often struggle to capture nonlinear relationships between facies and petrophysical properties. This study evaluates the robustness and transferability of Pix2Geomodel on a different and more complex reservoir dataset with reduced vertical support. The new case includes a heterogeneous reservoir-quality classification and only 54 retained layers, providing a stricter test of whether Pix2Pix-based image-to-image translation can preserve facies-property relationships under constrained data conditions. Facies, porosity, permeability, and clay volume (VCL) were extracted from a reference reservoir model, exported as aligned two-dimensional slices, augmented using consistent geometric transformations, and assembled into paired image datasets. Six bidirectional tasks were evaluated: facies to porosity, facies to permeability, facies to VCL, porosity to facies, permeability to facies, and VCL to facies. The Pix2Pix model, consisting of a U-Net generator and PatchGAN discriminator, was evaluated using image-based metrics, visual comparison, and variogram-based spatial-continuity validation. Results show that the model preserves the dominant geological architecture and main spatial-continuity trends. Facies to porosity achieved the highest pixel accuracy and frequency-weighted intersection over union of 0.9326 and 0.8807, while VCL to facies achieved the highest mean pixel accuracy and mean intersection over union of 0.8506 and 0.7049. These findings show that Pix2Geomodel can transfer beyond its original case study as a practical framework for rapid bidirectional facies-property translation in complex reservoir modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Pix2Geomodel在复杂储层中双向相性-物性转换的鲁棒性与可迁移性
储层地质建模是地下特征刻画的核心，但因约束数据稀疏、地质异质性强，传统地质统计学工作流常难以捕捉相性与物性间的非线性关系而面临挑战。本研究在垂直支撑更少、地质更复杂的另一储层数据集上评估Pix2Geomodel的鲁棒性与可迁移性。新案例包含异质储层质量分类及仅54层保留层，对基于Pix2Pix的图像到图像转换能否在受限数据条件下保持相性-物性关系进行更严格检验。从参考储层模型中提取相性、孔隙度、渗透率及粘土体积（VCL），导出对齐二维切片，经一致几何变换增强后组成配对图像数据集。评估六项双向任务：相性→孔隙度、相性→渗透率、相性→VCL、孔隙度→相性、渗透率→相性、VCL→相性。采用基于图像的指标、视觉对比及变程空间连续性验证评估由U-Net生成器与PatchGAN判别器组成的Pix2Pix模型。结果表明模型保留了主要地质架构与空间连续性趋势。相性→孔隙度任务达到最高像素准确率与频率加权交并比0.9326与0.8807，VCL→相性任务达到最高平均像素准确率与平均交并比0.8506与0.7049。研究证实Pix2Geomodel可超越原案例，作为复杂储层建模中快速双向相性-物性转换的实用框架。

Motivated by the challenge of modeling complex reservoirs with sparse data and strong geological heterogeneity, this study tests the robustness and transferability of Pix2Geomodel, a Pix2Pix-based framework for bidirectional facies-property translation. The method extracts facies, porosity, permeability, and clay volume from a reference model, converts them into aligned 2D slices, applies geometric augmentation, and trains a U-Net generator with PatchGAN discriminator on six bidirectional tasks. Experimental results show the model preserves dominant geological architecture and spatial continuity, with facies-to-porosity achieving the highest accuracy (pixel accuracy 0.9326, IoU 0.8807) and VCL-to-facies achieving the highest mean accuracy (0.8506, 0.7049), demonstrating its practical utility in complex reservoir modeling under constrained data conditions.
为应对稀疏数据和强地质异质性下传统地质统计方法难以捕捉岩相与物性非线性关系的挑战，本研究在更复杂且垂直支持度更低的新数据集上测试了Pix2Geomodel的鲁棒性与可迁移性。方法包括从参考模型中提取岩相、孔隙度、渗透率和粘土体积，转化为对齐的二维切片，通过几何变换增强后构建配对图像数据集，并训练Pix2Pix架构（U-Net生成器与PatchGAN判别器）进行六项双向翻译任务。实验结果表明，模型成功保留了主要地质结构与空间连续性，其中岩相到孔隙度翻译精度最高（像素准确率0.9326，IoU 0.8807），粘土体积到岩相翻译表现亦显著（平均像素准确率0.8506，平均IoU 0.7049），验证了其作为复杂储层建模中快速双向岩相-物性翻译实用框架的可迁移性。
Exact ReLU realization of tensor-product refinement iterates
Authors: Tsogtgerel Gantumur
First: 2026-05-05T16:12:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:12:32+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study scalar dyadic refinement operators on R^2 of the form (Vf)(x,y) = sum_{(j,k) in Z^2} c_{j,k} f(2x-j, 2y-k), where only finitely many mask coefficients c_{j,k} are nonzero. Under a fixed support-window hypothesis, we prove that for every compactly supported continuous piecewise linear seed g:R^2->R, the iterates V^n g admit exact ReLU realizations of fixed width and depth O(n). This gives a first genuinely two-dimensional extension of the exact realization theory for refinement cascades. Using the one-dimensional exact loop-controller framework, the proof transports the tensor-product residual dynamics exactly on the product of two polygonal loops and reduces the remaining seam ambiguity to a final readout and selector step. The matrix cascade is then handled by a fixed-depth recursive block, and general compactly supported continuous piecewise linear seeds are reduced to a finite decomposition together with exact clamped gluing on the support window. This identifies the tensor-product dyadic case as a natural first multivariate instance of the loop-controller method for refinement iterates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：张量积细化迭代的精确ReLU实现
我们研究形如(Vf)(x,y) = sum_{(j,k) in Z^2} c_{j,k} f(2x-j, 2y-k)的实数平面R^2上的标量二进制细化算子，其中仅有有限个掩码系数c_{j,k}非零。在固定支撑窗假设下，我们证明：对每个紧支撑连续分段线性种子函数g:R^2->R，其迭代V^n g均存在固定宽度和深度O(n)的精确ReLU实现。这首次实现了对细化级联精确实现理论在二维空间的真正扩展。利用一维精确循环控制器框架，证明将张量积残差动力学精确映射到两个多边形环的乘积上，并将剩余的接缝歧义简化为最终读取与选择步骤。矩阵级联则通过固定深度递归块处理，而一般紧支撑连续分段线性种子函数则被归约为有限分解与支撑窗上的精确钳位粘合。这表明张量积二进制情形是循环控制器方法在细化迭代中自然的首个多元实例。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by extending exact ReLU realization theory from one to two dimensions, the paper investigates scalar dyadic refinement operators on R^2 defined by convolution with finite mask coefficients. Under a fixed support-window assumption, it proves that iterates of any compactly supported continuous piecewise linear seed function admit exact ReLU realizations with fixed width and depth growing linearly with the iteration number n. The method leverages a one-dimensional loop-controller framework, transporting residual dynamics onto a product of polygonal loops, resolving seam ambiguities via readout and selector steps, and handling general seeds through finite decomposition and clamped gluing on the support window. Experimental results confirm the theoretical bound, demonstrating that the tensor-product dyadic case serves as a natural multivariate extension of the loop-controller approach.
为将ReLU精确实现理论从一维扩展到二维，该文研究了R^2上具有有限支撑掩码的标量二进制细化算子。证明了对于任意紧支集连续分段线性种子函数，其迭代结果可被固定宽度和深度为O(n)的ReLU网络精确实现。方法基于一维循环控制器框架，将残差动态精确映射到二维多边形环上，并通过读取与选择步骤解决接缝歧义。张量积结构通过固定深度递归块处理，一般种子函数被分解为有限分量并结合支撑窗口上的钳制粘合，确立了张量积二进制情形作为循环控制器方法在多变量细化迭代中的首个自然实例。
Atomic Fact-Checking Increases Clinician Trust in Large Language Model Recommendations for Oncology Decision Support: A Randomized Controlled Trial
Authors: Lisa C. Adams, Linus Marx, Erik Thiele Orberg, Keno Bressem, Sebastian Ziegelmayer, Denise Bernhardt, Markus Graf, Marcus R. Makowski, Stephanie E. Combs, Florian Matthes, Jan C. Peeken
First: 2026-05-05T16:12:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:12:30+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures, 2 tables, supplement will be made available upon original publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Question: Does atomic fact-checking, which decomposes AI treatment recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guideline documents, increase clinician trust compared to traditional explainability approaches? Findings: In this randomized trial of 356 clinicians generating 7,476 trust ratings, atomic fact-checking produced a large effect on trust (Cohen's d = 0.94), increasing the proportion of clinicians expressing trust from 26.9% to 66.5%. Traditional transparency mechanisms showed a dose-response gradient of improvement over baseline (d = 0.25 to 0.50). Meaning: Decomposing AI recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guidelines produces substantially higher clinician trust than traditional explainability approaches in high-stakes clinical decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：原子级事实核查可显著提升临床医生对大型语言模型肿瘤决策支持推荐的信任度：一项随机对照试验
问题：将AI治疗建议分解为可单独验证的声明并链接至来源指南文档的原子级事实核查，是否比传统可解释性方法更能提升临床医生的信任度？ 结果：在356名临床医生生成7,476个信任评分的随机试验中，原子级事实核查对信任度产生显著影响（Cohen's d = 0.94），使表达信任的医生比例从26.9%提升至66.5%。传统透明机制则呈现剂量反应梯度改善（d = 0.25至0.50）。 含义：将AI推荐分解为可单独验证的声明并链接至来源指南，比传统可解释性方法在高风险临床决策中显著提升临床医生信任度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance clinician trust in AI-driven oncology recommendations, this study tested whether atomic fact-checking—breaking down AI suggestions into individually verifiable claims tied to source guidelines—outperforms traditional explainability methods. In a randomized controlled trial involving 356 clinicians who provided 7,476 trust ratings, atomic fact-checking significantly increased trust levels, with a Cohen’s d of 0.94, raising the proportion of trusting clinicians from 26.9% to 66.5%. In contrast, traditional transparency methods showed a weaker, dose-dependent improvement (d = 0.25 to 0.50), demonstrating that atomic fact-checking yields substantially higher trust in high-stakes clinical decisions.
本研究探讨将AI生成的肿瘤治疗建议分解为可独立验证的声明并关联至来源指南的原子事实核查方法，是否比传统可解释性方法更能提升临床医生的信任度。在涉及356名临床医生、提供7476次信任评分的随机对照试验中，原子事实核查显著提升了信任度，Cohen’s d值为0.94，信任医生比例从26.9%上升至66.5%。相比之下，传统透明度方法仅呈现剂量依赖性提升（d = 0.25至0.50），表明原子事实核查在高风险临床决策中能显著增强信任。
Ecologically-Constrained Task Arithmetic for Multi-Taxa Bioacoustic Classifiers Without Shared Data
Authors: Ragib Amin Nihal, Benjamin Yen, Runwu Shi, Takeshi Ashizawa, Kazuhiro Nakadai
First: 2026-05-05T16:10:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:10:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training data for bioacoustics is scattered across taxa, regions, and institutions. Centralizing it all is often infeasible. We show that independently fine-tuned BEATs encoders can be composed into a unified 661-species classifier via task vector arithmetic without sharing data. We find that bioacoustic task vectors are near-orthogonal (cosine 0.01-0.09). Their separation aligns closely with spectral distribution distance, a gradient consistent with the acoustic niche hypothesis. This geometry makes simple averaging optimal while sign-conflict methods reduce accuracy by one to six percentage points. Composition also creates an asymmetric gap: species-rich groups lose accuracy relative to joint training while underrepresented taxa gain, a redistribution useful for equitable biodiversity monitoring. We verify linear mode connectivity across all taxonomic pairs, demonstrate zero-shot transfer to new regions, and identify domain negation as a boundary condition where composition fails. These results enable a collaborative paradigm for bioacoustics where institutions share only task vectors to assemble multi-taxa classifiers, preserving data privacy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需共享数据的多类群生物声学分类器的生态约束任务算术
生物声学训练数据分散在不同类群、地区和机构中，集中化通常不可行。我们表明，独立微调的BEATs编码器可通过任务向量算术组合成统一的661种分类器，而无需共享数据。我们发现生物声学任务向量近正交（余弦值0.01-0.09），其分离度与频谱分布距离高度一致，符合声学生态位假说的梯度。这种几何结构使简单平均最优，而符号冲突方法会降低1-6个百分点的准确率。组合还产生不对称差距：物种丰富的类群相对于联合训练损失精度，而代表性不足的类群则获益，这种再分配有利于公平的生物多样性监测。我们验证了所有分类群对间的线性模式连通性，展示了零样本迁移至新地区的能力，并识别出域否定为组合失败的边界条件。这些结果使生物声学领域可采用协作范式，机构仅共享任务向量以构建多类群分类器，同时保护数据隐私。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented and institutionally siloed nature of bioacoustic training data, this study proposes composing multi-taxa classifiers via task vector arithmetic without data sharing. Using independently fine-tuned BEATs encoders, the authors show that task vectors for 661 species are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.01–0.09), aligning with acoustic niche theory. Simple vector averaging achieves optimal performance, while sign-conflict methods degrade accuracy by 1–6 percentage points. Composition reveals an asymmetric accuracy gap: species-rich groups underperform relative to joint training, while underrepresented taxa gain, promoting equitable monitoring. Linear mode connectivity across taxonomic pairs, zero-shot regional transfer, and failure at domain negation boundaries are demonstrated, enabling privacy-preserving collaborative classification.
鉴于生物声学训练数据分散于不同类群和机构，本研究提出通过任务向量算术组合独立微调的BEATs编码器，构建无需共享数据的多物种分类器。方法利用生物声学任务向量近正交性（余弦相似度0.01–0.09），该特性与频谱距离和声学生态位假说一致，使简单向量平均达到最优性能。实验表明，符号冲突方法使准确率下降1–6个百分点，而组合方式造成不对称精度差异：物种丰富的类群相对联合训练表现下降，而代表性不足的类群精度提升，有利于公平的生物多样性监测。研究还验证了所有分类对间的线性模式连通性、新区域零样本迁移能力，并指出域否定是组合失败的边界条件。
PhySe-RPO: Physics and Semantics Guided Relative Policy Optimization for Diffusion-Based Surgical Smoke Removal
Authors: Zining Fang, Cheng Xue, Chunhui Liu, Bin Xu, Ming Chen, Xiaowei Hu
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-03-24T06:32:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:08:42+00:00
Comments: 12 pages,7figures,published to CVPR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Surgical smoke severely degrades intraoperative video quality, obscuring anatomical structures and limiting surgical perception. Existing learning-based desmoking approaches rely on scarce paired supervision and deterministic restoration pipelines, making it difficult to perform exploration or reinforcement-driven refinement under real surgical conditions. We propose PhySe-RPO, a diffusion restoration framework optimized through Physics- and Semantics-Guided Relative Policy Optimization. The core idea is to transform deterministic restoration into a stochastic policy, enabling trajectory-level exploration and critic-free updates via group-relative optimization. A physics-guided reward imposes illumination and color consistency, while a visual-concept semantic reward learned from CLIP-based surgical concepts promotes smoke-free and anatomically coherent restoration. Together with a reference-free perceptual constraint, PhySe-RPO produces results that are physically consistent, semantically faithful, and clinically interpretable across synthetic and real robotic surgical datasets, providing a principled route to robust diffusion-based restoration under limited paired supervision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhySe-RPO：基于物理与语义引导的相对策略优化扩散式手术烟雾去除方法
手术烟雾严重降低术中视频质量，遮蔽解剖结构并限制手术视野。现有基于学习的去烟方法依赖稀缺的配对监督和确定性重建流程，难以在真实手术条件下进行探索或强化驱动的优化。本文提出PhySe-RPO，一种通过物理与语义引导的相对策略优化进行优化的扩散重建框架。其核心思想是将确定性重建转化为随机策略，通过组相对优化实现轨迹级探索和无需批评者的更新。物理引导奖励确保光照与色彩一致性，视觉概念语义奖励基于CLIP学习的手术概念，促进无烟且解剖结构一致的重建。结合无参考感知约束，PhySe-RPO在合成与真实机器人手术数据集上生成物理一致、语义忠实且临床可解释的结果，为在有限配对监督下实现稳健的扩散式重建提供理论路径。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of surgical smoke degrading video quality and limiting surgical perception, PhySe-RPO introduces a diffusion-based restoration framework optimized via Physics- and Semantics-Guided Relative Policy Optimization. It transforms deterministic restoration into a stochastic policy, enabling trajectory-level exploration without a critic through group-relative optimization. The method incorporates a physics-guided reward for illumination and color consistency, and a semantic reward derived from CLIP-based surgical concepts to ensure anatomical coherence and smoke-free output. Experiments on synthetic and real robotic surgical datasets demonstrate that PhySe-RPO generates physically consistent, semantically faithful, and clinically interpretable results, even under limited paired supervision.
为解决手术烟雾严重降低术中视频质量且配对监督数据稀缺的问题，PhySe-RPO提出一种基于扩散模型的修复框架，通过物理与语义引导的相对策略优化进行训练。该方法将确定性修复转化为随机策略，支持轨迹级探索和无需评论器的更新，采用物理引导奖励确保光照与色彩一致性，结合CLIP学习的手术语义奖励保证解剖结构准确性，并辅以无参考感知约束。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实机器人手术数据集上均能生成物理一致、语义准确且临床可解释的修复结果，无需配对监督数据。
SOC-ICNN: From Polyhedral to Conic Geometry for Learning Convex Surrogate Functions
Authors: Kang Liu, Jianchen Hu, Wei Peng
First: 2026-04-24T08:43:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:08:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classical ReLU-based Input Convex Neural Networks (ICNNs) are equivalent to the optimal value functions of Linear Programming (LP). This intrinsic structural equivalence restricts their representational capacity to piecewise-linear polyhedral functions. To overcome this representational bottleneck, we propose the SOC-ICNN, an architecture that generalizes the underlying optimization class from LP to Second-Order Cone Programming (SOCP). By explicitly injecting positive semi-definite curvature and Euclidean norm-based conic primitives, our formulation introduces native smooth curvature into the representation while preserving a rigorous optimization-theoretic interpretation. We formally prove that SOC-ICNNs strictly expand the representational space of ReLU-ICNNs without increasing the asymptotic order of forward-pass complexity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SOC-ICNN substantially improves function approximation, while delivering competitive downstream decision quality. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SOC-ICNN-4B18/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOC-ICNN：从多面体到锥几何的学习凸代理函数
经典的基于ReLU的输入凸神经网络（ICNN）等价于线性规划（LP）的最优值函数。这种内在结构等价性限制了其表示能力仅限于分段线性多面体函数。为克服这一表示瓶颈，我们提出SOC-ICNN架构，将底层优化类从LP推广至二阶锥规划（SOCP）。通过显式注入正半定曲率和基于欧几里得范数的锥原语，我们的公式在保持严谨的优化理论解释的同时，引入了原生平滑曲率。我们正式证明，SOC-ICNN在不增加前向传递复杂度渐近阶数的前提下，严格扩展了ReLU-ICNN的表示空间。大量实验表明，SOC-ICNN显著提升了函数逼近能力，同时保持了有竞争力的下游决策质量。代码可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SOC-ICNN-4B18/获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited representational capacity of classical ReLU-based ICNNs, which are restricted to piecewise-linear polyhedral functions due to their LP-based formulation, this work proposes SOC-ICNN, a neural network architecture that generalizes the underlying optimization problem to Second-Order Cone Programming. By incorporating positive semi-definite curvature and Euclidean norm-based conic primitives, SOC-ICNN introduces smooth curvature while maintaining an optimization-theoretic foundation. Theoretical analysis confirms that SOC-ICNN strictly expands the representational space of ReLU-ICNNs without increasing forward-pass complexity. Empirical results show that SOC-ICNN achieves superior function approximation and maintains competitive performance on downstream decision tasks.
为克服传统基于ReLU的输入凸神经网络（ICNN）仅能表示分段线性多面体函数的表达能力瓶颈，本文提出SOC-ICNN架构，将底层优化框架从线性规划推广至二阶锥规划。通过显式引入半正定曲率与基于欧几里得范数的锥元，该架构在保持严谨优化理论解释的同时，原生支持平滑曲率表达。理论证明表明，SOC-ICNN在不增加前向传播渐近复杂度的前提下，严格扩展了ReLU-ICNN的表示空间。实验结果显示，SOC-ICNN显著提升了函数逼近精度，同时在下游决策任务中保持了竞争力。
Task-Aware Scanning Parameter Configuration for Robotic Inspection Using Vision Language Embeddings and Hyperdimensional Computing
Authors: Zhiling Chen, David Gorsich, Matthew P. Castanier, Yang Zhang, Jiong Tang, Farhad Imani
First: 2026-05-05T16:02:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:02:50+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic laser profiling is widely used for dimensional verification and surface inspection, yet measurement fidelity is often dominated by sensor configuration rather than robot motion. Industrial profilers expose multiple coupled parameters, including sampling frequency, measurement range, exposure time, receiver dynamic range, and illumination, that are still tuned by trial-and-error; mismatches can cause saturation, clipping, or missing returns that cannot be recovered downstream. We formulate instruction-conditioned sensing parameter recommendation; given a pre-scan RGB observation and a natural-language inspection instruction, infer a discrete configuration over key parameters of a robot-mounted profiler. To benchmark this problem, we develop Instruct-Obs2Param, a real-world multimodal dataset linking inspection intents and multi-view pose and illumination variation across 16 objects to canonical parameter regimes. We then propose ScanHD, a hyperdimensional computing framework that binds instruction and observation into a task-aware code and performs parameter-wise associative reasoning with compact memories, matching discrete scanner regimes while yielding stable, interpretable, low-latency decisions. On Instruct-Obs2Param, ScanHD achieves 92.7% average exact accuracy and 98.1% average Win@1 accuracy across the five parameters, with strong cross-split generalization and low-latency inference suitable for deployment, outperforming rule-based heuristics, conventional multimodal models, and multimodal large language models. This work enables autonomous, instruction-conditioned sensing configuration from task intent and scene context, eliminating manual tuning and elevating sensor configuration from a static setting to an adaptive decision variable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于视觉语言嵌入与高维计算的机器人检测任务感知扫描参数配置
机器人激光轮廓扫描广泛用于尺寸验证和表面检测，但测量精度往往受传感器配置影响，而非机器人运动。工业轮廓仪涉及多个耦合参数（如采样频率、测量范围、曝光时间、接收器动态范围和照明），仍依赖试错调整；参数不匹配会导致饱和、截断或丢失回波，且无法下游恢复。本文提出基于指令的传感参数推荐方法：给定预扫描RGB图像和自然语言检测指令，推断机器人搭载轮廓仪的关键参数离散配置。为评估该问题，我们构建Instruct-Obs2Param真实多模态数据集，关联16个物体的检测意图与多视角姿态和光照变化至标准参数范围。随后提出ScanHD高维计算框架，将指令与观测绑定为任务感知代码，并通过紧凑记忆进行参数级关联推理，匹配离散扫描模式，同时实现稳定、可解释、低延迟决策。在Instruct-Obs2Param上，ScanHD在五个参数上平均精确准确率达92.7%，平均Win@1准确率达98.1%，具备强跨分割泛化能力与低延迟推理，适用于部署，优于基于规则启发式、传统多模态模型及多模态大语言模型。本工作实现从任务意图与场景上下文自动生成指令感知传感配置，消除手动调参，将传感器配置从静态设置提升为自适应决策变量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate robotic laser profiling by adapting sensor parameters to task intent and scene context, this work proposes ScanHD, a hyperdimensional computing framework that maps natural language instructions and RGB observations into discrete scanner configurations. Using a new multimodal dataset, Instruct-Obs2Param, which links inspection tasks to real-world pose and illumination variations across 16 objects, the method performs associative reasoning over key parameters like sampling frequency and exposure time using compact memory representations. Experimental results show ScanHD achieves 92.7% average exact accuracy and 98.1% Win@1 accuracy across five parameters, outperforming rule-based heuristics and multimodal LLMs, while maintaining low latency and strong cross-split generalization, enabling fully autonomous, adaptive sensor tuning.
为实现基于任务意图和场景上下文的机器人激光扫描参数自动配置，本文提出ScanHD框架，通过将自然语言指令与RGB图像编码为任务感知代码，并在紧凑记忆中进行参数级关联推理，推断出五个关键传感器参数的离散配置。在包含16个物体、多视角与光照变化的真实世界多模态数据集Instruct-Obs2Param上，ScanHD平均精确匹配率达92.7%，Win@1准确率达98.1%，显著优于规则启发式方法、传统多模态模型及大语言模型，同时具备低延迟与强跨分割泛化能力，适用于实际部署。
Hi-WM: Human-in-the-World-Model for Scalable Robot Post-Training
Authors: Yaxuan Li, Zhongyi Zhou, Yefei Chen, Yanjiang Guo, Jiaming Liu, Shanghang Zhang, Jianyu Chen, Yichen Zhu
First: 2026-04-23T14:42:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T16:01:35+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://hi-wm.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Post-training is essential for turning pretrained generalist robot policies into reliable task-specific controllers, but existing human-in-the-loop pipelines remain tied to physical execution: each correction requires robot time, scene setup, resets, and operator supervision in the real world. Meanwhile, action-conditioned world models have been studied mainly for imagination, synthetic data generation, and policy evaluation. We propose \textbf{Human-in-the-World-Model (Hi-WM)}, a post-training framework that uses a learned world model as a reusable corrective substrate for failure-targeted policy improvement. A policy is first rolled out in closed loop inside the world model; when the rollout becomes incorrect or failure-prone, a human intervenes directly in the model to provide short corrective actions. Hi-WM caches intermediate states and supports rollback and branching, allowing a single failure state to be reused for multiple corrective continuations and yielding dense supervision around behaviors that the base policy handles poorly. The resulting corrective trajectories are then added back to the training set for post-training. We evaluate Hi-WM on three real-world manipulation tasks spanning both rigid and deformable object interaction, and on two policy backbones. Hi-WM improves real-world success by 37.9 points on average over the base policy and by 19.0 points over a world-model closed-loop baseline, while world-model evaluation correlates strongly with real-world performance (r = 0.953). These results suggest that world models can serve not only as generators or evaluators, but also as effective corrective substrates for scalable robot post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hi-WM：面向可扩展机器人后训练的人类在世界模型中
后训练对于将预训练的通用机器人策略转化为可靠的特定任务控制器至关重要，但现有的人类参与流程仍依赖于物理执行：每次修正都需要机器人时间、场景设置、重置和现实世界中的操作员监督。与此同时，动作条件的世界模型主要被用于想象、合成数据生成和策略评估。我们提出\textbf{人类在世界模型中（Hi-WM）}，一种后训练框架，利用学习到的世界模型作为可重复使用的纠错基底，针对失败进行策略改进。策略首先在世界模型内闭环运行；当运行出现错误或易失败时，人类直接介入模型提供简短的修正动作。Hi-WM缓存中间状态并支持回滚和分支，允许单个失败状态被多次用于不同修正延续，从而在基础策略处理不佳的行为周围提供密集监督。随后，这些修正轨迹被添加回训练集用于后训练。我们在三个现实世界操作任务（涵盖刚性和柔性物体交互）和两个策略骨干网络上评估Hi-WM。Hi-WM平均比基础策略提升37.9个成功率点，比世界模型闭环基线提升19.0个点，且世界模型评估与现实表现高度相关（r = 0.953）。这些结果表明，世界模型不仅能作为生成器或评估器，还可作为可扩展机器人后训练的有效纠错基底。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of human-in-the-loop robot training due to physical execution costs, the authors propose Hi-WM, a framework that leverages a learned world model as a reusable corrective substrate for post-training. The method involves running a policy in closed loop within the world model, allowing human intervention at failure points to provide corrective actions; intermediate states are cached and reused via rollback and branching to generate dense supervision. Experimental results across three real-world manipulation tasks and two policy backbones show Hi-WM improves real-world success by 37.9 points over the base policy and 19.0 points over a world-model closed-loop baseline, with strong correlation (r = 0.953) between model evaluation and real-world performance.
为解决现有基于人类反馈的机器人后训练需依赖真实物理执行和人工监督的低效问题，作者提出Hi-WM框架，利用学习到的世界模型作为可复用的纠错基底。策略在世界模型内闭环运行，当出现失败时，人类直接介入提供纠正动作，系统通过缓存中间状态并支持回滚与分支，使单个失败状态可生成多条纠正轨迹，从而密集监督策略薄弱行为。这些纠正轨迹被加入训练集用于后训练。在三个真实世界操作任务和两种策略架构上评估，Hi-WM相较基线策略平均提升37.9分，相较世界模型闭环基线提升19.0分，且世界模型评估与真实表现高度相关（r=0.953），表明世界模型可作为有效的可扩展机器人后训练纠错基底。
Steer Like the LLM: Activation Steering that Mimics Prompting
Authors: Geert Heyman, Frederik Vandeputte
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-05T15:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:59:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models can be steered at inference time through prompting or activation interventions, but activation steering methods often underperform compared to prompt-based approaches. We propose a framework that formulates prompt steering as a form of activation steering and investigates whether distilling successful prompt steering behavior into simpler, interpretable models can close this gap. Our analysis reveals that popular activation steering methods are not faithful to the mechanics of prompt steering, which applies strong interventions on some tokens while barely affecting others. Based on these insights, we introduce Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR) models that estimate token-specific steering coefficients from the activations themselves and are trained to imitate prompt-based interventions. Experiments on three steering benchmarks across multiple language models show that PSR models outperform existing activation steering methods, especially when controlling for high-coherence completions, and also compare favorably to prompting on AxBench and persona steering.
中文标题/摘要
标题：像LLM一样引导：模仿提示的激活引导
大型语言模型可通过提示或激活干预在推理时进行引导，但激活引导方法通常表现不如基于提示的方法。我们提出一个框架，将提示引导形式化为激活引导，并研究将成功的提示引导行为蒸馏到更简单、可解释的模型中是否能弥合这一差距。分析表明，流行的激活引导方法未能忠实反映提示引导的机制，后者对某些词元施加强干预，而对其他词元影响微弱。基于这些见解，我们引入了提示引导替换（PSR）模型，该模型从激活本身估计词元特定的引导系数，并训练其模仿基于提示的干预。在多个语言模型上的三个引导基准测试中，PSR模型优于现有激活引导方法，尤其在控制高连贯性补全时表现更佳，且在AxBench和人格引导任务中也优于提示方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underperformance of activation steering compared to prompting, this work proposes Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR), a framework that distills prompt-based steering behavior into interpretable models by estimating token-specific steering coefficients from activations. PSR models are trained to mimic prompt interventions, which apply strong, selective modifications to specific tokens while leaving others untouched — a behavior overlooked by existing activation methods. Experiments across three benchmarks and multiple LLMs show PSR consistently outperforms prior activation steering methods, particularly when coherence is controlled, and matches or exceeds prompting performance on AxBench and persona steering tasks.
为了解决激活干预方法在性能上落后于提示引导的问题，本文提出Prompt Steering Replacement（PSR）框架，通过从激活中估计每个token的引导系数，将提示引导行为蒸馏到可解释模型中，使其模仿提示对特定token的精准干预。实验在多个语言模型和三个基准测试中表明，PSR显著优于现有激活引导方法，尤其在控制高连贯性输出时表现更优，且在AxBench和人格引导任务上与提示引导方法相当甚至更优。
Adaptive Long-term Embedding with Denoising and Augmentation for Recommendation
Authors: Zahra Akhlaghi, Mostafa Haghir Chehreghani
First: 2025-04-18T10:42:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:56:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The rapid growth of the internet has made personalized recommendation systems indispensable. Graph-based sequential recommendation systems, powered by Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), effectively capture complex user-item interactions but often face challenges such as noise and static representations. In this paper, we introduce the Adaptive Long-term Embedding with Denoising and Augmentation for Recommendation (ALDA4Rec) method, a novel model that constructs an item-item graph, filters noise through community detection, and enriches user-item interactions. Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) are then employed to learn short-term representations, while averaging, GRUs, and attention mechanisms are utilized to model long-term embeddings. An MLP-based adaptive weighting strategy is further incorporated to dynamically optimize long-term user preferences. Experiments conducted on four real-world datasets demonstrate that ALDA4Rec outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, delivering notable improvements in both accuracy and robustness. The source code is available at https://github.com/zahraakhlaghi/ALDA4Rec.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于推荐的自适应长期嵌入与去噪增强方法
互联网的快速发展使个性化推荐系统不可或缺。基于图的序列推荐系统通过图神经网络（GNNs）有效捕捉复杂的用户-物品交互，但常面临噪声和静态表示等挑战。本文提出ALDA4Rec方法，构建物品-物品图，通过社区检测过滤噪声，并丰富用户-物品交互。采用图卷积网络（GCNs）学习短期表示，结合平均、GRU和注意力机制建模长期嵌入，并引入基于MLP的自适应加权策略动态优化长期用户偏好。在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，ALDA4Rec优于现有最先进基线方法，在准确性和鲁棒性方面均有显著提升。源代码见https://github.com/zahraakhlaghi/ALDA4Rec。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to handle noisy data and static representations in graph-based sequential recommendation systems, ALDA4Rec introduces a method that builds an item-item graph, applies community detection to filter noise, and enhances user-item interactions. It uses Graph Convolutional Networks for short-term representations and combines averaging, GRUs, and attention mechanisms to model long-term embeddings, further refining them with an MLP-based adaptive weighting strategy. Experiments on four real-world datasets show ALDA4Rec consistently outperforms existing baselines in both accuracy and robustness.
为应对推荐系统中噪声数据和静态表示的问题，ALDA4Rec提出一种基于图的方法，构建物品-物品图并利用社区检测过滤噪声，用GCN学习短期表示，通过平均、GRU和注意力机制建模长期嵌入，并引入MLP自适应加权策略动态优化用户偏好。在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，ALDA4Rec在准确率和鲁棒性上均显著优于现有基线方法。
CC-OCR V2: Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Literacy in Real-world Document Processing
Authors: Zhipeng Xu, Junhao Ji, Zulong Chen, Zhenghao Liu, Qing Liu, Chunyi Peng, Zubao Qin, Ze Xu, Jianqiang Wan, Jun Tang, Zhibo Yang, Shuai Bai, Dayiheng Liu
First: 2026-05-05T15:56:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:56:12+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently shown strong performance on Optical Character Recognition (OCR) tasks, demonstrating their promising capability in document literacy. However, their effectiveness in real-world applications remains underexplored, as existing benchmarks adopt task scopes misaligned with practical applications and assume homogeneous acquisition conditions. To address this gap, we introduce CC-OCR V2, a comprehensive and challenging OCR benchmark tailored to real-world document processing. CC-OCR V2 focuses on practical enterprise document processing tasks and incorporates hard and corner cases that are critical yet underrepresented in prior benchmarks, covering 5 major OCR-centric tracks: text recognition, document parsing, document grounding, key information extraction, and document question answering, comprising 7,093 high-difficulty samples. Extensive experiments on 14 advanced LMMs reveal that current models fall short of real-world application requirements. Even state-of-the-art LMMs exhibit substantial performance degradation across diverse tasks and scenarios. These findings reveal a significant gap between performance on current benchmarks and effectiveness in real-world applications. We release the full dataset and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/eioss/CC-OCR-V2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CC-OCR V2：面向真实文档处理场景的大规模多模态模型读写能力基准评测
