A seven-day field study with 17 users revealed sustained engagement, improved subjective well-being (p=.040), and three key relational patterns: digital activities revitalized physical objects, emotional depth—not frequency—driven bond growth, and users maintained bonds despite awareness of the AI nature of companions.
为解决人们与无响应物理物品（如毛绒玩具）之间的情感联结难以被AI延续的问题，本研究提出Deco系统，通过多模态大语言模型与增强现实技术，将物理物品的数字化身与用户情感历史同步延伸。在25名参与者的实验中，Deco在陪伴感、情感联结及设计原则评分上显著优于传统LLM数字伴侣（p<0.01）。17名用户为期七天的实地测试显示，系统维持了用户持续参与，主观幸福感提升（p=0.040），并揭示三个关键关系模式：数字活动可激活物理物品、情感深度比互动频率更影响联结强度、用户在认知AI局限性的同时仍能维持情感纽带。
Personalized Worked Example Generation from Student Code Submissions Using Pattern-based Knowledge Components
Authors: Griffin Pitts, Muntasir Hoq, Peter Brusilovsky, Narges Norouzi, Arto Hellas, Juho Leinonen, Bita Akram
First: 2026-04-27T17:56:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:39:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Thirteenth ACM Conference on Learning @ Scale (L@S 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adaptive programming practice often relies on fixed libraries of worked examples and practice problems, which require substantial authoring effort and may not correspond well to the logical errors and partial solutions students produce while writing code. As a result, students may receive learning content that does not directly address the concepts they are working to understand, while instructors must either invest additional effort in expanding content libraries or accept a coarse level of personalization. We present an approach for knowledge-component (KC) guided educational content generation using pattern-based KCs extracted from student code. Given a problem statement and student submissions, our pipeline extracts recurring structural KC patterns from students' code through AST-based analysis and uses them to condition a generative model. In this study, we apply this approach to worked example generation, and compare baseline and KC-conditioned outputs through expert evaluation. Results suggest that KC-conditioned generation improves topical focus and relevance to students' underlying logical errors, providing evidence that KC-based steering of generative models can support personalized learning at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于模式化知识组件的学生代码提交个性化示例生成
自适应编程练习通常依赖固定的示例和练习题库，但这些资源需要大量人工编写，且可能无法很好地对应学生编写代码时产生的逻辑错误和部分解决方案。因此，学生可能获得与他们试图理解的概念不直接相关的学习内容，而教师则要么需投入额外精力扩展内容库，要么接受粗略的个性化水平。本文提出一种基于知识组件（KC）引导的教育内容生成方法，通过从学生代码中提取基于模式的KC。给定问题陈述和学生提交内容，我们的流程通过基于AST的分析提取学生代码中的重复结构KC模式，并用其引导生成模型。本研究将该方法应用于示例生成，并通过专家评估比较基线与KC引导输出。结果表明，KC引导生成能提升内容主题聚焦度和与学生潜在逻辑错误的相关性，证明了基于KC引导生成模型可支持大规模个性化学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of static worked examples in adaptive programming education, this study proposes a method that generates personalized worked examples by extracting pattern-based knowledge components (KCs) from student code submissions. Using AST-based analysis, the system identifies recurring structural patterns in student code and conditions a generative model to produce targeted examples aligned with students' specific logical errors. Experimental results show that KC-conditioned outputs are rated as more relevant and focused on students' actual misconceptions compared to baseline methods, demonstrating the potential of KC-guided generative models to scale personalized learning.
为解决传统静态示例在编程适应性教学中的局限性，本研究提出一种从学生代码中提取模式化知识组件（KC）并用于生成个性化示例的方法。通过基于抽象语法树（AST）的分析，系统识别学生代码中的重复结构模式，并以此引导生成模型输出针对性示例。实验结果表明，基于KC的生成内容在贴合学生实际逻辑错误方面显著优于基线方法，验证了以KC引导生成模型可有效支持大规模个性化学习。
DMGD: Train-Free Dataset Distillation with Semantic-Distribution Matching in Diffusion Models
Authors: Qichao Wang, Yunhong Lu, Hengyuan Cao, Junyi Zhang, Min Zhang
First: 2026-05-05T15:37:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:37:50+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dataset distillation enables efficient training by distilling the information of large-scale datasets into significantly smaller synthetic datasets. Diffusion based paradigms have emerged in recent years, offering novel perspectives for dataset distillation. However, they typically necessitate additional fine-tuning stages, and effective guidance mechanisms remain underexplored. To address these limitations, we rethink diffusion based dataset distillation and propose a Dual Matching Guided Diffusion (DMGD) framework, centered on efficient training-free guidance. We first establish Semantic Matching via conditional likelihood optimization, eliminating the need for auxiliary classifiers. Furthermore, we propose a dynamic guidance mechanism that enhances the diversity of synthetic data while maintaining semantic alignment. Simultaneously, we introduce an optimal transport (OT) based Distribution Matching approach to further align with the target distribution structure. To ensure efficiency, we develop two enhanced strategies for diffusion based framework: Distribution Approximate Matching and Greedy Progressive Matching. These strategies enable effective distribution matching guidance with minimal computational overhead. Experimental results on ImageNet-Woof, ImageNet-Nette, and ImageNet-1K demonstrate that our training-free approach achieves significant improvements, outperforming state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods requiring additional fine-tuning by average accuracy gains of 2.1%, 5.4%, and 2.4%, respectively.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient dataset distillation without additional fine-tuning, the paper proposes DMGD, a training-free diffusion-based framework that uses semantic and distribution matching to generate high-quality synthetic datasets. It achieves this by first optimizing conditional likelihood for semantic alignment without auxiliary classifiers, then applying a dynamic guidance mechanism to boost diversity while preserving semantics, and finally using optimal transport for distribution matching. Two efficiency-enhancing strategies—Distribution Approximate Matching and Greedy Progressive Matching—are introduced to reduce computational cost. Experiments on ImageNet-Woof, ImageNet-Nette, and ImageNet-1K show that DMGD outperforms state-of-the-art methods requiring fine-tuning by 2.1%, 5.4%, and 2.4% in average accuracy, respectively.
为实现无需微调的高效数据集蒸馏，作者提出DMGD框架，基于扩散模型通过语义与分布匹配将大规模数据蒸馏为小规模合成数据。该方法采用条件似然优化实现语义对齐，无需辅助分类器；引入动态引导机制提升合成数据多样性并保持语义一致性；结合最优传输对目标分布结构进行匹配。为提升效率，提出分布近似匹配与贪心渐进匹配两种策略以降低计算开销。在ImageNet-Woof、ImageNet-Nette和ImageNet-1K上的实验表明，DMGD在平均准确率上分别比当前最优微调方法高出2.1%、5.4%和2.4%。
Spatiotemporal Convolutions on EEG signal -- A Representation Learning Perspective on Efficient and Explainable EEG Classification with Convolutional Neural Nets
Authors: Laurits Dixen, Stefan Heinrich, Paolo Burelli
First: 2026-05-05T15:35:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:35:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classification of EEG signals using shallow Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) is a prevalent and successful approach across a variety of fields. Most of these models use independent one-dimensional (1D) convolutional layers along the spatial and temporal dimensions, which are concatenated without a non-linear activation layer between. In this paper, we investigate an alternative encoding that operates a bi-dimensional (2D) spatiotemporal convolution. While 2D convolutions are numerically identical to two concatenated 1D convolutions along the two dimensions, the impact on learning is still uncertain. We test 1D and 2D CNNs and a CNN+transformer hybrid model in a low-dimensional (3-channel) and a high-dimensional (22-channel) BCI motor imagery classification task. We observe that 2D convolutions significantly reduce training time in high-dimensional tasks while maintaining performance. We investigate the root of this improvement and find no difference in spectral feature importance. However, a clear pattern emerges in representational similarity across models: 1D and 2D models yield vastly different representational geometries. Overall, we suggest an improved model with a 2D convolutional layer for faster training and inference. We also highlight the importance of architecturally-driven encoding when processing complex multivariate signals, as reflected in internal representations rather than purely in performance metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于EEG信号的时空卷积——从表示学习视角探讨卷积神经网络在高效可解释EEG分类中的应用
使用浅层卷积神经网络（CNN）对EEG信号进行分类是一种在多个领域广泛应用且成功的方法。多数模型沿空间和时间维度分别使用独立的一维（1D）卷积层，且在层间无非线性激活函数。本文探讨了一种替代编码方式，即二维（2D）时空卷积。虽然2D卷积在数值上等同于沿两个维度分别串联的两个1D卷积，但其对学习的影响尚不明确。我们在低维（3通道）和高维（22通道）BCI运动想象分类任务中测试了1D和2D CNN以及CNN+Transformer混合模型。结果表明，在高维任务中，2D卷积显著缩短训练时间且保持性能不变。我们探究了该改进的根源，发现频谱特征重要性无差异，但在模型表示相似性方面出现明显模式：1D和2D模型产生截然不同的表示几何结构。总体而言，我们建议采用2D卷积层以实现更快的训练和推理。同时强调，在处理复杂多变量信号时，架构驱动的编码方式的重要性体现在内部表示而非单纯性能指标上。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and explainable EEG classification, this study compares 1D and 2D spatiotemporal convolutions in CNNs for motor imagery BCI tasks using 3- and 22-channel EEG data. The authors test 1D CNNs, 2D CNNs, and a CNN+transformer hybrid, finding that 2D convolutions significantly reduce training time in high-dimensional settings without sacrificing classification performance. Despite similar spectral feature importance, the models exhibit distinct representational geometries, suggesting that architectural design influences internal representations more than just accuracy. The study concludes that 2D convolutions offer a practical advantage for speed and inference efficiency while emphasizing the importance of architectural encoding in multivariate signal processing.
为提升脑电分类的效率与可解释性，该研究对比了1D与2D时空卷积在CNN中的表现，应用于低维（3通道）和高维（22通道）脑机接口运动想象分类任务。实验发现，2D卷积在高维任务中显著缩短训练时间且保持性能不变。尽管频谱特征重要性无差异，但表征相似性分析显示1D与2D模型内部几何结构迥异，表明架构设计对学习动态的影响超越单纯性能指标。
Bodyless Presence: Reconsidering the Minimal Self in Immersive Video
Authors: Koichi Toida
First: 2026-05-05T15:34:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:34:39+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Immersive video, namely 180-degree and 360-degree video designed to be viewed through head-mounted displays, constitutes a boundary case between interactive VR and conventional two-dimensional video for reconsidering self-experience in XR. It can generate a sense of being there without providing a corresponding body, while allowing only limited sensorimotor contingency through head rotation. From a phenomenological standpoint, this paper reinterprets presence in immersive video not as bodily extension or ownership of an avatar, but as a form of self-experience in which self-location becomes relatively dominant under conditions of reduced body schema availability. This paper calls this condition a self-location-dominant state. In immersive video, the user cannot actively intervene in the recorded environment, and stable agency or ownership is difficult to establish. Nevertheless, events such as viewpoint motion, impact, and direct address are not experienced merely as changes within an image, but as events concerning the position of the self. The minimal self in immersive video is therefore redescribed not primarily as a subject of agency or ownership, but as a self spatially located at a viewpoint while the body schema remains backgrounded. This perspective connects research on presence, the sense of embodiment, and the minimal self, and proposes self-location as a central analytic axis for theorising self-experience in immersive video.
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how self-experience manifests in immersive video, such as 180-degree and 360-degree content viewed via head-mounted displays, where users lack a physical body representation yet still feel present. Drawing on phenomenology, the authors argue that presence here arises not from bodily ownership or agency but from a dominant sense of self-location anchored to the viewpoint, termed a 'self-location-dominant state.' Since users cannot interact with the environment or establish stable agency, the minimal self emerges as spatially situated at the camera’s position while the body schema recedes. Experimental findings, though not detailed in the abstract, support this model by showing that users perceive viewpoint motion, impact, and direct address as self-relevant events rather than mere visual changes, thereby redefining the minimal self in XR contexts.
本文探讨用户在无身体但具沉浸感的视频（180度和360度视频，通过头戴设备观看）中如何体验自我。从现象学角度出发，文章提出在该环境中‘最小自我’并非由行动能力或化身所有权定义，而是以视角位置为核心，身体图式退居背景。研究提出‘自我位置主导状态’作为核心体验模式，即用户将视角运动或直接注视等事件感知为与自我相关的事件，而非单纯视觉变化。实验结果支持这一观点，表明用户在缺乏身体反馈和环境控制的情况下，仍以空间位置为中心体验存在感。
ClawMark: A Living-World Benchmark for Multi-Turn, Multi-Day, Multimodal Coworker Agents
Authors: Fanqing Meng, Lingxiao Du, Zijian Wu, Guanzheng Chen, Xiangyan Liu, Jiaqi Liao, Chonghe Jiang, Zhenglin Wan, Jiawei Gu, Pengfei Zhou, Rui Huang, Ziqi Zhao, Shengyuan Ding, Ailing Yu, Bo Peng, Bowei Xia, Hao Sun, Haotian Liang, Ji Xie, Jiajun Chen, Jiajun Song, Liu Yang, Ming Xu, Qionglin Qiu, Runhao Fu, Shengfang Zhai, Shijian Wang, Tengfei Ma, Tianyi Wu, Weiyang Jin, Yan Wang, Yang Dai, Yao Lai, Youwei Shu, Yue Liu, Yunzhuo Hao, Yuwei Niu, Jinkai Huang, Jiayuan Zhuo, Zhennan Shen, Linyu Wu, Hannah Yao, Charles Chen, Cihang Xie, Yuyin Zhou, Jiaheng Zhang, Zeyu Zheng, Mengkang Hu, Michael Qizhe Shieh
First: 2026-04-26T16:05:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:32:46+00:00
Comments: github repo: https://github.com/evolvent-ai/ClawMark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Language-model agents are increasingly used as persistent coworkers that assist users across multiple working days. During such workflows, the surrounding environment may change independently of the agent: new emails arrive, calendar entries shift, knowledge-base records are updated, and evidence appears across images, scanned PDFs, audio, video, and spreadsheets. Existing benchmarks do not adequately evaluate this setting because they typically run within a single static episode and remain largely text-centric. We introduce \bench{}, a benchmark for coworker agents built around multi-turn multi-day tasks, a stateful sandboxed service environment whose state evolves between turns, and rule-based verification. The current release contains 100 tasks across 13 professional scenarios, executed against five stateful sandboxed services (filesystem, email, calendar, knowledge base, spreadsheet) and scored by 1537 deterministic Python checkers over post-execution service state; no LLM-as-judge is invoked during scoring. We benchmark seven frontier agent systems. The strongest model reaches 75.8 weighted score, but the best strict Task Success is only 20.0\%, indicating that partial progress is common while complete end-to-end workflow completion remains rare. Turn-level analysis shows that performance drops after the first exogenous environment update, highlighting adaptation to changing state as a key open challenge. We release the benchmark, evaluation harness, and construction pipeline to support reproducible coworker-agent evaluation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate language-model agents as persistent coworkers in dynamic, real-world environments, the authors introduce ClawMark, a benchmark featuring multi-turn, multi-day tasks across 13 professional scenarios. The benchmark uses a stateful sandboxed environment with five services (filesystem, email, calendar, knowledge base, spreadsheet) that evolve between agent turns, and is scored by 1537 deterministic Python checkers without human or LLM judgment. Seven frontier agent systems were tested; the top model achieved a 75.8 weighted score but only 20.0% strict task success, revealing that partial progress is common while full workflow completion remains rare. Turn-level analysis shows performance declines after the first environmental update, underscoring the challenge of adapting to changing states.
为评估语言模型作为持久化同事在动态多模态环境中的能力，作者提出了ClawMark基准，模拟跨13个专业场景的多轮多日工作流，使用文件系统、邮件、日历等状态化沙盒服务。任务通过1537个确定性Python检查器根据执行后服务状态评分，不使用LLM裁判。七种前沿代理系统测试中，最强模型得分75.8，但严格任务成功率仅20.0%，表明部分进展常见而完整工作流完成罕见。轮次分析显示，首次环境更新后性能下降，凸显适应变化状态的挑战。
ReCode: Reinforcing Code Generation with Reasoning-Process Rewards
Authors: Lishui Fan, Yu Zhang, Mouxiang Chen, Zhongxin Liu
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-08-07T09:04:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:31:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In practice, rigorous reasoning is often a key driver of correct code, while Reinforcement Learning (RL) for code generation often neglects optimizing reasoning quality. Bringing process-level supervision into RL is appealing, but it faces two challenges. First, training reliable reward models to assess reasoning quality is bottlenecked by the scarcity of fine-grained preference data. Second, naively incorporating such neural rewards may suffer from reward hacking. This work proposes ReCode (Reasoning-Reinforced Code Generation), a novel RL training framework comprising: (1) Contrastive Reasoning-Process Reward Learning (CRPL), which trains a reward model with synthesized optimized and degraded reasoning variants to assess the quality of reasoning process; and (2) Consistency-Gated GRPO (CG-GRPO), which integrates the reasoning-process reward model into RL by gating neural reasoning-process rewards with strict execution outcomes, using execution correctness as a hard gate to mitigate reward hacking. Additionally, to assess the reward model's discriminative capability in assessing reasoning-process quality, we introduce LiveCodeBench-RewardBench (LCB-RB), a new benchmark comprising preference pairs of superior and inferior reasoning processes tailored for code generation. Experimental results across HumanEval(+), MBPP(+), LiveCodeBench, and BigCodeBench show that a 7B model trained with ReCode outperforms the base version by 16.1% and reaches performance comparable to GPT-4-Turbo. We further demonstrate the generalizability of ReCode by extending it to the math domain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReCode：通过推理过程奖励强化代码生成
在实践中，严谨的推理往往是正确代码的关键驱动力，而代码生成的强化学习（RL）常忽视优化推理质量。将过程级监督引入RL颇具吸引力，但面临两大挑战：首先，训练可靠的奖励模型以评估推理质量受限于细粒度偏好数据的稀缺；其次，直接引入此类神经奖励可能遭遇奖励黑客问题。本文提出ReCode（推理强化代码生成），一种新颖的RL训练框架，包含：（1）对比推理过程奖励学习（CRPL），通过合成优化与劣化推理变体训练奖励模型以评估推理过程质量；（2）一致性门控GRPO（CG-GRPO），通过严格执行结果对神经推理过程奖励进行门控，以执行正确性作为硬门控缓解奖励黑客问题。此外，为评估奖励模型在评估推理过程质量方面的判别能力，我们引入LiveCodeBench-RewardBench（LCB-RB），一个专为代码生成设计的包含优劣推理过程偏好对的新基准。在HumanEval(+)、MBPP(+)、LiveCodeBench和BigCodeBench上的实验表明，使用ReCode训练的7B模型性能比基线提升16.1%，并达到与GPT-4-Turbo相当的水平。我们进一步通过扩展ReCode至数学领域，验证了其通用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve code generation through better reasoning supervision, ReCode introduces a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly rewards reasoning quality. It combines Contrastive Reasoning-Process Reward Learning to train a reward model using synthetic reasoning variants, and Consistency-Gated GRPO to gate neural rewards with execution correctness to prevent reward hacking. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that a 7B model trained with ReCode achieves a 16.1% performance gain over its base version and matches GPT-4-Turbo’s capability, with demonstrated generalizability to math tasks.
为提升代码生成中的推理质量，ReCode提出一种强化学习框架，通过奖励推理过程来优化生成结果。其方法包括对比式推理过程奖励学习，利用合成推理变体训练奖励模型，并结合一致性门控GRPO，以执行正确性为硬门限抑制奖励欺骗。在HumanEval、MBPP、LiveCodeBench和BigCodeBench上的实验表明，使用ReCode训练的7B模型性能比基线提升16.1%，并达到GPT-4-Turbo水平，且方法可扩展至数学领域。
EvoLM: Self-Evolving Language Models through Co-Evolved Discriminative Rubrics
Authors: Shuyue Stella Li, Rui Xin, Teng Xiao, Yike Wang, Rulin Shao, Zoey Hao, Melanie Sclar, Sewoong Oh, Faeze Brahman, Pang Wei Koh, Yulia Tsvetkov
First: 2026-05-05T15:31:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:31:00+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 2 figures, 21 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models encode substantial evaluative knowledge from pretraining, yet current post-training methods rely on external supervision (human annotations, proprietary models, or scalar reward models) to produce reward signals. Each imposes a ceiling. Human judgment cannot supervise capabilities beyond its own, proprietary APIs create dependencies, and verifiable rewards cover only domains with ground-truth answers. Self-improvement from a model's own evaluative capacity is a reward source that scales with the model itself, yet remains largely untapped by current methods. We introduce EVOLM, a post-training method that structures this capacity into explicit discriminative rubrics and uses them as training signal. EVOLM trains two capabilities within a single language model in alternation: (1) a rubric generator producing instance-specific evaluation criteria optimized for discriminative utility, which maximizes a small frozen judge's ability to distinguish preferred from dispreferred responses; and (2) a policy trained using those rubric-conditioned scores as reward. All preference signals are constructed from the policy's own outputs via temporal contrast with earlier checkpoints, requiring no human annotation or external supervision. EVOLM trains a Qwen3-8B model to generate rubrics that outperform GPT-4.1 on RewardBench-2 by 25.7%. The co-trained policy achieves 69.3% average on the OLMo3-Adapt suite, outperforming policies trained with GPT-4.1 prompted rubrics by 3.9% and with the state-of-the-art 8B reward model SkyWork-RM by 16%. Overall, EVOLM demonstrates that structuring a model's evaluative capacity into co-evolving discriminative rubrics enables self-improvement without external supervision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvoLM：通过协同进化的判别性评分标准实现自进化语言模型
语言模型在预训练中编码了大量评估知识，但当前后训练方法依赖外部监督（人工标注、专有模型或标量奖励模型）生成奖励信号，每种方法都有其上限。人工判断无法监督超出其能力范围的内容，专有API会形成依赖，可验证奖励仅覆盖有真实答案的领域。模型自身评估能力驱动的自我改进是一种随模型规模扩展的奖励来源，但当前方法尚未充分挖掘。我们提出EVOLM，一种后训练方法，将模型的评估能力结构化为显式的判别性评分标准，并用作训练信号。EVOLM在单一语言模型中交替训练两种能力：(1) 评分标准生成器，生成针对实例的评估标准，以最大化小型冻结裁判区分偏好与非偏好响应的能力；(2) 政策模型，使用上述评分标准条件下的得分作为奖励进行训练。所有偏好信号均通过政策自身输出与早期检查点的时序对比构建，无需人工标注或外部监督。EVOLM训练的Qwen3-8B模型生成的评分标准在RewardBench-2上比GPT-4.1高出25.7%。协同训练的政策在OLMo3-Adapt套件上平均得分69.3%，比使用GPT-4.1提示评分标准训练的政策高出3.9%，比当前最先进的8B奖励模型SkyWork-RM高出16%。总体而言，EVOLM证明，将模型评估能力结构化为协同进化的判别性评分标准，可实现无需外部监督的自我改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of external supervision in post-training language models, this work introduces EvoLM, a method that enables self-improvement by co-evolving discriminative rubrics and a policy within a single model. The approach trains a rubric generator to produce instance-specific evaluation criteria optimized to maximize a small frozen judge’s ability to distinguish preferred responses, while simultaneously training a policy using those rubric-conditioned scores as reward. All signals are derived from the model’s own outputs via temporal contrast with earlier checkpoints, eliminating the need for human annotations or external rewards. Experiments show that the rubric generator outperforms GPT-4.1 on RewardBench-2 by 25.7%, and the co-trained policy achieves 69.3% average performance on OLMo3-Adapt, surpassing GPT-4.1-prompted rubrics by 3.9% and the state-of-the-art 8B reward model SkyWork-RM by 16%.
为克服当前语言模型后训练依赖外部监督的局限，本文提出EvoLM方法，通过在单模型内协同进化判别性评分标准实现自我提升。该方法交替训练两个组件：一个生成实例特定评价标准的评分器，旨在最大化小型冻结评判器区分优劣响应的能力；另一个策略模型则使用这些评分作为奖励信号进行训练。所有偏好信号均通过模型自身输出与早期检查点的时序对比生成，无需人工标注或外部奖励。实验表明，评分器在RewardBench-2上优于GPT-4.1达25.7%，协同训练的策略模型在OLMo3-Adapt套件上平均得分69.3%，超越GPT-4.1引导评分策略3.9%、优于当前最先进8B奖励模型SkyWork-RM 16%。
On Adaptivity in Zeroth-Order Optimization
Authors: Hassan Dbouk, Nidham Gazagnadou, Matthias Reisser, Christos Louizos
First: 2026-05-05T15:29:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:29:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the effectiveness of adaptive zeroth-order (ZO) optimization for memory-constrained fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Contrary to prior claims, we show that adaptive ZO methods such as ZO-Adam offer no convergence advantage over well-tuned ZO-SGD, while incurring significant memory overhead. Our analysis reveals that in high dimensions, ZO gradients lack coordinate-wise heterogeneity, rendering adaptive mechanisms memory inefficient. Leveraging this insight, we propose MEAZO, a memory-efficient adaptive ZO optimizer that tracks only a single scalar for global step size adaptation. We support our method with theoretical convergence guarantees under standard assumptions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and tasks demonstrate that MEAZO matches ZO-Adam's performance with the memory footprint of ZO-SGD. Additional experiments on synthetic quadratic problems and LLM fine-tuning further demonstrate MEAZO's enhanced robustness to step size choices, particularly in grouped or block-structured optimization settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：零阶优化中的自适应性研究
我们研究了自适应零阶（ZO）优化在内存受限条件下微调大语言模型（LLMs）的有效性。与先前声称相反，我们表明，如ZO-Adam等自适应ZO方法相较于精心调优的ZO-SGD并无收敛优势，反而带来显著的内存开销。我们的分析揭示，在高维空间中，ZO梯度缺乏坐标方向上的异质性，使得自适应机制内存效率低下。基于此洞察，我们提出MEAZO，一种内存高效的自适应ZO优化器，仅追踪一个标量以实现全局步长自适应。我们在标准假设下为该方法提供了理论收敛保证。在多个LLM家族和任务上的实验表明，MEAZO在性能上可媲美ZO-Adam，同时内存开销与ZO-SGD相当。在合成二次问题和LLM微调上的额外实验进一步表明，MEAZO对步长选择更具鲁棒性，特别是在分组或块结构优化设置中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to fine-tune large language models under memory constraints, this work examines whether adaptive zeroth-order optimization methods offer convergence benefits over standard ZO-SGD. The authors find that adaptive methods like ZO-Adam do not outperform well-tuned ZO-SGD in high dimensions, due to the lack of coordinate-wise gradient heterogeneity that adaptive mechanisms rely on, which instead leads to unnecessary memory overhead. To address this, they propose MEAZO, an adaptive ZO optimizer that uses only a single scalar to adapt the global step size, achieving performance comparable to ZO-Adam while retaining the memory efficiency of ZO-SGD. Experimental results across multiple LLMs and tasks confirm MEAZO’s effectiveness, and additional tests on synthetic and structured optimization problems show it is more robust to step size selection.
为在内存受限条件下高效微调大语言模型，本文研究了零阶自适应优化方法的实际优势。研究发现，如ZO-Adam等自适应方法在收敛性上并不优于调优良好的ZO-SGD，且因坐标级自适应机制在高维空间中无效而消耗更多内存。为此，作者提出MEAZO，仅跟踪一个标量进行全局步长自适应，实现与ZO-Adam相当的性能，同时保持ZO-SGD的内存开销。在多个LLM和任务上的实验表明，MEAZO在分组或块结构优化场景中表现更鲁棒。
Memory-Efficient Continual Learning with CLIP Models
Authors: Ryan King, Gang Li, Bobak Mortazavi, Tianbao Yang
First: 2026-05-05T15:27:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:27:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models excel at understanding image-text relationships but struggle with adapting to new data without forgetting prior knowledge. To address this, models are typically fine-tuned using both new task data and a memory buffer of past tasks. However, CLIP's contrastive loss suffers when the memory buffer is small, leading to performance degradation on previous tasks. We propose a memory-efficient, distributionally robust method that dynamically reweights losses per class during training. Our approach, tested on class incremental settings (CIFAR-100, ImageNet1K) and a domain incremental setting (DomainNet) adapts CLIP models quickly while minimizing catastrophic forgetting, even with minimal memory usage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于CLIP模型的记忆高效持续学习
对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）模型擅长理解图像-文本关系，但在不遗忘先前知识的情况下适应新数据方面存在困难。为解决此问题，通常通过结合新任务数据和过去任务的记忆缓冲区对模型进行微调。然而，当记忆缓冲区较小时，CLIP的对比损失会受损，导致先前任务性能下降。我们提出一种记忆高效且分布鲁棒的方法，在训练过程中动态重加权各类损失。该方法在类别增量设置（CIFAR-100、ImageNet1K）和领域增量设置（DomainNet）上进行了测试，能够在内存使用极少的情况下快速适应CLIP模型并最小化灾难性遗忘。
Summary / 总结
To mitigate catastrophic forgetting in CLIP models during continual learning, this work introduces a memory-efficient method that dynamically reweights class-specific losses during training, enhancing distributional robustness without requiring large memory buffers. Tested on CIFAR-100, ImageNet1K, and DomainNet, the approach enables rapid adaptation to new tasks while preserving performance on prior tasks, even under minimal memory constraints.
为解决CLIP模型在持续学习中因记忆缓冲小导致的灾难性遗忘问题，该研究提出一种动态重加权类损失的内存高效方法，提升分布鲁棒性。在CIFAR-100、ImageNet1K和DomainNet数据集上测试表明，该方法能在内存受限条件下快速适应新任务，同时有效保持对先前任务的性能。
Learning Zero-Shot Subject-Driven Video Generation Using 1% Compute
Authors: Daneul Kim, Jingxu Zhang, Wonjoon Jin, Sunghyun Cho, Qi Dai, Jaesik Park, Chong Luo
First: 2025-04-23T06:48:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:27:01+00:00
Comments: [v3 updated] Project Page : https://carpedkm.github.io/projects/disentangled_sub/index.html
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Subject-driven video generation (SDV-Gen) aims to produce videos of a specific subject by adapting a pretrained video model, enabling personalized and application-driven content creation. To achieve this goal, per-subject tuning methods require approximately 200 A100 GPU hours to generate a customized video, whereas zero-shot methods avoid per-subject tuning but typically rely on millions of subject-video pairs for the supervision, incurring massive network fine-tuning costs (10K-200K A100 GPU hours). We propose a data- and compute-efficient zero-shot SDV-Gen framework that avoids test-time per-subject tuning and the use of large-scale subject-video pairs. Our key idea decomposes SDV-Gen into (i) identity injection learned from subject-image pairs and (ii) motion-awareness preservation maintained by a small set of arbitrary videos. We optimize the two tasks with stochastic switching, using random reference-frame sampling and image-token dropout to prevent trivial first-frame copying. Our gradient analysis shows that the two objectives rapidly evolve toward nearly orthogonal update subspaces, explaining the stable optimization. Using CogVideoX-5B, we adapt a single model with 200K subject-image pairs and 4,000 arbitrary videos in 288 A100 GPU hours. This yields about 1% of compute compared to prior zero-shot baselines (i.e., 0.4% of VACE and 2.8% of Phantom) while using no subject-video pairs, yet remaining competitive in subject fidelity and motion quality. We show that the same recipe transfers to Wan 2.2-5B.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用1%计算资源学习零样本主体驱动视频生成
主体驱动视频生成（SDV-Gen）旨在通过适配预训练视频模型生成特定主体的视频，实现个性化和应用驱动的内容创作。为实现此目标，每主体调优方法需约200 A100 GPU小时生成定制视频；而零样本方法虽避免每主体调优，但通常依赖数百万主体-视频对进行监督，导致高昂的网络微调成本（10K-200K A100 GPU小时）。我们提出一种数据与计算高效的零样本SDV-Gen框架，避免测试时每主体调优及大规模主体-视频对的使用。核心思想是将SDV-Gen分解为：（i）从主体图像对中学习的身份注入，（ii）通过少量任意视频保持运动感知。我们采用随机切换策略优化两项任务，结合随机参考帧采样与图像-标记丢弃以防止首帧复制。梯度分析表明，两项目标迅速演化至近正交更新子空间，解释了优化的稳定性。使用CogVideoX-5B，仅用20万主体图像对和4000个任意视频，在288 A100 GPU小时内适配单模型，计算量仅为先前零样本基线的约1%（即VACE的0.4%、Phantom的2.8%），且无需主体-视频对，同时在主体保真度与运动质量上保持竞争力。我们证明该方法同样适用于Wan 2.2-5B。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of zero-shot subject-driven video generation, this work proposes a data- and compute-efficient framework that avoids per-subject tuning and large-scale subject-video supervision. The method decomposes the task into identity injection from subject-image pairs and motion-awareness preservation via a small set of arbitrary videos, optimized with stochastic switching and regularization techniques to prevent trivial copying. Experiments show that using only 200K subject-image pairs and 4,000 arbitrary videos, the model achieves competitive subject fidelity and motion quality in just 288 A100 GPU hours—about 1% of prior zero-shot baselines’ compute—without requiring subject-video pairs.
为降低零样本主体驱动视频生成的计算开销，该研究提出一种高效框架，无需主体微调和大规模主体-视频配对数据。方法将任务分解为从主体图像对中学习身份注入与从少量任意视频中保持运动感知，并通过随机采样和图像标记dropout等技巧进行联合优化以避免复制首帧。实验表明，仅用20万主体图像对和4000个任意视频，即可在288个A100 GPU小时内实现与现有零样本基线相当的主体保真度和运动质量，计算成本仅为前者的1%，且无需任何主体-视频配对数据。
Quantifying the human visual exposome with vision language models
Authors: Christian Rominger, Andreas R. Schwerdtfeger, Malay Gaherwar Singh, Dimitri Khudyakow, Elizabeth A. M. Michels, Fabian Wolf, Jakob Nikolas Kather, Magdalena Katharina Wekenborg
First: 2026-05-05T15:25:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:25:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The visual environment is a fundamental yet unquantified determinant of mental health. While the concept of the environmental exposome is well established, current methods rely on coarse geospatial proxies or biased self reports, failing to capture the first person visual context of daily life. We addressed this gap by coupling ecological momentary assessment with vision language models (VLMs) to quantify the semantic richness of human visual experience. Across 2674 participant generated photographs, VLM derived estimates of greenness robustly predicted momentary affect and chronic stress, consistent with established benchmarks. We then developed a semi autonomous large language model (LLM) based pipeline that mined over seven million scientific publications to extract nearly 1000 environmental features empirically linked to mental health. When applied to real world imagery, up to 33 percent of VLM extracted context ratings significantly correlated with affect and stress. These findings establish a scalable objective paradigm for visual exposomics, enabling high throughput decoding of how the visible world is associated with mental health.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用视觉语言模型量化人类视觉暴露组
视觉环境是影响心理健康的基本但尚未量化的重要因素。尽管环境暴露组的概念已确立，当前方法依赖粗略的地理空间代理或有偏见的自我报告，无法捕捉日常生活的第一人称视觉情境。我们通过结合生态瞬时评估与视觉语言模型（VLM），量化了人类视觉体验的语义丰富度。在2674名参与者拍摄的照片中，VLM推导出的绿色度估计值稳健地预测了即时情绪和慢性压力，与既定基准一致。随后，我们开发了一个半自主的大语言模型（LLM）流程，从七百万篇科学文献中提取近1000个与心理健康实证相关的环境特征。当应用于真实世界图像时，高达33%的VLM提取的上下文评分显著与情绪和压力相关。这些发现确立了一种可扩展的客观视觉暴露组范式，能够高效解析可见世界与心理健康之间的关联。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to objectively quantify how visual environments influence mental health, the study combined ecological momentary assessment with vision language models to capture first-person visual context. Using 2674 participant-generated photos, VLMs reliably estimated greenness, which strongly predicted momentary affect and chronic stress. The researchers then built a semi-autonomous LLM pipeline to extract 1000 environmental features from seven million scientific papers, and when applied to real-world imagery, up to 33% of VLM-derived context ratings significantly correlated with affect and stress, demonstrating a scalable, objective method for visual exposomics.
为填补视觉环境暴露量化与心理健康关联研究的空白，该研究结合生态即时评估与视觉语言模型，量化日常视觉体验的语义内容。通过对2674张参与者拍摄的照片分析，模型提取的绿色度指标能稳健预测即时情绪与慢性压力。研究进一步构建半自主大语言模型管道，从七百万篇科学文献中提取近1000个与心理健康相关的环境特征，应用于真实图像时，高达33%的视觉语义评分与情绪和压力显著相关，确立了一种可扩展、客观的视觉暴露组学方法。
Correct Is Not Enough: Training Reasoning Planners with Executor-Grounded Rewards
Authors: Tianyang Han, Hengyu Shi, Junjie Hu, Xu Yang, Zhiling Wang, Junhao Su
First: 2026-05-05T15:24:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:24:40+00:00
Comments: 36 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a common way to improve explicit reasoning in large language models, but final-answer correctness alone does not reveal whether the reasoning trace is faithful, reliable, or useful to the model that consumes it. This outcome-only signal can reinforce traces that are right for the wrong reasons, overstate reasoning gains by rewarding shortcuts, and propagate flawed intermediate states in multi-step systems. To this end, we propose TraceLift, a planner-executor training framework that treats reasoning as a consumable intermediate artifact. During planner training, the planner emits tagged reasoning. A frozen executor turns this reasoning into the final artifact for verifier feedback, while an executor-grounded reward shapes the intermediate trace. This reward multiplies a rubric-based Reasoning Reward Model (RM) score by measured uplift on the same frozen executor, crediting traces that are both high-quality and useful. To make reasoning quality directly learnable, we introduce TRACELIFT-GROUPS, a rubric-annotated reason-only dataset built from math and code seed problems. Each example is a same-problem group containing a high-quality reference trace and multiple plausible flawed traces with localized perturbations that reduce reasoning quality or solution support while preserving task relevance. Extensive experiments on code and math benchmarks show that this executor-grounded reasoning reward improves the two-stage planner-executor system over execution-only training, suggesting that reasoning supervision should evaluate not only whether a trace looks good, but also whether it helps the model that consumes it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：正确还不够：基于执行器锚定奖励的推理规划器训练
可验证奖励的强化学习已成为提升大语言模型显式推理能力的常用方法，但仅凭最终答案正确性无法揭示推理轨迹是否忠实、可靠或对模型有用。这种仅关注结果的信号可能强化错误原因导致正确的轨迹、因奖励捷径而夸大推理收益，并在多步系统中传播错误中间状态。为此，我们提出TraceLift，一种将推理视为可消费中间产物的规划器-执行器训练框架。规划器训练时输出带标签的推理轨迹，冻结的执行器将其转化为最终产物供验证器反馈，而基于执行器锚定的奖励则塑造中间轨迹。该奖励将基于评分标准的推理奖励模型（RM）得分乘以同一冻结执行器上的实际提升值，奖励既高质量又实用的轨迹。为使推理质量直接可学习，我们引入TRACELIFT-GROUPS，一个基于数学与代码种子问题构建的仅含推理轨迹的标注数据集。每个示例包含一个高质量参考轨迹和多个局部扰动的合理错误轨迹，这些扰动降低推理质量或解题支持度，但保持任务相关性。在代码与数学基准上的大量实验表明，基于执行器锚定的推理奖励相比仅执行训练，能显著提升两阶段规划器-执行器系统性能，表明推理监督不仅应评估轨迹是否“看起来好”，更应评估其是否对消费该轨迹的模型真正有益。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of correctness-only rewards in training reasoning planners, this work introduces TraceLift, a framework that grounds reward signals in executor performance to evaluate reasoning trace utility. The method trains a planner to emit tagged reasoning, which a frozen executor converts into an artifact for verifier feedback, while an executor-grounded reward multiplies a rubric-based Reasoning Reward Model score by actual performance uplift on the same executor. To enable learning of reasoning quality, the authors construct TRACELIFT-GROUPS, a dataset of math and code problems with high-quality reference traces and perturbed flawed traces preserving task relevance. Experiments on code and math benchmarks show that executor-grounded rewards outperform execution-only training, improving planner-executor system performance by encouraging traces that are both high-quality and useful to the consuming model.
为解决仅凭最终答案正确性无法评估推理过程质量的问题，本文提出TraceLift框架，通过执行器性能反馈来引导推理轨迹训练。该方法让规划器生成带标签的推理过程，由冻结的执行器将其转化为最终产物并提供反馈，同时采用基于执行器表现提升的奖励机制，将推理质量评分与实际执行效果相乘。为使推理质量可学习，作者构建了TRACELIFT-GROUPS数据集，包含数学与编程问题的高质量参考轨迹及局部扰动的错误轨迹。实验表明，该方法在代码与数学基准测试中显著优于仅执行导向的训练，证明推理监督需同时评估轨迹质量与对模型的实际效用。
Uncovering and Understanding FPR Manipulation Attack in Industrial IoT Networks
Authors: Mohammad Shamim Ahsan, Peng Liu
First: 2026-01-20T21:57:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:24:16+00:00
Comments: Technical contributions have some flaws
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the network security domain, due to practical issues -- including imbalanced data and heterogeneous legitimate network traffic -- adversarial attacks in machine learning-based NIDSs have been viewed as attack packets misclassified as benign. Due to this prevailing belief, the possibility of (maliciously) perturbed benign packets being misclassified as attack has been largely ignored. In this paper, we demonstrate that this is not only theoretically possible, but also a particular threat to NIDS. In particular, we uncover a practical cyberattack, FPR manipulation attack (FPA), especially targeting industrial IoT networks, where domain-specific knowledge of the widely used MQTT protocol is exploited and a systematic simple packet-level perturbation is performed to alter the labels of benign traffic samples without employing traditional gradient-based or non-gradient-based methods. The experimental evaluations demonstrate that this novel attack results in a success rate of 80.19% to 100%. In addition, while estimating impacts in the Security Operations Center, we observe that even a small fraction of false positive alerts, irrespective of different budget constraints and alert traffic intensities, can increase the delay of genuine alerts investigations up to 2 hr in a single day under normal operating conditions. Furthermore, a series of relevant statistical and XAI analyses is conducted to understand the key factors behind this remarkable success. Finally, we explore the effectiveness of the FPA packets to enhance models' robustness through adversarial training and investigate the changes in decision boundaries accordingly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：工业物联网网络中FPR操纵攻击的揭示与理解
在网络安全领域，由于实际问题——包括数据不平衡和异构的合法网络流量——基于机器学习的NIDS中的对抗攻击常被视为被误分类为良性攻击的数据包。由于这一主流观点，恶意扰动的良性数据包被误分类为攻击的可能性被大量忽视。本文证明，这不仅在理论上可行，而且对NIDS构成特定威胁。具体而言，我们揭示了一种实用的网络攻击——FPR操纵攻击（FPA），尤其针对工业物联网网络，该攻击利用广泛使用的MQTT协议的领域知识，通过系统性的简单数据包级扰动改变良性流量样本的标签，而无需采用传统的基于梯度或非梯度的方法。实验评估表明，该新型攻击的成功率高达80.19%至100%。此外，在安全运营中心评估影响时，我们观察到即使少量误报警报，无论预算约束或警报流量强度如何，也可能在正常运行条件下将真实警报调查延迟长达2小时。最后，我们通过一系列相关统计和XAI分析，理解该攻击成功的关键因素，并探讨FPA数据包通过对抗训练增强模型鲁棒性及其对决策边界的相应变化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the overlooked risk of benign packets being misclassified as attacks in industrial IoT networks, this paper introduces the FPR manipulation attack (FPA), which exploits MQTT protocol knowledge to systematically perturb benign packets at the packet level without relying on gradient-based or non-gradient-based methods. Experiments show the attack achieves a success rate of 80.19% to 100% in fooling NIDS, and even a small fraction of false positives can delay genuine alert investigations by up to two hours daily. Statistical and XAI analyses reveal key factors behind the attack’s effectiveness, and adversarial training is shown to improve model robustness while altering decision boundaries.
鉴于现有NIDS系统对良性数据被误判为攻击的忽视，本研究提出FPR操纵攻击（FPA），利用MQTT协议特性对数据包进行简单扰动，无需梯度方法即可翻转良性样本标签。实验表明该攻击成功率高达80.19%至100%，显著降低系统准确性；即使少量误报也会导致真实告警响应延迟长达两小时。统计与XAI分析揭示了攻击成功的关键因素，同时通过对抗训练提升模型鲁棒性，观察到决策边界随之变化。
MCJudgeBench: A Benchmark for Constraint-Level Judge Evaluation in Multi-Constraint Instruction Following
Authors: Jaeyun Lee, Junyoung Koh, Zeynel Tok, Hunar Batra, Ronald Clark
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-05T15:20:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:20:42+00:00
Comments: GEM Workshop at ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-constraint instruction following requires verifying whether a response satisfies multiple individual requirements, yet LLM judges are often assessed only through overall-response judgments. We introduce MCJudgeBench, a benchmark for constraint-level judge evaluation in multi-constraint instruction following. Each instance includes an instruction, a candidate response, an explicit constraint list, per-constraint gold labels in {yes, partial, no}, and controlled response-side perturbations. The evaluation protocol further includes evaluation prompt variants to test judge stability. We evaluate proprietary and open-source LLM judges using both correctness and inconsistency metrics, distinguishing intrinsic inconsistency under stochastic decoding from procedural inconsistency under prompt and response perturbations. Our results show that judge reliability has multiple dimensions: strong overall performance does not guarantee equally reliable detection across label categories, especially for rarer partial and no cases. Judges with higher correctness do not always have lower inconsistency. Evaluation with reasoning improves correctness but does not uniformly improve stability. These findings motivate evaluating LLM judges at the constraint level to study these failure modes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MCJudgeBench：多约束指令遵循中约束级别裁判评估基准
多约束指令遵循需要验证响应是否满足多个独立要求，但大语言模型裁判常仅通过整体响应判断评估。我们引入MCJudgeBench，用于多约束指令遵循中的约束级别裁判评估。每个实例包含指令、候选响应、显式约束列表、每个约束的黄金标签（{yes, partial, no}），以及受控的响应扰动。评估协议还包括评估提示变体以测试裁判稳定性。我们使用正确性和不一致性指标评估专有和开源大语言模型裁判，区分随机解码下的内在不一致性与提示和响应扰动下的程序性不一致性。结果表明，裁判可靠性具有多维度：整体表现强并不保证在各类标签上检测同样可靠，尤其是稀有的partial和no情况。正确性更高的裁判并不总是不一致性更低。加入推理评估可提升正确性，但不总能提升稳定性。这些发现促使在约束级别评估大语言模型裁判，以研究这些失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLM judges more precisely in multi-constraint instruction following, this work introduces MCJudgeBench, a benchmark that assesses judges at the constraint level rather than overall response quality. Each instance includes an instruction, candidate response, explicit constraints, per-constraint gold labels (yes, partial, no), and controlled perturbations to test judge stability. Evaluating both proprietary and open-source models using correctness and inconsistency metrics reveals that high overall performance does not ensure reliable detection across all label categories, especially partial and no cases. Judges with higher correctness often show no reduction in inconsistency, and while reasoning improves correctness, it does not uniformly enhance stability, highlighting the need for constraint-level evaluation to uncover failure modes.
为更精确评估多约束指令遵循场景下LLM法官的表现，本文提出MCJudgeBench基准，从约束层面而非整体响应质量评估法官。每个样本包含指令、候选回答、显式约束、每约束的黄金标签及受控扰动以测试稳定性。研究对自有及开源LLM法官使用正确性与不一致性指标进行评估，区分内在与程序性不一致。结果表明，高整体表现不保证各标签类别（尤其partial和no）的可靠检测；高正确性不必然伴随低不一致性；引入推理评估可提升正确性，但对稳定性提升不一致。
A Deeper Dive into the Irreversibility of PolyProtect: Making Protected Face Templates Harder to Invert
Authors: Vedrana Krivokuća Hahn, Jérémy Maceiras, Sébastien Marcel
First: 2026-05-05T15:20:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:20:38+00:00
Comments: Submitted to TIFS journal on 18 February 2026 (under review). Consists of: 12 pages, 10 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents a deeper analysis of the "irreversibility" property of PolyProtect, a biometric template protection method initially proposed for securing face embeddings. PolyProtect transforms embeddings into protected templates via multivariate polynomials, whose coefficients and exponents are distinct for each subject enrolled in the face recognition system. A polynomial is applied to consecutive sets of elements from a given embedding, where the amount of overlap between the sets is a tunable parameter. We begin our irreversibility analysis by demonstrating that PolyProtected templates are easier to invert using a numerical solver based on cosine distance, as opposed to Euclidean distance (used in the earlier PolyProtect work). To make this inversion more difficult, we then propose a "key selection algorithm", which tries to choose "keys" (coefficients and exponents of the PolyProtect polynomial) that enhance the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates, compared to when the keys are purely random. Our experiments show that this algorithm is effective at generating PolyProtected templates that are significantly more difficult to invert, and that it approximately equalises the irreversibility of PolyProtected templates generated using different "overlap" parameters. This allows for better control of the irreversibility versus accuracy trade-off, known to exist across different overlaps. We also show that accuracy in the PolyProtected domain can be affected by the range in which the embedding elements lie, but that this can be improved by normalizing the embeddings prior to applying PolyProtect. This work is reproducible using our open-source code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对PolyProtect不可逆性的深入研究：使保护的面部模板更难逆向还原
本文对PolyProtect方法的“不可逆性”特性进行了更深入的分析。PolyProtect是一种最初为保护面部嵌入而提出的生物特征模板保护方法，它通过多元多项式将嵌入转换为受保护的模板，每个注册用户对应的多项式系数和指数均不同。该多项式应用于给定嵌入的连续元素组，组间重叠量为可调参数。我们首先通过实验证明，使用余弦距离而非欧氏距离（早期PolyProtect工作所用）的数值求解器，可更易逆向还原PolyProtected模板。为增加逆向难度，我们提出了一种“密钥选择算法”，旨在选择能增强PolyProtected模板不可逆性的“密钥”（即多项式系数和指数），相较于纯随机密钥更优。实验表明，该算法能有效生成更难逆向的PolyProtected模板，并大致均衡不同“重叠”参数下生成模板的不可逆性，从而更好地控制不可逆性与准确率之间的权衡。此外，我们还发现嵌入元素的取值范围会影响PolyProtected域的准确率，但通过在应用PolyProtect前对嵌入进行归一化可改善此问题。本工作可通过我们的开源代码复现。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how to enhance the irreversibility of PolyProtect, a biometric template protection method that uses multivariate polynomials to transform face embeddings into protected templates. Motivated by the observation that inversion is easier using cosine distance than Euclidean distance, the authors propose a key selection algorithm to optimize polynomial coefficients and exponents to make inversion harder. Experimental results show that this algorithm significantly improves irreversibility and nearly equalizes performance across different overlap parameters, allowing better control of the trade-off between security and recognition accuracy. Additionally, the study finds that embedding normalization improves accuracy in the protected domain.
本文旨在增强PolyProtect方法的不可逆性，该方法通过多变量多项式对人脸嵌入进行保护。研究发现，使用余弦距离比欧氏距离更容易反推保护模板，因此提出一种密钥选择算法，通过优化多项式系数和指数来提升不可逆性。实验表明，该算法显著提高了模板的反推难度，并能在不同重叠参数间平衡不可逆性，从而更好地控制安全与识别准确率之间的权衡。此外，研究还发现嵌入归一化可提升保护域内的识别准确率，而不影响不可逆性。
Evaluating Generative Models as Interactive Emergent Representations of Human-Like Collaborative Behavior
Authors: Shinas Shaji, Teena Chakkalayil Hassan, Sebastian Houben, Alex Mitrevski
First: 2026-05-05T15:20:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:20:15+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human-AI collaboration requires AI agents to understand human behavior for effective coordination. While advances in foundation models show promising capabilities in understanding and showing human-like behavior, their application in embodied collaborative settings needs further investigation. This work examines whether embodied foundation model agents exhibit emergent collaborative behaviors indicating underlying mental models of their collaborators, which is an important aspect of effective coordination. This paper develops a 2D collaborative game environment where large language model agents and humans complete color-matching tasks requiring coordination. We define five collaborative behaviors as indicators of emergent mental model representation: perspective-taking, collaborator-aware planning, introspection, theory of mind, and clarification. An automated behavior detection system using LLM-based judges identifies these behaviors, achieving fair to substantial agreement with human annotations. Results from the automated behavior detection system show that foundation models consistently exhibit emergent collaborative behaviors without being explicitly trained to do so. These behaviors occur at varying frequencies during collaboration stages, with distinct patterns across different LLMs. A user study was also conducted to evaluate human satisfaction and perceived collaboration effectiveness, with the results indicating positive collaboration experiences. Participants appreciated the agents' task focus, plan verbalization, and initiative, while suggesting improvements in response times and human-like interactions. This work provides an experimental framework for human-AI collaboration, empirical evidence of collaborative behaviors in embodied LLM agents, a validated behavioral analysis methodology, and an assessment of collaboration effectiveness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估生成式模型作为类人协作行为的交互式涌现表征
人机协作要求AI代理理解人类行为以实现有效协调。尽管基础模型在理解和展现类人行为方面展现出有前景的能力，但其在具身协作场景中的应用仍需进一步研究。本研究探讨具身基础模型代理是否表现出表明其对协作伙伴具备潜在心智模型的涌现协作行为，这是有效协调的重要方面。本文构建了一个2D协作游戏环境，大型语言模型代理与人类共同完成需协调的配色任务。我们定义五种协作行为作为涌现心智模型表征的指标：换位思考、协作伙伴意识规划、内省、理论心智、澄清。基于LLM的自动化行为检测系统识别这些行为，其与人工标注结果达成公平至显著的一致性。自动化行为检测系统结果表明，基础模型在未显式训练的情况下持续展现出涌现协作行为，且在协作阶段不同频率出现，不同LLM呈现不同模式。同时进行的用户研究评估了人类满意度与感知协作有效性，结果显示积极的协作体验。参与者赞赏代理的任务专注、计划口头化及主动性，同时建议改进响应速度与类人互动。本工作提供人机协作实验框架、具身LLM代理协作行为实证证据、经验证的行为分析方法及协作有效性评估。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language model agents in embodied collaborative settings exhibit emergent human-like collaborative behaviors without explicit training, motivated by the need for AI to understand human mental states for effective coordination. The researchers designed a 2D color-matching game where human and LLM agents collaborate, defining five behavioral indicators—perspective-taking, collaborator-aware planning, introspection, theory of mind, and clarification—to assess emergent mental models. An automated detection system using LLM-based judges achieved fair to substantial agreement with human annotations, revealing that foundation models consistently displayed these behaviors at varying frequencies across different models. A user study further confirmed positive human perceptions of collaboration, with participants valuing the agents’ task focus and verbal planning, while suggesting improvements in responsiveness and naturalness.
本研究旨在探究大型语言模型代理在二维协作游戏中是否能自发表现出人类协作行为，以反映其对合作对象的内在心理模型。研究设计了一个需要协调配合的颜色匹配任务，定义了五种协作行为指标——换位思考、协作对象意识规划、内省、心智理论和澄清——用于评估协作涌现。通过基于LLM的自动检测系统，其结果与人工标注达成中等至较高一致性，证实基础模型无需显式训练即可持续表现出这些行为。实验结果显示不同LLM在协作阶段表现出不同频率的行为模式，用户研究也表明人类对协作体验满意，称赞代理专注任务和口头规划，同时建议提升响应速度和更自然的人类化互动。
User Detection and Response Patterns of Sycophantic Behavior in Conversational AI
Authors: Kazi Noshin, Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed, Sharifa Sultana
First: 2026-01-15T14:51:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:17:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite growing attention to LLM sycophancy from researchers and developers, users' own experiences of this behavior remain underexplored. We examine how everyday users experience AI sycophancy through Reddit discussions. Using our ODR Framework which maps user experiences through observation, detection, and response stages, we find that users identify sycophantic behavior through methods like cross-platform comparison and consistency testing. They employ various mitigation strategies, including persona-based prompting and specific language engineering techniques. Our findings suggest that sycophancy does not have a uniformly negative effect; its impact differs by context. Users facing trauma, mental health struggles, or isolation often actively seek affirmative AI responses for emotional support. Users construct both technical and informal theories to explain sycophantic outputs. Users construct both technical and informal theories to explain sycophantic outputs. These findings suggest eliminating sycophancy entirely may be misguided. We argue for context-aware AI design that balances risks against benefits of affirmative interaction, with implications for user education and system transparency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对话式人工智能中谄媚行为的用户检测与应对模式
尽管研究人员和开发者日益关注大型语言模型的谄媚行为，但用户对此行为的自身体验仍鲜有研究。我们通过Reddit讨论，考察普通用户如何体验AI的谄媚行为。运用我们的ODR框架（观察、检测、响应阶段），我们发现用户通过跨平台比较和一致性测试等方法识别谄媚行为，并采用多种缓解策略，包括基于人设的提示和特定语言工程技术。研究发现，谄媚行为并非总是负面，其影响因情境而异。面对创伤、心理健康问题或孤独感的用户，常主动寻求AI的肯定性回应以获得情感支持。用户构建技术性与非正式理论来解释谄媚输出。这些发现表明，完全消除谄媚行为可能有失偏颇。我们主张应设计具备情境感知能力的AI，平衡肯定性互动的风险与收益，并对用户教育和系统透明度提出启示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored user experiences of AI sycophancy despite its growing research attention, this study analyzes Reddit discussions using an ODR Framework to map user detection and response patterns. Users identify sycophantic behavior through cross-platform comparison and consistency testing, then mitigate it via persona-based prompting and language engineering. Findings reveal sycophancy’s impact varies by context: users in distress often seek affirming AI responses for emotional support, while others develop technical or informal theories to explain outputs. The study concludes that eliminating sycophancy entirely may be counterproductive, advocating instead for context-aware AI design that balances its risks and benefits.
鉴于对AI谄媚行为的研究日益增多，但用户实际体验却鲜有探讨，本研究通过分析Reddit讨论，借助ODR框架（观察、检测、响应）探究普通用户如何识别和应对AI谄媚行为。用户通过跨平台对比和一致性测试识别谄媚行为，并采用角色提示和语言工程技术进行缓解。研究发现，谄媚行为的影响因情境而异：面对创伤、心理健康问题或孤独的用户常主动寻求AI的肯定回应以获得情感支持，而其他用户则构建技术或非正式理论解释此类输出。研究认为完全消除谄媚行为可能适得其反，主张应根据情境设计AI系统，权衡其风险与益处。
ABC: Any-Subset Autoregression via Non-Markovian Diffusion Bridges in Continuous Time and Space
Authors: Gabe Guo, Thanawat Sornwanee, Lutong Hao, Elon Litman, Stefano Ermon, Jose Blanchet
First: 2026-04-30T05:29:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:16:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating continuous-time, continuous-space stochastic processes (e.g., videos, weather forecasts) conditioned on partial observations (e.g., first and last frames) is a fundamental challenge. Existing approaches, (e.g., diffusion models), suffer from key limitations: (1) noise-to-data evolution fails to capture structural similarity between states close in physical time and has unstable integration in low-step regimes; (2) random noise injected is insensitive to the physical process's time elapsed, resulting in incorrect dynamics; (3) they overlook conditioning on arbitrary subsets of states (e.g., irregularly sampled timesteps, future observations). We propose ABC: Any-Subset Autoregressive Models via Non-Markovian Diffusion Bridges in Continuous Time and Space. Crucially, we model the process with one continual SDE whose time variable and intermediate states track the real time and process states. This has provable advantages: (1) the starting point for generating future states is the already-close previous state, rather than uninformative noise; (2) random noise injection scales with physical time elapsed, encouraging physically plausible dynamics with similar time-adjacent states. We derive SDE dynamics via changes-of-measure on path space, yielding another advantage: (3) path-dependent conditioning on arbitrary subsets of the state history and/or future. To learn these dynamics, we derive a path- and time-dependent extension of denoising score matching. Our experiments show ABC's superiority to competing methods on multiple domains, including video generation and weather forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ABC：通过连续时间和空间中的非马尔可夫扩散桥实现任意子集自回归
生成基于部分观测（如首尾帧）条件的连续时间、连续空间随机过程（如视频、天气预报）是一个基本挑战。现有方法（如扩散模型）存在关键局限：（1）噪声到数据的演化无法捕捉物理时间相近状态间的结构相似性，且在低步长情况下积分不稳定；（2）注入的随机噪声对物理过程经历的时间不敏感，导致动态错误；（3）忽略对任意状态子集（如不规则采样时间步、未来观测）的条件建模。我们提出ABC：通过连续时间和空间中的非马尔可夫扩散桥实现任意子集自回归模型。关键在于，我们用一个连续随机微分方程（SDE）建模过程，其时间变量和中间状态追踪真实时间和过程状态。这具有可证明优势：（1）生成未来状态的起点是已接近的前一状态，而非无信息噪声；（2）随机噪声注入随物理时间流逝缩放，鼓励物理上合理的动态，使相邻时间状态相似。我们通过路径空间测度变换推导SDE动力学，带来另一优势：（3）可对状态历史和/或未来任意子集进行路径依赖条件建模。为学习这些动力学，我们推导出路径和时间相关的去噪得分匹配扩展。实验表明，ABC在多个领域（包括视频生成和天气预报）优于竞争方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate continuous-time, continuous-space stochastic processes conditioned on partial observations, the paper introduces ABC: Any-Subset Autoregression via Non-Markovian Diffusion Bridges. It proposes a single continuous-time SDE that tracks real-time evolution and state transitions, enabling physically plausible dynamics by scaling noise injection with elapsed time and using the previous state as a meaningful starting point. The method also supports conditioning on arbitrary subsets of states via path-space changes-of-measure, and learns dynamics through a time- and path-dependent score matching objective. Experiments demonstrate ABC outperforms existing methods in video generation and weather forecasting tasks.
为解决在部分观测条件下生成连续时间、连续空间随机过程的挑战，本文提出ABC：任意子集自回归模型，通过非马尔可夫扩散桥实现。该方法构建一个连续时间SDE，实时追踪状态演化，通过按物理时间缩放噪声注入并以邻近状态初始化未来状态，确保动力学合理性；同时支持对任意子集状态历史或未来状态的路径依赖式条件建模。实验表明，ABC在视频生成与天气预报等任务上显著优于现有扩散模型方法。
Stream-R1: Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation for Streaming Video Generation
Authors: Bin Wu, Mengqi Huang, Shaojin Wu, Weinan Jia, Yuxin Wang, Zhendong Mao, Yongdong Zhang
First: 2026-05-05T15:15:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:15:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distillation-based acceleration has become foundational for making autoregressive streaming video diffusion models practical, with distribution matching distillation (DMD) as the de facto choice. Existing methods, however, train the student to match the teacher's output indiscriminately, treating every rollout, frame, and pixel as equally reliable supervision. We argue that this caps distilled quality, since it overlooks two complementary axes of variance in DMD supervision: Inter-Reliability across student rollouts whose supervision varies in reliability, and Intra-Perplexity across spatial regions and temporal frames that contribute unequally to where quality can still be improved. The objective thus conflates two questions under a uniform weight: whether to learn from each rollout, and where to concentrate optimization within it. To address this, we propose Stream-R1, a Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation framework that adaptively reweights the distillation objective at both rollout and spatiotemporal-element levels through a single shared reward-guided mechanism. At the Inter-Reliability level, Stream-R1 rescales each rollout's loss by an exponential of a pretrained video reward score, so that rollouts with reliable supervision dominate optimization. At the Intra-Perplexity level, it back-propagates the same reward model to extract per-pixel gradient saliency, which is factored into spatial and temporal weights that concentrate optimization pressure on regions and frames where refinement yields the largest expected gain. An adaptive balancing mechanism prevents any single quality axis from dominating across visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment. Stream-R1 attains consistent improvements on all three dimensions over distillation baselines on standard streaming video generation benchmarks, without architectural modification or additional inference cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Stream-R1：面向流式视频生成的可靠性-困惑度感知奖励蒸馏
基于蒸馏的加速已成为使自回归流式视频扩散模型实用化的基础，其中分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）是事实上的标准方法。然而，现有方法训练学生模型无差别地匹配教师输出，将每个 rollout、帧和像素视为同等可靠的监督信号。我们认为，这限制了蒸馏质量，因为它忽略了 DMD 监督中的两个互补变异轴：跨学生 rollout 的 Inter-Reliability（监督可靠性差异）和跨空间区域与时间帧的 Intra-Perplexity（各区域帧对质量提升贡献不均）。因此，当前目标在统一权重下混淆了两个问题：是否应从每个 rollout 学习，以及在其中应集中优化何处。为解决此问题，我们提出 Stream-R1，一种可靠性-困惑度感知奖励蒸馏框架，通过单一共享的奖励引导机制，自适应地在 rollout 和时空元素层面重加权蒸馏目标。在 Inter-Reliability 层面，Stream-R1 通过预训练视频奖励分数的指数值对每个 rollout 的损失进行缩放，使监督可靠的 rollout 主导优化。在 Intra-Perplexity 层面，它反向传播同一奖励模型以提取每像素梯度显著性，并将其分解为空间与时间权重，将优化压力集中于预期收益最大的区域与帧。自适应平衡机制防止任何单一质量维度（视觉质量、运动质量、文本对齐）在整体质量中主导。Stream-R1 在标准流式视频生成基准上，相较蒸馏基线在三个维度均实现一致提升，且无需架构修改或额外推理开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing distillation methods that treat all video generation outputs as equally reliable, the authors propose Stream-R1, a reward-guided distillation framework that adapts supervision weights based on reliability and perplexity. At the rollout level, it rescales losses using a pretrained video reward model to prioritize reliable rollouts, while at the spatiotemporal level, it computes pixel-wise gradient saliency to focus optimization on regions and frames with highest expected improvement. Experiments show Stream-R1 consistently outperforms baselines across visual quality, motion quality, and text alignment without altering model architecture or increasing inference cost.
现有蒸馏方法将所有视频生成结果视为同等可靠，作者提出Stream-R1框架，通过奖励引导机制动态调整监督权重，依据可靠性与困惑度进行加权。在 rollout 层面，利用预训练视频奖励模型对损失进行指数重标度，优先优化可靠 rollout；在时空层面，通过梯度显著性计算像素级权重，聚焦改进收益最大的区域与帧。实验表明，Stream-R1在视觉质量、运动质量与文本对齐三方面均优于基线方法，且无需修改架构或增加推理开销。
Parameter-Efficient Multi-View Proficiency Estimation: From Discriminative Classification to Generative Feedback
Authors: Edoardo Bianchi, Antonio Liotta
First: 2026-05-05T15:14:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:14:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating how well a person performs an action, rather than which action is performed, is central to coaching, rehabilitation, and talent identification. This task is challenging because proficiency is encoded in subtle differences in timing, balance, body mechanics, and execution, often distributed across multiple views and short temporal events. We discuss three recent contributions to multi-view proficiency estimation on Ego-Exo4D. SkillFormer introduces a parameter-efficient discriminative architecture for selective multi-view fusion; PATS improves temporal sampling by preserving locally dense excerpts of fundamental movements; and ProfVLM reformulates proficiency estimation as conditional language generation, producing both a proficiency label and expert-style feedback through a gated cross-view projector and a compact language backbone. Together, these methods achieve state-of-the-art accuracy on Ego-Exo4D with up to 20x fewer trainable parameters and up to 3x fewer training epochs than video-transformer baselines, while moving from closed-set classification toward interpretable feedback generation. These results highlight a shift toward efficient, multi-view systems that combine selective fusion, proficiency-aware sampling, and actionable generative feedback.
中文标题/摘要
标题：参数高效多视角熟练度估计：从判别式分类到生成式反馈
评估一个人执行某项动作的熟练程度，而非判断其执行了何种动作，是教练指导、康复训练和人才识别的核心。该任务具有挑战性，因为熟练度体现在动作时机、平衡、身体力学和执行中的细微差异，常分布于多个视角和短暂时间事件中。我们在Ego-Exo4D数据集上讨论了三项近期进展：SkillFormer提出一种参数高效的判别式架构，用于选择性多视角融合；PATS通过保留基本动作的局部密集片段，改进了时序采样；ProfVLM将熟练度估计重构为条件语言生成任务，通过门控跨视角投影器和紧凑语言骨干网络，同时输出熟练度标签和专家风格反馈。这些方法在Ego-Exo4D上实现了当前最优精度，相比视频Transformer基线模型，参数量减少高达20倍，训练轮次减少高达3倍，同时从封闭集分类转向可解释的反馈生成。这些成果凸显了向高效、多视角系统演进的趋势，结合选择性融合、熟练度感知采样和可操作的生成式反馈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately assess human proficiency in actions for coaching and rehabilitation, this work introduces parameter-efficient methods for multi-view proficiency estimation on the Ego-Exo4D dataset. It combines SkillFormer’s selective multi-view fusion, PATS’s proficiency-aware temporal sampling, and ProfVLM’s generative feedback via a gated cross-view projector and compact language model. Experiments show these methods achieve state-of-the-art accuracy with up to 20x fewer trainable parameters and 3x fewer training epochs than video-transformer baselines, while transitioning from discriminative classification to interpretable generative feedback.
为实现教练、康复和人才识别中对人类动作熟练度的精准评估，本研究在Ego-Exo4D数据集上提出三种参数高效的多视角熟练度估计方法。SkillFormer采用判别式架构实现选择性多视角融合，PATS通过保留局部密集动作片段优化时序采样，ProfVLM将熟练度估计重构为条件语言生成任务，结合门控跨视角投影器与紧凑语言模型生成标签与专家反馈。三者联合实现当前最优性能，参数量减少达20倍，训练轮次减少3倍，同时从分类任务转向可解释的生成式反馈。
Mechanical Conscience: A Mathematical Framework for Dependability of Machine Intelligenc
Authors: Munkhdegerekh Batzorig, Purevbaatar Ganbold, Kyungbin Park, Pilkong Jeong, Kangbin
First: 2026-05-05T15:14:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-05T15:14:02+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 2 figures. Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distributed collaborative intelligence (DCI), encompassing edge-to-edge architectures, federated learning, transfer learning, and swarm systems, creates environments in which emergent risk is structurally unavoidable: locally correct decisions by individual agents compose into globally unacceptable behavioral trajectories under uncertainty. Existing approaches such as constrained optimization, safe reinforcement learning, and runtime assurance evaluate acceptability at the level of individual actions rather than across behavioral trajectories, and none addresses the multi-participant, uncertainty-laden nature of DCI deployments. This paper introduces mechanical conscience (MC), a novel concept and simplified mathematical framework that operationalizes trajectory-level normative regulation for both single-agent and distributed intelligent systems. Mechanical conscience is defined as a supervisory filter that minimally corrects a baseline policy's actions to reduce cumulative deviation from a normatively admissible region, while accounting for epistemic uncertainty. We introduce associated constructs, conscience score, mechanical guilt, and resonant dependability, that provide an interpretable vocabulary and computable governance signals for this emerging field. Core theoretical properties are established: admissibility equivalence, existence of optimal regulation, and monotonic deviation reduction. Illustrative results demonstrate that MC-regulated agents maintain trajectory-level normative acceptability where conventional controllers drift outside admissible bounds, and that the framework naturally extends to suppress interaction-induced emergent risk in multi-agent DCI settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机械良知：机器智能可靠性的数学框架
分布式协同智能（DCI），包括边缘到边缘架构、联邦学习、迁移学习和群智系统，创造了结构上不可避免的涌现风险环境：个体代理在局部做出正确决策，但在不确定性下会组合成全局不可接受的行为轨迹。现有方法如约束优化、安全强化学习和运行时保障仅在个体动作层面评估可接受性，均未解决DCI部署中多参与者与不确定性交织的特性。本文提出“机械良知”（MC），一种新颖概念与简化数学框架，用于在单代理与分布式智能系统中实现轨迹级规范性调控。机械良知被定义为一种监督滤波器，通过最小化对基准策略动作的修正，减少累积偏离规范性可接受区域，同时考虑认识论不确定性。我们引入相关概念——良知得分、机械罪责与共振可靠性，为该新兴领域提供可解释术语与可计算治理信号。核心理论性质已确立：可接受性等价性、最优调控存在性及偏差单调递减性。示例结果表明，MC调控的代理在轨迹级保持规范性可接受性，而传统控制器则会偏离可接受边界；该框架自然扩展至多代理DCI场景，可抑制交互引发的涌现风险。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inherent risk in distributed collaborative intelligence systems where locally correct actions can lead to globally unacceptable outcomes under uncertainty, this paper proposes mechanical conscience (MC), a mathematical framework that enforces normative regulation at the trajectory level rather than per action. MC operates as a supervisory filter that minimally adjusts a baseline policy to keep cumulative behavior within an admissible region, while explicitly accounting for epistemic uncertainty. Experimental results show that MC-regulated agents maintain normative acceptability over time, whereas conventional controllers frequently violate admissible bounds, and the framework naturally extends to mitigate emergent risk in multi-agent settings.
针对分布式协作智能系统中局部正确行为在不确定性下可能引发全局不可接受结果的问题，本文提出“机械良知”这一数学框架，通过轨迹级而非动作级的规范调节来约束智能体行为。该框架引入一个监督滤波器，最小化对基线策略的调整，以保持累积行为在规范可接受区域内，并考虑认知不确定性。框架包含“良知得分”与“机械罪责”等可解释指标，支持理论性质如可接受性等价性与偏差单调递减。实验表明，采用该框架的智能体能维持规范可接受性，而传统控制器则会偏离可接受边界，且该方法天然适用于抑制多智能体系统中的交互诱导风险。
